gandhapuṣpendukalikā dalapuṣpā tripañcadhā || 10.68
svarṇādiketakī tvanyā jñeyā sā hemaketakī |
kanakaprasavā puṣpī haimī chinnaruhā tathā |
viṣṭaruhā svarṇapuṣpī kāmakhaḍgadalā ca sā || 10.69
ketakīkusumaṃ varṇyaṃ keśadaurgandhyanāśanam |
hemābhaṃ madanonmādavarddhanaṃ saukhyakāri ca || 10.70
tasyāḥ stano'tiśiśiraḥ kaṭuḥ pittakaphāpahaḥ |
rasāyanakaro balyo dehadārḍhyakaraḥ paraḥ || 10.71
sindūrī vīrapuṣpaśca tṛṇapuṣpī karacchadaḥ |
sindūrapuṣpī śoṇādipuṣpī ṣaḍāhvayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.72
sindūrī kaṭukā tiktā kaṣāyā śleṣmavātajit |
śiraārtiśamanī bhūtanāśā caṇḍīpriyā bhavet || 10.73
jātī surabhigandhā syātsumanā tu surapriyā |
cetakī sukumārā tu sandhyāpuṣpī manoharā || 10.74
rājaputrī manojñā ca mālatī tailabhāvinī |
janeṣṭā hṛdyagandhā ca nāmānyasyāścaturdaśa |
mālatī śītatiktā syātkaphaghnī mukhapākanut |
kudmalaṃ netrarogaghnaṃ vraṇavisphoṭakuṣṭhanut || 10.75
mudgaro gandhasārastu saptapatraśca kardamī |
vṛttapuṣpo'tigandhaśca gandharājo viṭapriyaḥ |
geyapriyo janeṣṭaśca mṛgeṣṭo rudrasammitaḥ || 10.76
mudgaro madhuraḥ śītaḥ surabhiḥ saukhyadāyakaḥ |
manojño madhupānandakārī pittaprakopahṛt || 10.77
śatapatrī tu sumanā suśītā śivavallabhā |
saumyagandhā śatadalā suvṛttā śatapatrikā || 10.78
śatapatrī himā tiktā kaṣāyā kuṣṭhanāśanī |
mukhasphoṭaharā rucyā surabhiḥ pittadāhanut || 10.79
mallikā bhadravallī tu gaurī ca vanacandrikā |
śītabhīruḥ priyā saumyā nārīṣṭā girijā sitā |
mallī ca damayantī ca candrikā modinī manuḥ || 10.80
mallikā kaṭutiktā syāccakṣuṣyā mukhapākanut |
kuṣṭhavisphoṭakaṇḍūtiviṣavraṇaharā parā || 10.81
vallikā modinī cānyā vaṭapatrā kumārikā |
sugandhāḍhyā vṛttapuṣpā muktābhā vṛttamallikā || 10.82
netrarogāpahantrī syātkaṭūṣṇā vṛttamallikā |
vraṇaghnī gandhabahalā dārayatyāsyajān gadān || 10.83
vārṣikā tripuṭā tryasrā surūpā sulabhā priyā |
śrīvallī ṣaṭpadānandā muktabandhā navābhidhā || 10.84
vārṣikā śiśirā hṛdyā sugandhiḥ pittanāśanī |
kaphavātaviṣasphoṭakrimidoṣāmanāśanī || 10.85
sā dīrghavarttulapuṣpaviśeṣādanekanirdeśā |
mṛgamadavāsā tvanyā kastūrīmallikā jñeyā || 10.86
prātarvikasvaraikā sāyodbhidurāpi mallikā kāpi |
vanamallikā nu sā syādāsphotā kintu samaguṇopetā || 10.87
vāsantī prahasantī vasantajā mādhavī mahājātiḥ |
śītasahā madhubahalā vasantadūtī ca vasunāmnī || 10.88
vāsantī śiśirā hṛdyā surabhiḥ śramahāriṇī |
dhammillāmodinī mandamadanonmādadāyinī || 10.89
navamallikātimodā graiṣmī grīṣmodbhavā ca sā |
saptalā sukumārā ca surabhī sūcimallikā |
sugandhā śikhariṇī syānnevālī cendubhūhvayā || 10.90
navamallikātiśaityā surabhiḥ sarvarogahṛt || 10.91
saivātimuktakākhyā puṇḍrakanāmnī ca kāciduktānyā |
madanī bhramarānandā kāmakāntā ca pañcākhyā || 10.92
atimuktaḥ kaṣāyaḥ syācchiśiraḥ śramanāśanaḥ |
pittadāhajvaronmādahikkācchardinivāraṇaḥ || 10.93
yūthikā gaṇikāmbaṣṭhā māgadhī bālapuṣpikā |
modanī bahugandhā ca bhṛṅgānandā gajāhvayā || 10.94
anyā yūthī suvarṇāhvā sugandhā hemayūthikā |
yuvatīṣṭā vyaktagandhā śikhaṇḍī nāgapuṣpikā || 10.95
hariṇī pītayūthī ca potikā kanakaprabhā |
manoharā ca gandhāḍhyā proktā trayodaśāhvayā || 10.96
yūthikāyugalaṃ svādu śiśiraṃ śarkarārtinut |
pittadāhatṛṣāhāri nānātvagdoṣanāśanam || 10.97
sitapītanīlamecakanāmnyaḥ kusumena yūthikāḥ kathitāḥ |
tiktahimapittakaphāmayajvaraghnyo vraṇādidoṣaharāḥ || 10.98
sarvāsāṃ yūthikānāṃ tu rasavīryādisāmyatā |
surūpaṃ tu sugandhāḍhyaṃ svarṇayūthyā viśeṣataḥ || 10.99
kubjako bhadrataruṇo vṛttapuṣpo'tikesaraḥ |
mahāsahaḥ kaṇṭakāḍhyaḥ kharvo'likulasaṅkulaḥ || 10.100
kubjakaḥ surabhiḥ śīto raktapittakaphāpahaḥ |
puṣpaṃ tu śītalaṃ varṇyaṃ dāhaghnaṃ vātapittajit || 10.101
mucakundo bahupatraḥ sudalo harivallabhaḥ supuṣpaśca |
arghyārho lakṣmaṇako raktaprasavaśca vasunāmā || 10.102
mucakundaḥ kaṭutiktaḥ kaphakāsavināśanaśca kaṇṭhadoṣaharaḥ |
tvagdoṣaśophaśamano vraṇapāmāvināśanaścaiva || 10.103
karuṇī grīṣmapuṣpī syādraktapuṣpī ca vāruṇī |
rājapriyā rājapuṣpī sūkṣmā ca brahmacāriṇī || 10.104
karuṇī kaṭutiktoṣṇā kaphamārutanāśinī |
ādhmānaviṣavicchardijatrūrdhvaśvāsahāriṇī || 10.105
mādhavī candravallī ca sugandhā bhramarotsavā |
bhṛṅgapriyā bhadralatā bhūmimaṇḍapabhūṣaṇī || 10.106
mādhavī kaṭukā tiktā kaṣāyā madagandhikā |
pittakāsavraṇān hanti dāhaśoṣavināśinī || 10.107
gaṇikārī kāñcanikā kāñcanapuṣpī vasantadūtī ca |
gandhakusumātimodā vāsantī madamādinī caiva || 10.108
gaṇikārī surabhitarā tridoṣaśamanī ca dāhaśoṣaharā |
kāmakrīḍāḍambaraśambaraharacāpalaprasarā || 10.109
kundastu makarandaśca mahāmodo manoharaḥ |
muktāpuṣpaḥ sadāpuṣpastārapuṣpo'ṭṭahāsakaḥ || 10.110
damano vanahāsaśca manojño rudrasammitaḥ || 10.111
kundo'timadhuraḥ śītaḥ kaṣāyaḥ kaiśyabhāvanaḥ |
kaphapittaharaścaiva saro dīpanapācanaḥ || 10.112
bakaḥ pāśupataḥ śaivaḥ śivapiṇḍaśca suvrataḥ |
vasukaśca śivāṅkaśca śiveṣṭaḥ kramapūrakaḥ |
śivamallī śivāhlādaḥ śāmbhavo ravisammitaḥ || 10.113
bako'tiśiśirastikto madhuro madhugandhakaḥ |
pittadāhakaphaśvāsaśramahārī ca dīpanaḥ || 10.114
kevikā kavikā kevā bhṛṅgārirnṛpavallabhā |
bhṛṅgamārī mahāgandhā rājakanyālimohinī || 10.115
kevikā madhurā śītā dāhapittaśramāpahā |
vātaśleṣmarujāṃ hantrī pittacchardivināśinī || 10.116
bandhūko bandhujīvaḥ syādoṣṭhapuṣpo'rkavallabhaḥ |
madhyandino raktapuṣpo rāgapuṣpo haripriyaḥ || 10.117
asitasitapītalohitapuṣpaviśeṣāccaturvidho bandhūkaḥ |
jvarahārī vividhagrahapiśācaśamanaḥ prasādanaḥ savituḥ syāt || 10.118
trisandhiḥ sāndhyakusumā sandhivallī sadāphalā |
trisandhyakusumā kāntā sukumārā ca sandhijā || 10.119
trisandhistrividhā jñeyā raktā cānyā sitāsitā |
kaphakāsaharā rucyā tvagdoṣaśamanī parā || 10.120
japākhyā oḍrakākhyā ca raktapuṣpī javā ca sā |
arkapriyā raktapuṣpī prātikā harivallabhā || 10.121
japā tu kaṭuruṣṇā syādindraluptakanāśakṛt |
vicchardijantujananī sūryārādhanasādhanī || 10.122
bhramarārirbhṛṅgamārī bhṛṅgārirmāṃsapuṣpikā |
kuṣṭhārirbhramarī caiva jñeyā yaṣṭilatā muniḥ || 10.123
tiktā bhramaramārī syādvātaśleṣmajvarāpahā |
śophakaṇḍūtikuṣṭhaghnī vraṇadoṣāsthidoṣanut || 10.124
taruṇī sahā kumārī gandhāḍhyā cārukesarā bhṛṅgeṣṭā |
rāmataruṇī tu sudalā bahupatrā bhṛṅgavallabhā ca daśāhvā || 10.125
taruṇī śiśirā snigdhā pittadāhajvarāpahā |
madhurā mukhapākaghnī tṛṣṇāvicchardivāriṇī || 10.126
mahatī tu rājataruṇī mahāsahā varṇyapuṣpako'mlānaḥ |
amilātakaḥ supuṣpaḥ suvarṇapuṣpaśca saptāhvaḥ || 10.127
vijñeyā rājataruṇī kaṣāyā kaphakāriṇī |
cakṣuṣyā harṣadā hṛdyā surabhiḥ suravallabhā || 10.128
atha raktāmlānaḥ syādraktasahākhyaḥ sa cāparimlānaḥ |
raktāmalāntako'pi ca raktaprasavaśca kuravakaścaiva || 10.129
rāmāliṅganakāmo rāgaprasavo madhūtsavaḥ prasavaḥ |
subhago bhramarānandaḥ syādityayaṃ pakṣacandramitaḥ || 10.130
uṣṇaḥ kaṭuḥ kuravako vātāmayaśophanāśano jvaranut |
ādhmānaśūlakāsaśvāsārtipraśamano varṇyaḥ || 10.131
pītaḥ sa kiṅkirātaḥ pītāmlānaḥ kuraṇṭakaḥ kanakaḥ |
pītakuravaḥ supītaḥ sa pītakusumaśca saptasaṃjñakaḥ syāt || 10.132
kiṅkirātaḥ kaṣāyoṣṇastiktaśca kaphavātajit |
dīpanaḥ śophakaṇḍūtiraktatvagdoṣanāśanaḥ || 10.133
nīlapuṣpā tu sā dāsī nīlāmlānastu chādanaḥ |
bālā cārttagalā caiva nīlapuṣpā ca ṣaḍvidhā || 10.134
ārttagalā kaṭustiktā kaphamārutaśūlanut |
kaṇḍūkuṣṭhavraṇān hanti śophatvagdoṣanāśanī || 10.135
kaṇṭakaraṇṭo jhiṇṭī sā vanyasahacarī tu sā pītā |
śoṇī kuravakanāmnī kaṇṭakinī śoṇajhiṇṭikā caiva || 10.136
sānyā tu nīlajhiṇṭī nīlakuraṇṭaśca nīlakusumā ca |
bāṇo bāṇā dāsī kaṇṭārttagalā ca saptasaṃjñā syāt || 10.137
jhiṇṭikāḥ kaṭukāstiktā dantāmayaśāntidāśca śūlaghnāḥ |
vātakaphaśophakāsatvagdoṣavināśakāriṇyaḥ || 10.138
uṣṭrakāṇḍī raktapuṣpī jñeyā karabhakāṇḍikā |
raktā lohitapuṣpī ca varṇapuṣpī ṣaḍāhvayā || 10.139
uṣṭrakāṇḍī tu tiktoṣṇā rucyā hṛdrogahāriṇī |
tadbījaṃ madhuraṃ śītaṃ vṛṣyaṃ santarpaṇaṃ smṛtam || 10.140
tagaraṃ kuṭilaṃ vakraṃ vinamraṃ kuñcitaṃ natam |
śaṭhaṃ ca nahuṣākhyaṃ ca dadruhastaṃ ca barhaṇam || 10.141
piṇḍītagarakaṃ caiva pārthivaṃ rājaharṣaṇam |
kālānusārakaṃ kṣatraṃ dīnaṃ jihmaṃ munīndudhā || 10.142
tagaraṃ śītalaṃ tiktaṃ dṛṣṭidoṣavināśanam |
viṣārtiśamanaṃ pathyaṃ bhūtonmādabhayāpaham || 10.143
atha damanakastu damano dānto gandhotkaṭo munirjaṭilaḥ |
daṇḍī ca pāṇḍurāgo brahmajaṭā puṇḍarīkaśca || 10.144
tāpasapatraḥ patrī pavitrako devaśekharaścaiva |
kulapatraśca vinītastapasvipatraśca saptadhātrīkaḥ || 10.145
damanaḥ śītalatiktaḥ kaṣāyakaṭukaśca kuṣṭhadoṣaharaḥ |
dvandvatridoṣaśamano viṣavisphoṭavikāraharaṇaḥ syāt || 10.146
anyaśca vanyadamano vanādināmā ca damanaparyāyaḥ |
vīryastambhanakārī baladāyī cāmadoṣahārī ca || 10.147
tulasī subhagā tīvrā pāvanī viṣṇuvallabhā |
surejyā surasā jñeyā kāyasthā suradundubhī || 10.148
surabhirbahupatrī ca mañjarī sā haripriyā |
apetarākṣasī śyāmā gaurī tridaśamañjarī |
bhūtaghnī pūtapatrī ca jñeyā caikonaviṃśatiḥ || 10.149
tulasī kaṭutiktoṣṇā surabhiḥ śleṣmavātajit |
jantubhūtakrimiharā rucikṛdvātaśāntikṛt || 10.150
kṛṣṇā tu kṛṣṇatulasī śvetā lakṣmīḥ sitāhvayā |
kāsavātakrimivamibhūtāpahāriṇī pūtā || 10.151
maruvaḥ kharapatrastu gandhapatraḥ phaṇiñjhakaḥ |
bahuvīryaḥ śītalakaḥ surāhvaśca samīraṇaḥ || 10.152
jambīraḥ prasthakusumo jñeyo maruvakastathā |
ājanmasurabhipatro marīcaśca trayodaśa || 10.153
dvidhā maruvakaḥ proktaḥ śvetaścaiva sitetaraḥ |
śveto bheṣajakārye syādaparaḥ śivapūjane || 10.154
maruvaḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇaḥ kṛmikuṣṭhavināśanaḥ |
viḍbandhādhmānaśūlaghno māndyatvagdoṣanāśanaḥ || 10.155
arjakaḥ kṣudratulasī kṣudraparṇo mukhārjakaḥ |
ugragandhaśca jambīraḥ kuṭheraśca kaṭhiñjaraḥ || 10.156
sitārjakastu vaikuṇṭho vaṭapatraḥ kuṭherakaḥ |
jambīro gandhabahulaḥ sumukhaḥ kaṭupatrakaḥ || 10.157
kṛṣṇārjakaḥ kālamālo mālūkaḥ kṛṣṇamālukaḥ |
syātkṛṣṇamallikā proktā garaghno vanabarbaraḥ || 10.158
trayo'rjakāḥ kaṭūṣṇāḥ syuḥ kaphavātāmayāpahāḥ |
netrāmayaharā rucyāḥ sukhaprasavakārakāḥ || 10.159
vanabarbarikānyā tu sugandhiḥ suprasannakaḥ |
doṣotkleśī viṣaghnaśca sumukhaḥ sūkṣmapatrakaḥ |
nidrāluḥ śophahārī ca suvaktraśca daśāhvayaḥ || 10.160
vanabarbarikā coṣṇā sugandhā kaṭukā ca sā |
piśācavāntibhūtaghnī ghrāṇasantarpaṇī parā || 10.161
gaṅgāpatrī tu patrī syātsugandhā gandhapatrikā |
gaṅgāpatrī kaṭūṣṇā ca vātajidvraṇaropaṇī || 10.162
pācī marakatapatrī haritalatā haritapatrikā patrī |
surabhirmallāriṣṭā gārutmatapatrikā caiva || 10.163
pācī kaṭutiktoṣṇā sakaṣāyā vātadoṣahantrī ca |
grahabhūtavikārakārī tvagdoṣapraśamanī vraṇeṣu hitā || 10.164
bālakaṃ vāriparyāyairuktaṃ hrīverakaṃ tathā |
keśyaṃ vajramudīcyaṃ ca piṅgaṃ ca lalanāpriyam |
bālaṃ ca kuntalośīraṃ kacāmodaṃ śaśīndudhā || 10.165
bālakaṃ śītalaṃ tiktaṃ pittavāntitṛṣāpaham |
jvarakuṣṭhātisāraghnaṃ keśyaṃ śvitravraṇāpanut || 10.166
barbaraḥ sumukhaścaiva garaghnaḥ kṛṣṇabarbaraḥ |
sukandano gandhapatraḥ pūtagandhaḥ surārhakaḥ || 10.167
barbaraḥ kaṭukoṣṇaśca sugandhirvāntināśanaḥ |
visarpaviṣavidhvaṃsī tvagdoṣaśamanastathā || 10.168
suraparṇaṃ devaparṇaṃ vīraparṇaṃ sugandhikam |
mañcipatraṃ sūkṣmapatraṃ devārhaṃ gandhapatrakam || 10.169
kaṭūṣṇaṃ suraparṇaṃ ca krimiśvāsabalāsajit |
dīpanaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ varṇyaṃ bālahitaṃ tathā || 10.170
ārāmaśītalā nandā śītalā sā sunandinī |
rāmā caiva mahānandā gandhāḍhyārāmaśītalā || 10.171
ārāmaśītalā tiktā śītalā pittahāriṇī |
dāhaśoṣapraśamanī visphoṭavraṇaropaṇī || 10.172
pāthojaṃ kamalaṃ nabhaṃ ca nalināmbhojāmbujanmāmbujaṃ śrīpadmāmburuhābjapadmajalajānyambhoruhaṃ sārasam |
paṅkejaṃ sarasīruhaṃ ca kuṭapaṃ pāthoruhaṃ puṣkaraṃ vārjaṃ tāmarasaṃ kuśeśayakaje kañjāravinde tathā || 10.173
śatapatraṃ bisakusumaṃ sahasrapatraṃ mahotpalaṃ vāriruham |
sarasijasalilajapaṅkeruharājīvāni vedavahnimitāni || 10.174
kamalaṃ śītalaṃ svādu raktapittaśramārtinut |
sugandhi bhrāntisaṃtāpaśāntidaṃ tarpaṇaṃ param || 10.175
puṇḍarīkaṃ śvetapatraṃ sitābjaṃ śvetavārijam |
harinetraṃ śaratpadmaṃ śāradaṃ śambhuvallabham || 10.176
puṇḍarīkaṃ himaṃ tiktaṃ madhuraṃ pittanāśanam |
dāhāsraśramadoṣaghnaṃ pipāsādoṣanāśanam || 10.177
kokanadamaruṇakamalaṃ raktāmbhojaṃ ca śoṇapadmaṃ ca |
raktotpalamaravindaṃ ravipriyaṃ raktavārijaṃ vasavaḥ || 10.178
kokanadaṃ kaṭutiktaṃ madhuraṃ śiśiraṃ ca raktadoṣaharam |
pittakaphavātaśamanaṃ santarpaṇakāraṇaṃ vṛṣyam || 10.179
utpalaṃ nīlakamalaṃ nīlābjaṃ nīlapaṅkajam |
nīlapadmaṃ ca bāṇāhvaṃ nīlādikamalābhidham || 10.180
nīlābjaṃ śītalaṃ svādu sugandhi pittanāśakṛt |
rucyaṃ rasāyane śreṣṭhaṃ keśyaṃ ca dehadārḍhyadam || 10.181
īṣatśvetaṃ padmaṃ nalinaṃ ca taduktamīṣadāraktam |
utpalamīṣannīlaṃ trividhamitīdaṃ bhavetkamalam || 10.182
utpalādirayaṃ dāharaktapittaprasādanaḥ |
pipāsādāhahṛdrogacchardimūrchāharo gaṇaḥ || 10.183
padminī nalinī proktā kūṭapinyabjinī tathā |
itthaṃ tatpadmaparyāyanāmnī jñeyā prayogataḥ || 10.184
padminī madhurā tiktā kaṣāyā śiśirā parā |
pittakṛmiśoṣavāntibhrāntisaṃtāpaśāntikṛt || 10.185
padmabījaṃ tu padmākṣaṃ gāloḍyaṃ kandalī ca sā |
bheḍā krauñcādanī krauñcā śyāmā syātpadmakarkaṭī || 10.186
padmabījaṃ kaṭu svādu pittacchardiharaṃ param |
dāhāsradoṣaśamanaṃ pācanaṃ rucikārakam || 10.187
mṛṇālaṃ padmanālaṃ ca mṛṇālī ca mṛṇālinī |
bisaṃ ca padmatantuśca bisinī nalinīruham || 10.188
mṛṇālaṃ śiśiraṃ tiktaṃ kaṣāyaṃ pittadāhajit |
mūtrakṛcchravikāraghnaṃ raktavāntiharaṃ param || 10.189
padmakandastu śālūkaṃ padmamūlaṃ kaṭāhvayam |
śālīnaṃ ca jalālūkaṃ syādityevaṃ ṣaḍāhvayam || 10.190
śālūkaṃ kaṭu viṣṭambhi rūkṣaṃ rucyaṃ kaphāpaham |
kaṣāyaṃ kāsapittaghnaṃ tṛṣṇādāhanivāraṇam || 10.191
kiñjalkaṃ makarandaṃ ca kesaraṃ padmakesaram |
kiñjaṃ pītaṃ parāgaṃ ca tuṅgaṃ cāmpeyakaṃ nava || 10.192
kiñjalkaṃ madhuraṃ rūkṣaṃ kaṭu cāsyavraṇāpaham |
pittaghnaṃ śiśiraṃ rucyaṃ tṛṣṇādāhanivāraṇam || 10.193
anūṣṇaṃ cotpalaṃ caiva rātripuṣpaṃ jalāhvayam |
himābjaṃ śītajalajaṃ niśāphullaṃ ca saptadhā || 10.194
utpalaṃ śiśiraṃ svādu pittaraktārtidoṣanut |
dāhaśramavamibhrāntikrimijvaraharaṃ param || 10.195
dhavalotpalaṃ tu kumudaṃ kahlāraṃ kairavaṃ ca śītalakam |
śaśikāntamindukamalaṃ candrābjaṃ candrikāmbujaṃ ca nava || 10.196
kumudaṃ śītalaṃ svādu pāke tiktaṃ kaphāpaham |
raktadoṣaharaṃ dāhaśramapittapraśāntikṛt || 10.197
nīlotpalamutpalakaṃ kuvalayamindīvaraṃ ca kandottham |
saugandhikaṃ sugandhaṃ kuḍmalakaṃ cāsitotpalaṃ navadhā || 10.198
nīlotpalamatisvādu śītaṃ surabhi saukhyakṛt |
pāke tu tiktamatyantaṃ raktapittāpahārakam || 10.199
utpalinī kairaviṇī kumudvatī kumudinī ca candreṣṭā |
kuvalayinīndīvariṇī nīlotpalinī ca vijñeyā || 10.200
utpalinī himatiktā raktāmayahāriṇī ca pittaghnī |
tāpakaphakāsatṛṣṇāśramavamiśamanī ca vijñeyā || 10.201
puṣpadravaḥ puṣpasāraḥ puṣpasvedaśca puṣpajaḥ |
puṣpaniryāsakaścaiva puṣpāmbujaḥ ṣaḍāhvayaḥ || 10.202
puṣpadravaḥ surabhiśītakaṣāyagaulyo dāhaśramātivamimohamukhāmayaghnaḥ |
tṛṣṇārtipittakaphadoṣaharaḥ saraśca santarpaṇaściramarocakahārakaśca || 10.203
jātī bhāti mṛdurmanojñamadhurāmodā muhūrtadvayaṃ dvaiguṇyena ca mallikā madakarī gandhādhikā yūthikā |
ekāhaṃ navamālikā madakaraṃ cāhnāṃ trayaṃ campakaṃ tīvrāmodamathāṣṭavāsaramitāmodānvitā ketakī || 10.204
itthaṃ nānāprathitasumanaḥpatrapadmābhidhānasaṃsthānoktipraguṇitatayā tadguṇākhyāpravīṇam |
vācoyuktisthiraparimalaṃ vargamenaṃ paṭhitvā nityāmodairmukhasarasijaṃ vāsayatvāśu vaidyaḥ || 10.205
sthairye śailaśilopamānyapi śanairāsādya tadbhāvanāṃ bhedyatvaṃ yamināṃ manāṃsyapi yayuḥ puṣpāśugasyāśugaiḥ |
teṣāṃ bhūṣayatāṃ surādikaśiraḥ patraprasūnātmanāṃ vargo'yaṃ vasatirmatā sumanasāmuttaṃsavargākhyayā || 10.206
lokān sparśanayogataḥ prasṛmarāṇyāmodayantyañjasā protphullāni ca yadyaśāṃsi viśadānyuttaṃsayante diśaḥ |
tasyāyaṃ daśamaḥ kṛtau sthitimagādvargo nṛsiṃheśituḥ sūrīndoḥ karavīrakādirabhidhāsambhāracūḍāmaṇau || 10.207


rājanighaṇṭu, āmrādivarga
āmrāḥ pañcavidhāḥ proktā jambūścaiva caturvidhā |
panasaḥ kadalī cābdhiḥ nārikeladvayaṃ tathā || 11.1
kharjūrī pañcadhā caiva cāro bhallātarāyaṇī |
dāḍimaṃ tindukau cātho akṣoṭaḥ pīluko dvidhā || 11.2
pārevate madhūkaṃ tu dvidhā bhavyāruke kramāt |
drākṣā tridhātha karmāraḥ paruṣaḥ pippalo vaṭaḥ || 11.3
vaṭī cāśvatthikā plakṣastathā codumbarastridhā |
tattvacā badaraṃ cābdhi bījapūraṃ tridhā matam || 11.4
āmalakyau dvidhā caiva ciñcā ciñcārasastathā |
āmrātako'tha nāraṅgo nimbūrjambīrakadvayam || 11.5
kapitthastumbaraścātha rudrākṣo bilvaśallakī |
katakaḥ karkaṭaścaiva dvidhā śleṣmātakastathā || 11.6
muṣkakaḥ karamardaśca tathā tejaḥphalastathā |
vikaṇṭakaḥ śivā saptāpyakṣaḥ pūgo'ṣṭadhā smṛtaḥ || 11.7
saptadhā nāgavallī syāccūrṇaṃ caivāṣṭadhā smṛtam |
uktā āmrādike varge śūnyacandrendusaṅkhyayā || 11.8
āmraḥ kāmaśaraścūto rasālaḥ kāmavallabhaḥ |
kāmāṅgaḥ sahakāraśca kīreṣṭo mādhavadrumaḥ || 11.9
bhṛṅgābhīṣṭaḥ sīdhuraso madhūlī kokilotsavaḥ |
vasantadūto'mlaphalo madāḍhyo manmathālayaḥ || 11.10
madhvāvāsaḥ sumadanaḥ pikarāgo nṛpapriyaḥ |
priyāmbuḥ kokilāvāsaḥ sa proktastrikarāhvayaḥ || 11.11
āmraḥ kaṣāyāmlarasaḥ sugandhiḥ kaṇṭhāmayaghno'gnikaraśca bālaḥ |
pittaprakopānilaraktadoṣapradaḥ paṭutvādirucipradaśca || 11.12
bālaṃ pittānilakaphakaraṃ tacca baddhāsthi tādṛkpakvaṃ doṣatritayaśamanaṃ svādu puṣṭiṃ guruṃ ca |
datte dhātupracayamadhikaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kāntikāri khyātaṃ tṛṣṇāśramaśamakṛtau cūtajātaṃ phalaṃ syāt || 11.13
kośāmraśca ghanaskandho vanāmro jantupādapaḥ |
kṣudrāmraśceti raktāmro lākṣāvṛkṣaḥ suraktakaḥ || 11.14
kośāmramamlamanilāpaharaṃ kaphārttipittapradaṃ guru vidāhaviśophakāri |
pakvaṃ bhavenmadhuramīṣadapāramamlaṃ paṭvādiyuktarucidīpanapuṣṭibalyam || 11.15
rājāmro'nyo rājaphalaḥ smarāmraḥ kokilotsavaḥ |
madhuraḥ kokilānandaḥ kāmeṣṭo nṛpavallabhaḥ || 11.16
anyo mahārājacūto mahārājāmrakastathā |
sthūlāmro manmathāvāsaḥ kaṅko nīlakapitthakaḥ || 11.17
kāmāyudhaḥ kāmaphalo rājaputro nṛpātmajaḥ |
mahārājaphalaḥ kāmo mahācūtastrayodaśa || 11.18
tasyāpi śreṣṭhato'nyāmro rasālo baddhapūrvakaḥ |
jñeyaścakralatāmraśca madhvāmraḥ sitajāmrakaḥ |
vanejyo manmathānando madanecchāphalo muniḥ || 11.19
rājāmrāḥ komalāḥ sarve kaṭvamlāḥ pittadāhadāḥ |
supakvāḥ svādumādhuryāḥ puṣṭivīryabalapradāḥ || 11.20
rājāmreṣu triṣu proktaṃ sāmyameva rasādhikam |
guṇādhikaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ paryāyāduttarottaram || 11.21
bālaṃ rājaphalaṃ kaphāsrapavanaśvāsārtipittapradaṃ madhyaṃ tādṛśameva doṣabahulaṃ bhūyaḥ kaṣāyāmlakam |
pakvaṃ cenmadhuraṃ tridoṣaśamanaṃ tṛṣṇāvidāhaśramaśvāsārocakamocakaṃ guru himaṃ vṛṣyāticūtāhvayam || 11.22
āmratvacā kaṣāyā ca mūlaṃ saugandhi tādṛśam |
rucyaṃ saṃgrāhi śiśiraṃ puṣpaṃ tu rucidīpanam || 11.23
jambūstu surabhipatrā nīlaphalā śyāmalā mahāskandhā |
rājārhā rājaphalā śukapriyā meghamodinī navāhvā || 11.24
jambūḥ kaṣāyamadhurā śramapittadāhakaṇṭhārtiśoṣaśamanī krimidoṣahantrī |
śvāsātisārakaphakāsavināśanī ca viṣṭambhinī bhavati rocanapācanī ca || 11.25
mahājambū rājajambūḥ svarṇamātā mahāphalā |
śukapriyā kokileṣṭā mahānīlā bṛhatphalā || 11.26
mahājambūruṣṇā samadhurakaṣāyā śramaharā nirasyatyāsyasthaṃ jhaṭiti jaḍimānaṃ svarakarī |
vidhatte viṣṭambhaṃ śamayati ca śoṣaṃ vitanute śramātīsārārttiśvasitakaphakāsapraśamanam || 11.27
kākajambūḥ kākaphalā nādeyī kākavallabhā |
bhṛṅgeṣṭā kākanīlā ca dhvāṅkṣajambūrdhanapriyā || 11.28
kākajambūḥ kaṣāyāmlā pāke tu madhurā guruḥ |
dāhaśramātisāraghnī vīryapuṣṭibalapradā || 11.29
anyā ca bhūmijambūrhrasvaphalā bhṛṅgavallabhā hrasvā |
bhūjambūrbhramareṣṭā pikabhakṣā kāṣṭhajambūśca || 11.30
bhūmijambūḥ kaṣāyā ca madhurā śleṣmapittanut |
hṛdyā saṃgrāhihṛtkaṇṭhadoṣaghnī vīryapuṣṭidā || 11.31
panasastu mahāsarjaḥ phalinaḥ phalavṛkṣakaḥ |
sthūlaḥ kaṇṭaphalaścaiva syānmūlaphaladaḥ smṛtaḥ |
apuṣpaphaladaḥ pūtaphalo hyaṅkamitastathā || 11.32
panasaṃ madhuraṃ supicchilaṃ guru hṛdyaṃ balavīryavṛddhidam |
śramadāhaviśoṣanāśanaṃ rucikṛdgrāhi ca durjaraṃ param || 11.33
īṣatkaṣāyaṃ madhuraṃ tadbījaṃ vātalaṃ guru |
tatphalasya vikāraghnaṃ rucyaṃ tvagdoṣanāśanam || 11.34
bālaṃ tu nīrasaṃ hṛdyaṃ madhyapakvaṃ tu dīpanam |
rucidaṃ lavaṇādyuktaṃ panasasya phalaṃ smṛtam || 11.35
kadalī suphalā rambhā sukumārā sakṛtphalā |
mocā gucchaphalā hastiviṣāṇī gucchadantikā || 11.36
kāṣṭhīrasā ca niḥsārā rājeṣṭā bālakapriyā |
ūrustambhā bhānuphalā vanalakṣmīśca ṣoḍaśa || 11.37
bālaṃ phalaṃ madhuramalpatayā kaṣāyaṃ pittāpahaṃ śiśirarucyamathāpi nālam |
puṣpaṃ tadapyanuguṇaṃ krimihāri kandaṃ parṇaṃ ca śūlaśamakaṃ kadalībhavaṃ syāt || 11.38
rambhāpakvaphalaṃ kaṣāyamadhuraṃ balyaṃ ca śītaṃ tathā pittaṃ cāsravimardanaṃ gurutaraṃ pathyaṃ na mandānale |
sadyaḥ śukravivṛddhidaṃ klamaharaṃ tṛṣṇāpahaṃ kāntidaṃ dīptāgnau sukhadaṃ kaphāmayakaraṃ santarpaṇaṃ durjaram || 11.39
kāṣṭhakadalī sukāṣṭhā vanakadalī kāṣṭhikā śilārambhā |
dārukadalī phalāḍhyā vanamocā cāśmakadalī ca || 11.40
syātkāṣṭhakadalī rucyā raktapittaharā himā |
gururmandāgnijananī durjarā madhurā parā || 11.41
girikadalī girirambhā parvatamocāpyaraṇyakadalī ca |
bahubījā vanarambhā girijā gajavallabhābhihitā || 11.42
girikadalī madhurahimā balavīryavivṛddhidāyinī rucyā |
tṛṭpittadāhaśoṣapraśamanakartrī ca durjarā ca guruḥ || 11.43
anyā suvarṇakadalī suvarṇarambhā ca kanakarambhā ca |
pītā suvarṇamocā campakarambhā surambhikā subhagā || 11.44
hemaphalā svarṇaphalā kanakastambhā ca pītarambhā ca |
gaurā ca gaurarambhā kāñcanakadalī surapriyā ṣaḍbhūḥ || 11.45
suvarṇamocā madhurā himā ca svalpāśane dīpanakāriṇī ca |
tṛṣṇāpahā dāhavimocanī ca kaphāvahā vṛṣyakarī guruśca || 11.46
nārikelo rasaphalaḥ sutuṅgaḥ kūrcaśekharaḥ |
dṛḍhanīlo nīlatarurmaṅgalyoccatarustathā || 11.47
tṛṇarājaḥ skandhatarurdākṣiṇātyo durāruhaḥ |
lāṅgalī tryambakaphalastathā dṛḍhaphalasthitiḥ || 11.48
nārikelo guruḥ snigdhaḥ śītaḥ pittavināśanaḥ |
arddhapakvastṛṣāśoṣaśamano durjaraḥ paraḥ || 11.49
nārikelasalilaṃ laghu balyaṃ śītalaṃ ca madhuraṃ guru pāke pittapīnasatṛṣāśramadāhaśānti śoṣaśamanaṃ sukhadāyi |
pakvametadapi kiṃcidihoktaṃ pittakāri rucidaṃ madhuraṃ ca dīpanaṃ balakaraṃ guru vṛṣyaṃ vīryavardhanamidaṃ tu vadanti || 11.50
khubaraṃ nārikelasya snigdhaṃ guru ca durjaram |
dāhaviṣṭambhadaṃ rucyaṃ balavīryavivardhanam || 11.51
madhunārikelako'nyo mādhvīkaphalaśca madhuphalo'sitajaphalaḥ |
mākṣikaphalo mṛduphalo bahukūrco hrasvaphalaśca vasugaṇitāhvaḥ || 11.52
madhuraṃ madhunārikelamuktaṃ śiśiraṃ dāhatṛṣārtipittahāri |
balapuṣṭikaraṃ ca kāntimagryaṃ kurute vīryavivardhanaṃ ca rucyam || 11.53
mādhvīkaṃ nārikelaṃ phalamatimadhuraṃ durjaraṃ jantukāri snigdhaṃ vātātisāraśramaśamanamatha dhvaṃsanaṃ vahnidīpteḥ |
āmaśleṣmaprakopaṃ janayati kurute cārukāntiṃ balaṃ ca sthairyaṃ dehasya dhatte ghanamadanakalāvardhanaṃ pittanāśam || 11.54
kharjūrī tu kharaskandhā duṣpradharṣāṃ durāruhā |
niḥśreṇī ca kaṣāyā ca yavaneṣṭā haripriyā || 11.55
kharjūrī tu kaṣāyā ca pakvā gaulyakaṣāyakā |
pittaghnī kaphadā caiva krimikṛdvṛṣyabṛṃhaṇī || 11.56
madhukharjūrī tvanyā madhukarkaṭikā ca kolakarkaṭikā |
kaṇṭakinī madhuphalikā mādhvī madhurā ca madhurakharjūrī || 11.57
madhukharjūrī madhurā vṛṣyā saṃtāpapittaśāntikarī |
śiśirā ca jantukarī bahuvīryavivardhanaṃ tanute || 11.58
bhūkharjūrī bhuktā vasudhākharjūrikā ca bhūmikharjūrī |
bhūkharjūrī madhurā śiśirā ca vidāhapittaharā || 11.59
dīpyā ca piṇḍakharjūrī sthalapiṇḍā madhusravā |
phalapuṣpā svādupiṇḍā hayabhakṣyā svarābhidhā || 11.60
tathānyā rājakharjūrī rājapiṇḍā nṛpapriyā |
munikharjūrikā vanyā rājeṣṭā ripusammitā || 11.61
piṇḍakharjūrikāyugmaṃ gaulyaṃ svāde himaṃ guru |
pittadāhārttiśvāsaghnaṃ śramahṛdvīryavṛddhidam || 11.62
dāhaghnī madhurāsrapittaśamanī tṛṣṇārtidoṣāpahā śītā śvāsakaphaśramodayaharā santarpaṇī puṣṭidā |
vahnermāndyakarī gururviṣaharā hṛdyā ca datte balaṃ snigdhā vīryavivardhanī ca kathitā piṇḍākhyakharjūrikā || 11.63
cāraḥ khadruḥ kharaskandho lalanaścārakastathā |
bahuvalkaḥ priyālaśca navadrustāpasapriyaḥ |
snehabījaścopavaṭo bhakṣabījaḥ karendudhā || 11.64
cārasya ca phalaṃ pakvaṃ vṛṣyaṃ gaulyāmlakaṃ guru |
tadbījaṃ madhuraṃ vṛṣyaṃ pittadāhārtināśanam || 11.65
bhallātako'gnirdahanastapano'ruṣkaro'nalaḥ |
krimighnastailabījaśca vātāriḥ sphoṭabījakaḥ || 11.66
pṛthagbījo dhanurbījo bhallāto bījapādapaḥ |
vahnirvarataruśceti vijñeyaḥ ṣoḍaśāhvayaḥ || 11.67
bhallātakaḥ kaṭustiktaḥ kaṣāyoṣṇaḥ krimīñjayet |
kaphavātodarānāhamehadurnāmanāśanaḥ || 11.68
bhallātasya phalaṃ kaṣāyamadhuraṃ koṣṇaṃ kaphārtiśramaśvāsānāhavibandhaśūlajaṭharādhmānakrimidhvaṃsanam |
tanmajjā ca viśoṣadāhaśamanī pittāpahā tarpaṇī vātārocakahāridīptijananī pittāpahā tvañjasā || 11.69
rājādano rājaphalaḥ kṣīravṛkṣo nṛpadrumaḥ |
nimbabījo madhuphalaḥ kapīṣṭo mādhavodbhavaḥ || 11.70
kṣīrī gucchaphalaḥ proktaḥ śukeṣṭo rājavallabhaḥ |
śrīphalo'tha dṛḍhaskandhaḥ kṣīraśuklastripañcadhā || 11.71
rājādanī tu madhurā pittahṛdgurutarpaṇī |
vṛṣyā sthaulyakarī hṛdyā susnigdhā mehanāśakṛt || 11.72
dāḍimo dāḍimīsāraḥ kuṭṭimaḥ phalaṣāḍavaḥ |
karako raktabījaśca suphalo dantabījakaḥ || 11.73
madhubījaḥ kucaphalo rocanaḥ śukavallabhaḥ |
maṇibījastathā valkaphalo vṛttaphalaśca saḥ |
sunīlo nīlapatraśca jñeyaḥ saptadaśāhvayaḥ || 11.74
dāḍimaṃ madhuramamlakaṣāyaṃ kāsavātakaphapittavināśi |
grāhi dīpanakaraṃ ca laghūṣṇaṃ śītalaṃ śramaharaṃ rucidāyi || 11.75
dāḍimaṃ dvividhamīritamāryairamlamekamaparaṃ madhuraṃ ca |
tatra vātakaphahāri kilāmlaṃ tāpahāri madhuraṃ laghu pathyam || 11.76
tinduko nīlasāraśca kālaskandho'timuktakaḥ |
sphūrjako rāmaṇaścaiva sphūrjanaḥ syandanāhvayaḥ || 11.77
tindukastu kaṣāyaḥ syātsaṃgrāhī vātakṛtparaḥ |
pakvastu madhuraḥ snigdho durjaraḥ śleṣmalo guruḥ || 11.78
tinduko'nyaḥ kākapīluḥ kākāṇḍaḥ kākatindukaḥ |
kākasphūrjaśca kākenduḥ kākāhvaḥ kākabījakaḥ || 11.79
kākatinduḥ kaṣāyo'mlo gururvātavikārakṛt |
pakvastu madhuraḥ kiṃcitkaphakṛtpittavāntihṛt || 11.80
akṣoṭaḥ pārvatīyaśca phalasneho guḍāśayaḥ |
kīreṣṭaḥ kandarālaśca madhumajjā bṛhacchadaḥ || 11.81
akṣoṭo madhuro balyaḥ snigdhoṣṇo vātapittajit |
raktadoṣapraśamanaḥ śītalaḥ kaphakopanaḥ || 11.82
pītuḥ śītaḥ sahasrāṃśī dhānī guḍaphalastathā |
virecanaphalaḥ śākhī śyāmaḥ karabhavallabhaḥ || 11.83
aṅkāhvaḥ kaṭukaḥ pīluḥ kaṣāyo madhurāmlakaḥ |
saraḥ svāduśca gulmārśaḥśamano dīpanaḥ paraḥ || 11.84
anyaścaiva bṛhatpīlurmahāpīlurmahāphalaḥ |
rājapīlurmahāvṛkṣo madhupīluḥ ṣaḍāhvayaḥ || 11.85
madhurastu mahāpīlurvṛṣyo viṣavināśanaḥ |
pittapraśamano rucya āmaghno dīpanīyakaḥ || 11.86
pārevataṃ tu raivatamārevatakaṃ ca kiṃca raivatakam |
madhuphalamamṛtaphalākhyaṃ pārevatakaṃ ca saptāhvam || 11.87
pārevataṃ tu madhuraṃ krimivātahāri vṛṣyaṃ tṛṣājvaravidāhaharaṃ ca hṛdyam |
mūrchābhramaśramaviśoṣavināśakāri snigdhaṃ ca rucyamuditaṃ bahuvīryadāyi || 11.88
mahāpārevataṃ cānyatsvarṇapārevataṃ tathā |
sāmrāṇijaṃ khārikaṃ ca raktaraivatakaṃ ca tat |
bṛhatpārevataṃ proktaṃ dvīpajaṃ dvīpakharjūrī || 11.89
mahāpārevataṃ gaulyaṃ balakṛtpuṣṭivardhanam |
vṛṣyaṃ mūrchājvaraghnaṃ ca pūrvoktādadhikaṃ guṇaiḥ || 11.90
madhūko madhuvṛkṣaḥ syātmadhuṣṭhīlo madhusravaḥ |
guḍapuṣpo lodhrapuṣpo vānaprasthaśca mādhavaḥ || 11.91
madhukaṃ madhuraṃ śītaṃ pittadāhaśramāpaham |
vātalaṃ jantudoṣaghnaṃ vīryapuṣṭivivardhanam || 11.92
anyo jalamadhūko maṅgalyo dīrghapatrako madhupuṣpaḥ |
kṣaudrapriyaḥ pataṅgaḥ kīreṣṭo gairikākṣaśca || 11.93
jñeyo jalamadhūkastu madhuro vraṇanāśanaḥ |
vṛṣyo vāntiharaḥ śīto balakārī rasāyanaḥ || 11.94
madhūkapuṣpaṃ madhuraṃ ca vṛṣyaṃ hṛdyaṃ himaṃ pittavidāhahāri |
phalaṃ ca vātāmayapittanāśi jñeyaṃ madhūkadvayamevametat || 11.95
bhavyaṃ bhavaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca bhāvanaṃ vaktraśodhanam |
tathā picchalabījaṃ ca tacca lomaphalaṃ matam || 11.96
bhavyamamlaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca bālaṃ vātakaphāpaham |
pakvaṃ tu madhurāmlaṃ ca rucikṛtsāmaśūlahṛt || 11.97
ārukaṃ vīrasenaṃ ca vīraṃ vīrārukaṃ tathā |
tatra vidyāccaturjātīḥ patrapuṣpādibhedataḥ || 11.98
ārukāṇi ca sarvāṇi madhurāṇi himāni ca |
arśaḥpramehagulmāsradoṣavidhvaṃsanāni ca || 11.99
drākṣā cāruphalā kṛṣṇā priyālā tāpasapriyā |
gucchaphalā rasālā ca jñeyāmṛtaphalā ca sā || 11.100
drākṣātimadhurāmlā ca śītā pittārtidāhajit |
mūtradoṣaharā rucyā vṛṣyā saṃtarpaṇī parā || 11.101
anyā kapiladrākṣā mṛdvīkā gostanī ca kapilaphalā |
amṛtarasā dīrghaphalā madhuvallī madhuphalā madhūlī ca || 11.102
haritā ca hārahūrā suphalā mṛdvī himottarāpathikā |
haimavatī śatavīryā kāśmīrī gajarājamahigaṇitā || 11.103
gostanī madhurā śītā hṛdyā ca madaharṣaṇī |
dāhamūrchājvaraśvāsatṛṣāhṛllāsanāśinī || 11.104
anyā sā kākalīdrākṣā jambukā ca phalottamā |
laghudrākṣā ca nirbījā suvṛttā rucikāriṇī |
śiśirā śvāsahṛllāsanāśinī janavallabhā || 11.105
drākṣābālaphalaṃ kaṭūṣṇaviśadaṃ pittāsradoṣapradaṃ madhyaṃ cāmlarasaṃ rasāntaragate rucyātivahnipradam |
pakvaṃ cenmadhuraṃ tathāmlasahitaṃ tṛṣṇāsrapittāpahaṃ pakvaṃ śuṣkatamaṃ śramārtiśamanaṃ santarpaṇaṃ puṣṭidam || 11.106
śītā pittāsradoṣaṃ damayati madhurā snigdhapākātirucyā cakṣuṣyā śvāsakāsaśramavamiśamanī śophatṛṣṇājvaraghnī |
dāhādhmānabhramādīnapanayati parā tarpaṇī pakvaśuṣkā drākṣā sukṣīṇavīryānapi madanakalākelidakṣān vidhatte || 11.107
karmāraḥ karmarakaḥ pītaphalaḥ karmaraśca mudgarakaḥ |
mudgaraphalaśca dhārāphalakastu karmārakaścaiva || 11.108
karmārako'mla uṣṇaśca vātahṛtpittakārakaḥ |
pakvastu madhurāmlaḥ syādbalapuṣṭirucipradaḥ || 11.109
parūṣakaṃ tīlaparṇaṃ giripīlu parāvaram |
nīlamaṇḍalamalpāsthi paruṣaṃ ca parustathā || 11.110
parūṣamamlaṃ kaṭukaṃ kaphārtijidvātāpahaṃ tatphalameva pittadam |
soṣṇaṃ ca pakvaṃ madhuraṃ rucipradaṃ pittāpahaṃ śophaharaṃ ca pītam || 11.111
aśvatthaścācyutāvāsaścalapatraḥ pavitrakaḥ |
śubhado bodhivṛkṣaśca yājñiko gajabhakṣakaḥ || 11.112
śrīmān kṣīradrumo vipro maṅgalyaḥ śyāmalaśca saḥ |
pippalo guhyapuṣpaśca sevyaḥ satyaḥ śucidrumaḥ |
caityadrumo dharmavṛkṣo jñeyo viṃśatisaṃjñakaḥ || 11.113
pippalaḥ sumadhurastu kaṣāyaḥ śītalaśca kaphapittavināśī |
raktadāhaśamanaḥ sa hi sadyo yonidoṣaharaṇaḥ kila pakvaḥ || 11.114
aśvatthavṛkṣasya phalāni pakvānyatīvahṛdyāni ca śītalāni |
kurvanti pittāsraviṣārtidāhavicchardiśoṣārucidoṣanāśam || 11.115
syādatha vaṭo jaṭālo nyagrodho rohiṇo'varohī ca |
viṭapī raktaphalaśca skandharuho maṇḍalī mahācchāyaḥ || 11.116
śṛṅgī yakṣāvāso yakṣataruḥ pādarohiṇo nīlaḥ |
kṣīrī śiphāruhaḥ syādbahupādaḥ sa tu vanaspatirnavabhūḥ || 11.117
vaṭaḥ kaṣāyo madhuraḥ śiśiraḥ kaphapittajit |
jvaradāhatṛṣāmohavraṇaśophāpahārakaḥ || 11.118
nadīvaṭo yajñavṛkṣaḥ siddhārtho vaṭako vaṭī |
amarā saṅginī caiva kṣīrakāṣṭhā ca kīrtitā || 11.119
vaṭī kaṣāyamadhurā śiśirā pittahāriṇī |
dāhatṛṣṇāśramaśvāsavicchardiśamanī parā || 11.120
aśvatthī laghupatrī syātpavitrā hrasvapatrikā |
pippalikā vanasthā ca kṣudrā cāśvatthasaṃnibhā || 11.121
aśvatthikā tu madhurā kaṣāyā cāsrapittajit |
viṣadāhapraśamanī gurviṇyā hitakāriṇī || 11.122
plakṣaḥ kapītanaḥ kṣīrī supārśvo'tha kamaṇḍaluḥ |
śṛṅgī varohaśākhī ca gardabhāṇḍaḥ kapītakaḥ |
dṛḍhaprarohaḥ plavakaḥ plavaṅgaśca mahābalaḥ || 11.123
plakṣaścaivāparo hrasvaḥ suśītaḥ śītavīryakaḥ |
puṇḍro mahāvarohaśca hrasvaparṇastu pimpariḥ |
bhiduro maṅgalacchāyo jñeyo dvāviṃśadhābhidhaḥ || 11.124
plakṣaḥ kaṭukaṣāyaśca śiśiro raktadoṣajit |
mūrchābhramapralāpaghno hrasvaplakṣo viśeṣataḥ || 11.125
udumbaraḥ kṣīravṛkṣo hemadugdhaḥ sadāphalaḥ |
kālaskandho yajñayogyo yajñīyaḥ supratiṣṭhitaḥ || 11.126
śītavalko jantuphalaḥ puṣpaśūnyaḥ pavitrakaḥ |
saumyaḥ śītaphalaśceti manusaṃjñaḥ samīritaḥ || 11.127
udumbaraṃ kaṣāyaṃ syātpakvaṃ tu madhuraṃ himam |
kṛmikṛtpittaraktaghnaṃ mūrchādāhatṛṣāpaham || 11.128
audumbaraṃ phalamatīva himaṃ supakvaṃ pittāpahaṃ ca madhuraṃ śramaśophahāri |
āmaṃ kaṣāyamatidīpanarocanaṃ ca māṃsasya vṛddhikaramasravikārakāri || 11.129
nadyudumbarikā cānyā laghupatraphalā tathā |
proktā laghuhemadugdhā laghupūrvasadāphalā || 11.130
laghvādyumbarāhvā syādbāṇāhvā ca prakīrtitā |
rasavīryavipākeṣu kiṃcinnyūnā ca pūrvataḥ || 11.131
kṛṣṇodumbarikā cānyā kharapatrī ca rājikā |
udumbarī ca kaṭhinā kuṣṭhaghnī phalguvāṭikā || 11.132
ajākṣī phalgunī caiva malapūścitrabheṣajā |
kākodumbarikā caiva dhvāṅkṣanāmnī trayodaśa || 11.133
kākodumbarikā śītā pakvā gaulyāmlikā kaṭuḥ |
tvagdoṣapittaraktaghnī tadvalkaṃ cātisarajit || 11.134
udumbaratvacā śītā kaṣāyā vraṇanāśinī |
gurviṇīgarbhasaṃrakṣe hitā stanyapradāyinī || 11.135
badaro badarī kolī karkandhūḥ kolaphenilau |
sauvīrako guḍaphalo bāleṣṭaḥ phalaśaiśiraḥ || 11.136
dṛḍhabījo vṛttaphalaḥ kaṇṭakī vakrakaṇṭakaḥ |
subījaḥ suphalaḥ svacchaḥ surasaḥ smṛtisammitaḥ || 11.137
badaraṃ madhuraṃ kaṣāyamamlaṃ paripakvaṃ madhurāmlamuṣṇametat |
kaphakṛtpacanātisāraraktaśramaśoṣārtivināśanaṃ ca rucyam || 11.138
badarasya patralepo jvaradāhavināśanaḥ |
tvacā visphoṭaśamanī bījaṃ netrāmayāpaham || 11.139
rājabadaro nṛpeṣṭo nṛpabadaro rājavallabhaścaiva |
pṛthukolastanubījo madhuraphalo rājakolaśca || 11.140
rājabadaraḥ sumadhuraḥ śiśiro dāhārtipittavātaharaḥ |
vṛṣyaśca vīryavṛddhiṃ kurute śoṣaśramaṃ harati || 11.141
bhūbadarī kṣitibadarī vallībadarī ca badarivallī ca |
bahuphalikā laghubadarī badaraphalī sūkṣmabadarī ca || 11.142
bhūbadarī madhurāmlā kaphavātavikārahāriṇī pathyā |
dīpanapācanakartrī kiṃcitpittāsrakāriṇī rucyā || 11.143
sūkṣmaphalo laghubadaro bahukaṇṭaḥ sūkṣmapatrako duḥsparśaḥ |
madhuraḥ śambarāhāraḥ śikhipriyaścaiva nirdiṣṭaḥ || 11.144
laghubadaraṃ madhurāmlaṃ pakvaṃ kaphavātanāśanaṃ rucyam |
snigdhaṃ tu jantukārakamīṣatpittārtidāhaśoṣaghnam || 11.145
bījapūro bījapūrṇaṃ pūrṇabījaḥ sukesaraḥ |
bījakaḥ keśarāmlaśca mātuluṅgaḥ supūrakaḥ || 11.146
rucako bījaphalako jantughno danturatvacaḥ |
pūrako rocanaphalo dvidevamunisammitaḥ || 11.147
bījapūraphalamamlakaṭūṣṇaṃ śvāsakāsaśamanaṃ pacanaṃ ca |
kaṇṭhaśodhanaparaṃ laghu hṛdyaṃ dīpanaṃ ca rucikṛjjaraṇaṃ ca || 11.148
bālaṃ pittamarutkaphāsrakaraṇaṃ madhyaṃ ca tādṛgvidhaṃ pakvaṃ varṇakaraṃ ca hṛdyamatha tatpuṣṇāti puṣṭiṃ balam |
śūlājīrṇavibandhamārutakaphaśvāsārtimandāgnijitkāsārocakaśophaśāntidamidaṃ syānmātuluṅgaṃ sadā || 11.149
tvaktiktā durjarā syātkṛmikaphapavanadhvaṃsinī snigdhamuṣṇaṃ madhyaṃ śūlārtipittapraśamanamakhilārocakaghnaṃ ca gaulyam |
vātārtighnaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ jaṭharagadaharaṃ kesaraṃ dīpyamamlaṃ bījaṃ tiktaṃ kaphārśaḥśvayathuśamakaraṃ bījapūrasya pathyam || 11.150
vanabījapūrako'nyo vanajo vanapūrakaśca vanabījaḥ |
atyamlā gandhāḍhyā vanodbhavā devadūtī ca || 11.151
pītā ca devadāsī deveṣṭā mātuluṅgikā caiva |
pavanī mahāphalā ca syādiyamiti vedabhūmimitā || 11.152
amlaḥ kaṭūṣṇo vanabījapūro ruciprado vātavināśanaśca |
syādamladoṣakṛmināśakārī kaphāpahaḥ śvāsaniṣūdanaśca || 11.153
madhurabījapūro madhuparṇī madhurakarkaṭī madhuvallī |
madhukarkaṭī madhuraphalā mahāphalā vardhamānā ca || 11.154
madhukarkaṭī madhurā śiśirā dāhanāśanī |
tridoṣaśamanī rucyā vṛṣyā ca gurudurjarā || 11.155
āmalakī vayaḥsthā ca śrīphalā dhātrikā tathā |
amṛtā ca śivā śāntā śītāmṛtaphalā tathā || 11.156
jātīphalā ca dhātreyī jñeyā dhātrīphalā tathā |
vṛṣyā vṛttaphalā caiva rocanī śarabhūhvayā || 11.157
āmalakaṃ kaṣāyāmlaṃ madhuraṃ śiśiraṃ laghu |
dāhapittavamīmehaśophaghnaṃ ca rasāyanam || 11.158
kaṭu madhurakaṣāyaṃ kiṃcidamlaṃ kaphaghnaṃ rucikaramatiśītaṃ hanti pittāsratāpam |
śramavamanavibandhādhmānaviṣṭambhadoṣapraśamanamamṛtābhaṃ cāmalakyāḥ phalaṃ syāt || 11.159
anyaccāmalakaṃ proktaṃ kāṣṭhadhātrīphalaṃ tathā |
kṣudrāmalakasaṃproktaṃ kṣudrajātīphalaṃ ca tat || 11.160
kāṣṭhadhātrīphalaṃ svāde kaṣāyaṃ kaṭukaṃ tathā |
śītaṃ pittāsradoṣaghnaṃ pūrvoktamadhikaṃ guṇaiḥ || 11.161
ciñcā tu cukrikā cukrā sāmlikā śākacukrikā |
amlī sutintiḍī cāmlā cukrikā ca navābhidhā || 11.162
ciñcātyamlā bhavedāmā pakvā tu madhurāmlikā |
vātaghnī pittadāhāsrakaphadoṣaprakopaṇī || 11.163
amlikāyāḥ phalaṃ tvāmamatyamlaṃ laghu pittakṛt |
pakvaṃ tu madhurāmlaṃ syādbhedi viṣṭambhavātajit || 11.164
pakvaciñcāphalaraso madhurāmlo rucipradaḥ |
śophapākakaro lepādvraṇadoṣavināśanaḥ || 11.165
ciñcāpatraṃ ca śophaghnaṃ raktadoṣavyathāpaham |
tasyāḥ śuṣkatvacākṣāraṃ śūlamandāgnināśanam || 11.166
amlasārastu śākāmlaṃ cukrāmlaṃ cāmlacukrikā |
ciñcāmlamamlacūḍaśca ciñcāraso'pi saptadhā || 11.167
amlasārastvatīvāmlo vātaghnaḥ kaphadāhakṛt |
sāmyena śarkarāmiśro dāhapittakaphārttinut || 11.168
āmrātakaḥ pītanakaḥ kapicūto'mlavāṭakaḥ |
śṛṅgī kapirasāḍhyaśca tanukṣīraḥ kapipriyaḥ || 11.169
āmrātakaṃ kaṣāyāmlamāmahṛtkaṇṭhaharṣaṇam |
pakvaṃ tu madhurāmlāḍhyaṃ snigdhaṃ pittakaphāpaham || 11.170
nāraṅgaḥ syānnāgaraṅgaḥ suraṅgastvaggandhaścairāvato vaktravāsaḥ |
yogīraṅgo nāgaro yogaraṅgaḥ gandhāḍhyo'yaṃ gandhapatro ravīṣṭaḥ || 11.171
nāraṅgaṃ madhuraṃ cāmlaṃ gurūṣṇaṃ caiva rocanam |
vātāmakrimiśūlaghnaṃ śramahṛdbalarucyadam || 11.172
nimbūkaḥ syādamlajambīrakākhyo vahnirdīpyo vahnibījo'mlasāraḥ |
dantāghātaḥ śodhano jantumārī nimbūśca syādrocano rudrasaṃjñaḥ || 11.173
nimbūphalaṃ prathitamamlarasaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ gulmāmavātaharamagnivivṛddhikāri |
cakṣuṣyametadatha kāsakaphārttikaṇṭhavicchardihāri paripakvamatīva rucyam || 11.174
jambīro dantaśaṭho jambho jambhīrajambhalau caiva |
rocanako mukhaśodhī jāḍyārirjantujinnavadhā || 11.175
jambīrasya phalaṃ rase'mlamadhuraṃ vātāpahaṃ pittakṛtpathyaṃ pācanarocanaṃ balakaraṃ vahnervivṛddhipradam |
pakvaṃ cenmadhuraṃ kaphārttiśamanaṃ pittāsradoṣāpanutvarṇyaṃ vīryavivarddhanaṃ ca rucikṛtpuṣṭipradaṃ tarpaṇam || 11.176
anyo madhujambīro madhujambho madhurajambhalaścaiva |
śaṅkhadrāvī śarkarakaḥ pittadrāvī ca ṣaṭsaṃjñaḥ || 11.177
madhuro madhujambīraḥ śiśiraḥ kaphapittanut |
śoṣaghnastarpaṇo vṛṣyaḥ śramaghnaḥ puṣṭikārakaḥ || 11.178
mālūrastu kapittho maṅgalyo nīlamallikā ca dadhi |
grāhiphalaścirapākī granthiphalaḥ kucaphalo dadhiphalaśca || 11.179
gandhaphalaśca kapīṣṭo vṛttaphalaḥ karabhavallabhaścaiva |
dantaśaṭhaḥ kaṭhinaphalaḥ karaṇḍaphalakaśca saptadaśasaṃjñaḥ || 11.180
kapittho madhurāmlaśca kaṣāyastiktaśītalaḥ |
vṛṣyaḥ pittānilaṃ hanti saṃgrāhī vraṇanāśanaḥ || 11.181
āmaṃ kapitthamamloṣṇāṃ kaphaghnaṃ grāhi vātalam |
doṣatrayaharaṃ pakvaṃ madhurāmlarasaṃ guru || 11.182
āmaṃ kaṇṭharujaṃ kapitthamadhikaṃ jihvājaḍatvāvahaṃ taddoṣatrayavardhanaṃ viṣaharaṃ saṃgrāhakaṃ rocakam |
pakvaṃ śvāsavamiśramaklamaharaṃ hikkāpanodakṣamaṃ sarvaṃ grāhi rucipradaṃ ca kathitaṃ sevyaṃ tataḥ sarvadā || 11.183
tumbaruḥ saurabhaḥ sauro vanajaḥ sānujo dvijaḥ |
tīkṣṇavalkastīkṣṇaphalastīkṣṇapatro mahāmuniḥ |
sphuṭaphalaḥ sugandhiśca sa prokto dvādaśāhvayaḥ || 11.184
tumbarurmadhurastiktaḥ kaṭūṣṇaḥ kaphavātanut |
śūlagulmodarādhmānakṛmighno vahnidīpanaḥ || 11.185
rudrākṣaśca śivākṣaśca śarvākṣo bhūtanāśanaḥ |
pāvano nīlakaṇṭhākṣo harākṣaśca śivapriyaḥ || 11.186
rudrākṣamamlamuṣṇaṃ ca vātaghnaṃ kaphanāśanam |
śiraārtiśamanaṃ rucyaṃ bhūtagrahavināśanam || 11.187
bilvaḥ śalyo hṛdyagandhaḥ śalāṭuḥ śāṇḍilyaḥ syācchrīphalaḥ karkaṭāhvaḥ |
śailūṣaḥ syācchaivapatraḥ śiveṣṭaḥ patraśreṣṭho gandhapatrastripattraḥ || 11.188
lakṣmīphalo gandhaphalo durāruhastriśākapattrastriśikhaḥ śivadrumaḥ |
sadāphalaḥ satphaladaḥ subhūtikaḥ samīrasāraḥ śikhinetrasaṃjñitaḥ || 11.189
bilvastu madhuro hṛdyaḥ kaṣāyaḥ pittajitguruḥ |
kaphajvarātisāraghno rucikṛddīpanaḥ paraḥ || 11.190
bilvamūlaṃ tridoṣaghnaṃ madhuraṃ laghu vātanut |
phalaṃ tu komalaṃ snigdhaṃ guru saṃgrāhi dīpanam || 11.191
tadeva pakvaṃ vijñeyaṃ madhuraṃ sarasaṃ guru |
kaṭutiktakaṣāyoṣṇaṃ saṃgrāhi ca tridoṣajit || 11.192
sallakaḥ sallakī sallī sugandhā surabhisravā |
surabhirgajabhakṣyā ca suvahā gajavallabhā || 11.193
gandhamūlā mukhāmodā suśrīkā jalavikramā |
hṛdyā kuṇṭarikā caiva proktā tryasraphalā ca sā |
chinnaruhā gandhaphalā jñeyā cāṣṭādaśāhvayā || 11.194
sallakī tiktamadhurā kaṣāyā grāhiṇī parā |
kuṣṭhāsrakaphavātārśovraṇadoṣārtināśinī || 11.195
katako'mbuprasādaśca katastiktaphalastathā |
rucyastu chedanīyaśca jñeyo guḍaphalaḥ smṛtaḥ |
proktaḥ kataphalastiktamarīcaśca navāhvayaḥ || 11.196
katakaḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇaścakṣuṣyaḥ kṛmidoṣanut |
rucikṛcchūladoṣaghno bījamambuprasādanam || 11.197
karkaṭaḥ kārkaṭaḥ karkaḥ kṣudradhātrī ca sa smṛtaḥ |
kṣudrāmalakasaṃjñaśca proktaḥ karkaphalaśca ṣaṭ || 11.198
kārkaṭaṃ tu phalaṃ rucyaṃ kaṣāyaṃ dīpanaṃ param |
kaphapittaharaṃ grāhi cakṣuṣyaṃ laghu śītalam || 11.199
śleṣmātako bahuvāraḥ picchalo dvijakutsitaḥ |
śeluḥ śītaphalaḥ śītaḥ śākaṭaḥ karbudārakaḥ |
bhūtadrumo gandhapuṣpaḥ khyāta ekādaśāhvayaḥ || 11.200
śleṣmātakaḥ kaṭuhimo madhuraḥ kaṣāyaḥ svāduśca pācanakaraḥ krimiśūlahārī |
āmāsradoṣamalarodhabahuvraṇārtivisphoṭaśāntikaraṇaḥ kaphakārakaśca || 11.201
bhūkarbudārakaścānyaḥ kṣudraśleṣmātakastathā |
bhūśelurlaghuśeluśca picchalo laghupūrvakaḥ |
laghuśītaḥ sūkṣmaphalo laghubhūtadrumaśca saḥ || 11.202
bhūkarbudāro madhuraḥ krimidoṣavināśanaḥ |
vātaprakopaṇaḥ kiṃcitsaśītaḥ svarṇamārakaḥ || 11.203
muṣkako mocako muṣko mokṣako muñcakastathā |
gauliko mehanaścaiva kṣāravṛkṣaśca pāṭaliḥ || 11.204
viṣāpaho jaṭālaśca vanavāsī sutīkṣṇakaḥ |
śvetaḥ kṛṣṇaśca sa dvedhā syāttrayodaśasaṃjñakaḥ || 11.205
muṣkakaḥ kaṭuko'mlaśca rocanaḥ pācanaḥ paraḥ |
plīhagulmodarārtighno dvidhā tulyaguṇānvitaḥ || 11.206
karamardaḥ suṣeṇaśca karāmlaḥ karamardakaḥ |
avignaḥ pāṇimardaśca kṛṣṇapākaphalo muniḥ || 11.207
karamardaḥ satiktāmlo bālo dīpanadāhakaḥ |
pakvastridoṣaśamano'rucighno viṣanāśanaḥ || 11.208
tejaḥphalo bahuphalastathoktaḥ śālmalīphalaḥ |
phalastīkṣṇādisaṃyuktaḥ phalāntastavakādikaḥ |
steyīphalo gandhaphalaḥ kaṇṭavṛkṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 11.209
tejaḥphalaḥ kaṭustīkṣṇaḥ sugandhirdīpanaḥ paraḥ |
vātaśleṣmārucighnaśca bālarakṣākaraḥ paraḥ || 11.210
vikaṇṭako mṛduphalo granthilaḥ svādukaṇṭakaḥ |
gokaṇṭakaḥ kākanāśo vyāghrapādo ghanadrumaḥ || 11.211
garjāphalo ghanaphalo meghastanitodbhavaśca mudiraphalaḥ |
prāvṛṣyo hāsyaphalaḥ stanitaphalaḥ pañcadaśasaṃjñaḥ || 11.212
vikaṇṭakaḥ kaṣāyaḥ syātkaṭū rūkṣo rucipradaḥ |
dīpanaḥ kaphahārī ca vastraraṅgavidhāyakaḥ || 11.213
harītakī hemavatī jayābhayā śivāvyathā cetanikā ca rohiṇī |
pathyā prapathyāpi pūtanāmṛtā jīvapriyā jīvanikā bhiṣagvarā || 11.214
jīvantī prāṇadā jīvyā kāyasthā śreyasī ca sā |
devī divyā ca vijayā vahninetramitābhidhā || 11.215
harītakī pañcarasā ca recanī koṣṭhāmayaghnī lavaṇena varjitā |
rasāyanī netrarujāpahāriṇī tvagāmayaghnī kila yogavāhinī || 11.216
bījāsthitiktā madhurā tadantastvagbhāgataḥ sā kaṭuruṣṇavīryā |
māṃsāṃśataścāmlakaṣāyayuktā harītakī pañcarasā smṛteyam || 11.217
harītakyamṛtotpannā saptabhedairudīritā |
tasyā nāmāni varṇāṃśca vakṣyāmyatha yathākramam || 11.218
vijayā rohiṇī caiva pūtanā cāmṛtābhayā |
jīvantī cetakī ceti nāmnā saptavidhā matā || 11.219
alābunābhirvijayā suvṛttā rohiṇī matā |
svalpatvakpūtanā jñeyā sthūlamāṃsāmṛtā smṛtā || 11.220
pañcāsrā cābhayā jñeyā jīvantī svarṇavarṇabhāk |
tryasrāṃ tu cetakīṃ vidyādityāsāṃ rūpalakṣaṇam || 11.221
vindhyādrau vijayā himācalabhavā syāccetakī pūtanā sindhau syādatha rohiṇī tu vijayā jātā pratiṣṭhānake |
campāyāmamṛtābhayā ca janitā deśe surāṣṭrāhvaye jīvantīti harītakī nigaditā saptaprabhedā budhaiḥ || 11.222
sarvaprayoge vijayā ca rohiṇī kṣateṣu lepeṣu tu pūtanoditā |
virecane syādamṛtā guṇādhikā jīvantikā syādiha jīrṇarogajit || 11.223
syāccetakī sarvarujāpahārikā netrāmayaghnīmabhayāṃ vadanti |
itthaṃ yathāyogamiyaṃ prayojitā jñeyā guṇāḍhyā na kadācidanyathā || 11.224
cetakī ca dhṛtā haste yāvattiṣṭhati dehinaḥ |
tāvadviricyate vegāttatprabhāvānna saṃśayaḥ || 11.225
saptānāmapi jātīnāṃ pradhānaṃ vijayā smṛtā |
sukhaprayogasulabhā sarvavyādhiṣu śasyate || 11.226
kṣiptāpsu nimajjati yā sā jñeyā guṇavatī bhiṣagvaryaiḥ |
yasyā yasyā bhūyo nimajjanaṃ sā guṇāḍhyā syāt || 11.227
harate prasabhaṃ vyādhīn bhūyastarati yadvapuḥ |
harītakī tu sā proktā tatra kīrdīptivācakaḥ || 11.228
harītakī tu tṛṣṇāyāṃ hanustambhe galagrahe |
śoṣe navajvare jīrṇe gurviṇyāṃ naiva śasyate || 11.229
vibhītakastailaphalo bhūtāvāsaḥ kalidrumaḥ |
saṃvartakastu vāsantaḥ kalkivṛkṣo vaheḍakaḥ || 11.230
hāryaḥ karṣaphalaḥ kalkirdharmaghno'kṣo'nilaghnakaḥ |
bahuvīryaśca kāsaghnaḥ sa proktaḥ ṣoḍaśāhvayaḥ || 11.231
vibhītakaḥ kaṭustiktaḥ kaṣāyoṣṇaḥ kaphāpahaḥ |
cakṣuṣyaḥ palitaghnaśca vipāke madhuro laghuḥ || 11.232
pūgastu pūgavṛkṣaśca kramuko dīrghapādapaḥ |
valkatarurdṛḍhavalkaścikkaṇaśca munirmataḥ || 11.233
pūgavṛkṣasya niryāso himaḥ saṃmohano guruḥ |
vipāke soṣṇakakṣāraḥ sāmlo vātaghnapittalaḥ || 11.234
pūgaṃ tu cikkaṇī cikkā cikkaṇaṃ ślakṣṇakaṃ tathā |
udvegaṃ kramukaphalaṃ jñeyaṃ pūgaphalaṃ vasu || 11.235
serī ca madhurā rucyā kaṣāyāmlā kaṭustathā |
pathyā ca kaphavātaghnī sārikā mukhadoṣanut || 11.236
tailvanaṃ madhuraṃ rucyaṃ kaṇṭhaśuddhikaraṃ laghu |
tridoṣaśamanaṃ dīpyaṃ rasālaṃ pācanaṃ samam || 11.237
gaulyaṃ guhāgaraṃ ślakṣṇaṃ kaṣāyaṃ kaṭu pācanam |
viṣṭambhajaṭharādhmānaharaṇaṃ drāvakaṃ laghu || 11.238
ghoṇṭā kaṭukaṣāyoṣṇā kaṭhinā rucikāriṇī |
malaviṣṭambhaśamanī pittahṛddīpanī ca sā || 11.239
pūgīphalaṃ ceulasaṃjñakaṃ yattatkoṅkaṇeṣu prathitaṃ sugandhi |
śleṣmāpahaṃ dīpanapācanaṃ ca balapradaṃ puṣṭikaraṃ rasāḍhyam || 11.240
yatkoṅkaṇe valligulābhidhānakaṃ grāmodbhavaṃ pūgaphalaṃ tridoṣanut |
āmāpahaṃ rocanarucyapācanaṃ viṣṭambhatundāmayahāri dīpanam || 11.241
candrāpurodbhavaṃ pūgaṃ kaphaghnaṃ malaśodhanam |
kaṭu svādu kaṣāyaṃ ca rucyaṃ dīpanapācanam |
āndhradeśodbhavaṃ pūgaṃ kaṣāyaṃ madhuraṃ rase |
vātajidvaktrajāḍyaghnamīṣadamlaṃ kaphāpaham || 11.242
pūgaṃ sammohakṛtsarvaṃ kaṣāyaṃ svādu recanam |
tridoṣaśamanaṃ rucyaṃ vaktrakledamalāpaham || 11.243
āmaṃ pūgaṃ kaṣāyaṃ mukhamalaśamanaṃ kaṇṭhaśuddhiṃ vidhatte raktāmaśleṣmapittapraśamanamudarādhmānahāraṃ saraṃ ca |
śuṣkaṃ kaṇṭhāmayaghnaṃ rucikaramuditaṃ pācanaṃ recanaṃ syāttatparṇenāyutaṃ cetjhaṭiti vitanute pāṇḍuvātaṃ ca śoṣam || 11.244
atha bhavati nāgavallī tāmbūlī phaṇilatā ca saptaśirā |
parṇalatā phaṇivallī bhujagalatā bhakṣyapatrī ca || 11.245
nāgavallī kaṭustīkṣṇā tiktā pīnasavātajit |
kaphakāsaharā rucyā dāhakṛddīpanī parā || 11.246
sā śrīvāṭyamlādivāṭādinānāgrāmastomasthānabhedādvibhinnā |
ekāpyeṣā deśamṛtsnāviśeṣānnānākāraṃ yāti kāye guṇe ca || 11.247
śrīvāṭī madhurā tīkṣṇā vātapittakaphāpahā |
rasāḍhyā surasā rucyā vipāke śiśirā smṛtā || 11.248
syādamlavāṭī kaṭukāmlatiktā tīkṣṇā tathoṣṇā mukhapākakartrī |
vidāhapittāsravikopanī ca viṣṭambhadā vātanibarhaṇī ca || 11.249
satasā madhurā tīkṣṇā kaṭuruṣṇā ca pācanī |
gulmodarādhmānaharā rucikṛddīpanī parā || 11.250
guhāgare saptaśirā prasiddhā sāparṇajūrṇātirasātirucyā |
sugandhi tīkṣṇā madhurātihṛdyā sandīpanī puṃstvakarātibalyā || 11.251
nāmnānyāmlasarā sutīkṣṇamadhurā rucyā himā dāhanutpittodrekaharā sudīpanakarī balyā mukhāmodinī |
strīsaubhāgyavivardhanī madakarī rājñāṃ sadā vallabhā gulmādhmānavibandhajicca kathitā sā mālave tu sthitā || 11.252
andhre paṭulikā nāma kaṣāyoṣṇā kaṭustathā |
malāpakarṣā kaṇṭhasya pittakṛdvātanāśanī || 11.253
hvesaṇīyā kaṭustīkṣṇā hṛdyā dīrghadalā ca sā |
kaphavātaharā rucyā kaṭurdīpanapācanī || 11.254
sadyastroṭitabhakṣitaṃ mukharujājāḍyāvahaṃ doṣakṛtdāhārocakaraktadāyi malakṛdviṣṭambhi vāntipradam |
yadbhūyo jalapānapoṣitarasaṃ tacceccirāttroṭitaṃ tāmbūlīdalamuttamaṃ ca rucikṛdvarṇyaṃ tridoṣārtinut || 11.255
kṛṣṇaṃ parṇaṃ tiktamuṣṇaṃ kaṣāyaṃ dhatte dāhaṃ vaktrajādyaṃ malaṃ ca |
śubhraṃ parṇaṃ śleṣmavātāmayaghnaṃ pathyaṃ rucyaṃ dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ ca || 11.256
śirā parṇasya śaithilyaṃ kuryāttasyāsrahṛdrasaḥ |
śīrṇaṃ tvagdoṣadaṃ tasya bhakṣite ca śitaṃ sadā || 11.257
anidhāya mukhe parṇaṃ pūgaṃ khādāta yo naraḥ |
matibhraṃśo daridraḥ syādante smarati no harim || 11.258
parṇādhikye dīpanī raṅgadātrī pūgādhikye rūkṣadā kṛcchradātrī |
sārādhikye khādire śoṣadātrī cūrṇādhikye pittakṛtpūtigandhā || 11.259
cūrṇaṃ cārjunavṛkṣajaṃ kaphaharaṃ gulmaghnamarkāhvayaṃ śophaghnaṃ kuṭajaṃ karañjajanitaṃ vātāpahaṃ rucyadam |
pittaghnaṃ jalajaṃ balāgnirucidaṃ śailāhvayaṃ pittadaṃ sphāṭikyaṃ dṛḍhadantapaṅktijananaṃ śuktyādijaṃ rūkṣadam || 11.260
itthaṃ nānāphalatarulatānāmatattadguṇādivyaktākhyānapraguṇaracanācārusaurabhyasāram |
vargaṃ vaktrāmburuhavalabhīlāsyalīlārasālaṃ vidyāvaidyaḥ khalu saphalayedetamāmnāyabhūmnā || 11.261
yānyupabhuñjānānāṃ sa bhavati saṃsārapādapaḥ saphalaḥ |
teṣāmeṣa phalānāṃ vargaḥ phalavarga iti kathitaḥ || 11.262
yasyājasravikasvarāmalayaśaḥprāgbhārapuṣpodgamaḥ sāścaryaṃ vibudhepsitāni phalati śrīmān karaḥ svardrumaḥ |


rājanighaṇṭu, Candanādivarga
śrīkhaṇḍaṃ śabaraṃ pītaṃ patrāṅgaṃ raktacandanam |
barbaraṃ harigandhaṃ ca candanaṃ saptadhā smṛtam || 12.1
devadāru dvidhā proktaṃ cīḍā saptacchadastathā |
saralaḥ kuṅkume kaṅguḥ kastūrī rocanā tathā || 12.2
karpūrau syājjavādistu nandī ca jātipatrikā |
jātīphalaṃ ca kakkolaṃ lavaṅgaṃ svādurucyate || 12.3
agarvyaśca tridhā māṃsī turuṣko guggulustridhā |
rālaḥ kundurukaḥ kuṣṭhaṃ sārivā tu dvidhā nakhau || 12.4
spṛkkā sthauṇeyakaṃ caiva murā śaileyacorakaḥ |
padmaprapauṇḍarīke ca lāmajjaṃ rohiṇī dvidhā |
śrīveṣṭośīranalikā munibāṇamitāhvayāḥ || 12.5
śrīkhaṇḍaṃ candanaṃ proktaṃ mahārhaṃ śvetacandanam |
gośīrṣaṃ tilaparṇaṃ ca maṅgalyaṃ malayodbhavam || 12.6
gandharājaṃ sugandhaṃ ca sarpāvāsaṃ ca śītalam |
gandhāḍhyaṃ gandhasāraṃ ca bhadraśrīrbhogivallabham |
śītagandho malayajaṃ pāvanaṃ cāṅkabhūhvayam || 12.7
śrīkhaṇḍaṃ kaṭutiktaśītalaguṇaṃ svāde kaṣāyaṃ kiyatpittabhrāntivamijvarakrimitṛṣāsaṃtāpaśāntipradam |
vṛṣyaṃ vaktrarujāpahaṃ pratanute kāntiṃ tanordehināṃ liptaṃ suptamanojasindhuramadārambhādisaṃrambhadam || 12.8
śreṣṭhaṃ koṭarakarparopakalitaṃ sugranthi sadgauravaṃ chede raktamayaṃ tathā ca vimalaṃ pītaṃ ca yaddharṣaṇe |
svāde tiktakaṭuḥ sugandhabahulaṃ śītaṃ yadalpaṃ guṇe kṣīṇaṃ cārdhaguṇānvitaṃ tu kathitaṃ taccandanaṃ madhyamam || 12.9
candanaṃ dvividhaṃ proktaṃ beṭṭasukvaḍisaṃjñakam |
beṭṭaṃ tu sārdravicchedaṃ svayaṃ śuṣkaṃ tu sukvaḍi || 12.10
malayādrisamīpasthāḥ parvatā beṭṭasaṃjñakāḥ |
tajjātaṃ candanaṃ yattu beṭṭavācyaṃ kvacinmate || 12.11
beṭṭacandanamatīva śītalaṃ dāhapittaśamanaṃ jvarāpaham |
chardimohatṛṣikuṣṭhataimirotkāsaraktaśamanaṃ ca tiktakam || 12.12
sukvaḍicandanaṃ tiktaṃ kṛcchrapittāsradāhanut |
śaityasugandhadaṃ cārdraṃ śuṣkaṃ lepe tadanyathā || 12.13
nātipītaṃ kairātaṃ śabaraṃ candanaṃ sugandham |
vanyaṃ ca gandhakāṣṭhaṃ kirātakāntaṃ ca śailagandhaṃ ca || 12.14
kairātamuṣṇaṃ kaṭuśītalaṃ ca śleṣmānilaghnaṃ śramapittahāri |
visphoṭapāmādikanāśanaṃ ca tṛṣāpahaṃ tāpavimohanāśi || 12.15
pītagandhaṃ tu kālīyaṃ pītakaṃ mādhavapriyam |
kālīyakaṃ pītakāṣṭhaṃ barbaraṃ pītacandanam || 12.16
pītaṃ ca śītalaṃ tiktaṃ kuṣṭhaśleṣmānilāpaham |
kaṇḍūvicarcikādadrukṛmihṛtkāntidaṃ param || 12.17
pattaṅgaṃ caiva patrāṅgaṃ raktakāṣṭhaṃ suraṅgadam |
patrāḍhyaṃ paṭṭarāgaṃ ca bhāryāvṛkṣaśca raktakaḥ || 12.18
lohitaṃ raṅgakāṣṭhaṃ ca rāgakāṣṭhaṃ kucandanam |
paṭṭarañjanakaṃ caiva suraṅgaṃ ca caturdaśa || 12.19
patrāṅgaṃ kaṭukaṃ rūkṣamamlaṃ śītaṃ tu gaulyakam |
vātapittajvaraghnaṃ ca visphoṭonmādabhūtahṛt || 12.20
raktacandanamidaṃ ca lohitaṃ śoṇitaṃ ca haricandanaṃ himam |
raktasāramatha tāmrasārakaṃ kṣudracandanamathārkacandanam || 12.21
raktacandanamatīva śītalaṃ tiktamīkṣaṇagadāsradoṣanut |
bhūtapittakaphakāsasajvarabhrāntijantuvamijittṛṣāpaham || 12.22
barbarotthaṃ barbarakaṃ śvetabarbarakaṃ tathā |
śītaṃ sugandhi pittāri surabhi ceti saptadhā || 12.23
barbaraṃ śītalaṃ tiktaṃ kaphamārutapittajit |
kuṣṭhakaṇḍūvraṇān hanti viśeṣādraktadoṣajit || 12.24
haricandanaṃ surārhaṃ harigandhamindracandanaṃ divyam |
divijaṃ ca mahāgandhaṃ nandanajaṃ lohitaṃ ca navasaṃjñam || 12.25
haricandanaṃ tu divyaṃ tiktahimaṃ tadiha durlabhaṃ manujaiḥ |
pittāṭopavilopi candanavacchramaśoṣamāndyatāpaharam || 12.26
candanāni samānāni rasato vīryatastathā |
bhidyante kiṃtu gandhena tatrādyaṃ guṇavattaram || 12.27
devadāru suradāru dārukaṃ snigdhadāruramarādidāru ca |
bhadradāru śivadāru śāmbhavaṃ bhūtahāri bhavadāru rudravat || 12.28
snigdhadāru smṛtaṃ tiktaṃ snigdhoṣṇaṃ śleṣmavātajit |
āmadoṣavibandhārśaḥpramehajvaranāśanam || 12.29
devakāṣṭhaṃ pūtikāṣṭhaṃ bhadrakāṣṭhaṃ sukāṣṭhakam |
asnigdhadārukaṃ caiva kāṣṭhadāru ṣaḍāhvayam || 12.30
devakāṣṭhaṃ tu tiktoṣṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ śleṣmānilāpaham |
bhūtadoṣāpahaṃ dhatte liptamaṅgeṣu kālikam || 12.31
devadāru dvidhā jñeyaṃ tatrādyaṃ snigdhadārukam |
dvitīyaṃ kāṣṭhadāru syāddvayornāmānyabhedataḥ || 12.32
cīḍā ca dārugandhā gandhavadhūrgandhamādanī taruṇī |
tārā ca bhūtamārī maṅgalyā tu kapāṭinī grahabhītijit |
cīḍā kaṭūṣṇā kāsaghnī kaphajiddīpanī parā |
atyantasevitā sā tu pittadoṣabhramāpahā || 12.33
saptaparṇaḥ patravarṇaḥ śuktiparṇaḥ suparṇakaḥ |
saptacchado gucchapuṣpo'yugmaparṇo municchadaḥ || 12.34
bṛhattvagbahuparṇaśca tathā śālmalipatrakaḥ |
madagandho gandhiparṇo vijñeyo vahnibhūmitaḥ || 12.35
saptaparṇastu tiktoṣṇastridoṣaghnaśca dīpanaḥ |
madagandho nirunddhe'yaṃ vraṇaraktāmayakrimīn || 12.36
saralastu pūtikāṣṭhaṃ tumbī pītadrurutthito dīpataruḥ |
sa snigdhadārusaṃjñaḥ snigdho mārīcapatrako navadhā || 12.37
saralaḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇaḥ kaphavātavināśanaḥ |
tvagdoṣaśophakaṇḍūtivraṇaghnaḥ koṣṭhaśuddhidaḥ || 12.38
jñeyaṃ kuṅkumamagnisekharamasṛkkāśmīrajaṃ pītakaṃ kāśmīraṃ rudhiraṃ varaṃ ca piśunaṃ raktaṃ śaṭhaṃ śoṇitam |
vāhlīkaṃ ghusṛṇaṃ vareṇyamaruṇaṃ kāleyakaṃ jāguḍaṃ kāntaṃ vahniśikhaṃ ca kesaravaraṃ gauraṃ karākṣīritam || 12.39
kuṅkumaṃ surabhi tiktakaṭūṣṇaṃ kāsavātakaphakaṇṭharujāghnam |
mūrdhaśūlaviṣadoṣanāśanaṃ rocanaṃ ca tanukāntikārakam || 12.40
tṛṇakuṅkumaṃ tṛṇāsraṃ gandhitṛṇaṃ śoṇitaṃ ca tṛṇapuṣpam |
gandhādhikaṃ tṛṇotthaṃ tṛṇagauraṃ lohitaṃ ca navasaṃjñam || 12.41
tṛṇakuṅkumaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ kaphamārutaśophanut |
kaṇḍūtipāmākuṣṭhāmadoṣaghnaṃ bhāskaraṃ param || 12.42
priyaṅguḥ phalinī śyāmā priyavallī phalapriyā |
gaurī govandanī vṛttā kārambhā kaṅgu kaṅgunī || 12.43
bhaṅgurā gauravallī ca subhagā parṇabhedinī |
śubhā pītā ca maṅgalyā śreyasī cāṅkabhūmitā || 12.44
priyaṅguḥ śītalā tiktā dāhapittāsradoṣajit |
vāntibhrāntijvaraharā vaktrajāḍyavināśanī || 12.45
kastūrī mṛganābhistu madanī gandhacelikā |
vedhamukhyā ca mārjārī subhagā bahugandhadā || 12.46
sahasravedhī śyāmā syātkāmānandā mṛgāṇḍajā |
kuraṅganābhī lalitā mado mṛgamadastathā |
śyāmalī kāmamodī ca vijñeyāṣṭādaśāhvayā || 12.47
kastūrī surabhistiktā cakṣuṣyā mukharogajit |
kilāsakaphadaurgandhyavātālakṣmīmalāpahā || 12.48
kapilā piṅgalā kṛṣṇā kastūrī trividhā kramāt |
nepāle'pi ca kāśmīre kāmarūpe ca jāyate || 12.49
sāpyekā kharikā tataśca tilakā jñeyā kulitthāparā piṇḍānyāpi ca nāyiketi ca parā yā pañcabhedābhidhā |
sā śuddhā mṛganābhitaḥ kramavaśādeṣā kṣitīśocitā pakṣatyādidinatrayeṣu janitā kastūrikā stūyate || 12.50
cūrṇākṛtistu kharikā tilakā tilābhā kaulatthabījasadṛśī ca kulitthakā ca |
sthūlā tataḥ kiyadiyaṃ kila piṇḍikākhyā tasyāśca kiṃcidadhikā yadi nāyikā sā || 12.51
svāde tiktā piñjarā ketakīnāṃ gandhaṃ dhatte lāghavaṃ tolane ca |
yāpsu nyastā naiva vaivarṇyamīyātkastūrī sā rājabhogyāpraśastā || 12.52
yā gandhaṃ ketakīnāmapaharati madaṃ sindhurāṇāṃ ca varṇe svāde tiktā kaṭurvā laghuratha tulitā marditā cikkaṇā syāt |
dāhaṃ yā naiti vahnau śimiśimiti ciraṃ carmagandhā hutāśe sā kastūrī praśastā varamṛgatanujā rājate rājabhogyā || 12.53
bāle jarati ca hariṇe kṣīṇe rogiṇi ca mandagandhayutā |
kāmāture ca taruṇe kastūrī bahulaparimalā bhavati || 12.54
yā snigdhā dhūmagandhā vahati vinihitā pītatāṃ pāthaso'ntarniḥśeṣaṃ yā niviṣṭā bhavati hutavahe bhasmasādeva sadyaḥ |
yā ca nyastā tulāyāṃ kalayati gurutāṃ marditā rūkṣatāṃ ca jñeyā kastūrikeyaṃ khalu kṛtamatibhiḥ kṛtrimā naiva sevyā || 12.55
śuddho vā malino'stu vā mṛgamadaḥ kiṃ jātametāvatā ko'pyasyānavadhiścamatkṛtinidhiḥ saurabhyameko guṇaḥ |
yenāsau smaramaṇḍanaikavasatirbhāle kapole gale dormūle kucamaṇḍale ca kurute saṅgaṃ kuraṅgīdṛśām || 12.56
gorocanā ruciḥ śobhā rucirā śobhanā śubhā |
gaurī ca rocanā piṅgā maṅgalyā piṅgalā śivā || 12.57
pītā ca gautamī gavyā vandanīyā ca kāñcanī |
medhyā manoramā śyāmā rāmā bhūmikarāhvayā || 12.58
gorocanā ca śiśirā viṣadoṣahantrī rucyā ca pācanakarī krimikuṣṭhahantrī |
bhūtagrahopaśamanaṃ kurute ca pathyā śṛṅgāramaṅgalakarī janamohinī ca || 12.59
karpūro ghanasārakaḥ sitakaraḥ śītaḥ śaśāṅkaḥ śilā śītāṃśurhimavālukā himakaraḥ śītaprabhaḥ śāmbhavaḥ |
śubhrāṃśuḥ sphaṭikābhrasāramihikātārābhracandrendavaścandrālokatuṣāragaurakumudānyekādaśāhvā dviśaḥ || 12.60
potāso bhīmasenastadanu sitakaraḥ śaṃkarāvāsasaṃjñaḥ prāṃśuḥ piñjo'bdasārastadanu himayutā vālukā jūṭikā ca |
paścādasyāstuṣārastadupari sahimaḥ śītalaḥ pakvikānyā karpūrasyeti bhedā guṇarasamahasāṃ vaidyadṛśyena dṛśyāḥ || 12.61
karpūro nūtanastiktaḥ snigdhaścoṣṇo'sradāhadaḥ |
cirastho dāhadoṣaghnaḥ sa dhautaḥ śubhakṛtparaḥ || 12.62
śiro madhyaṃ talaṃ ceti karpūrastrividhaḥ smṛtaḥ |
śiraḥ stambhāgrasaṃjātaṃ madhyaṃ parṇatale talam || 12.63
bhāsvadviśadapulakaṃ śirojātaṃ tu madhyamam |
sāmānyapulakaṃ svacchaṃ tale cūrṇaṃ tu gaurakam || 12.64
stambhagarbhasthitaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ stambhabāhye ca madhyamam |
svacchamīṣatharidrābhaṃ śubhraṃ tanmadhyamaṃ smṛtaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ śubhrarūkṣaṃ ca pulakaṃ bāhyajaṃ vadet || 12.65
svacchaṃ bhṛṅgārapattraṃ laghutaraviśadaṃ tolane tiktakaṃ cetsvāde śaityaṃ suhṛdyaṃ bahalaparimalāmodasaurabhyadāyi |
niḥsnehaṃ dārḍhyapattraṃ śubhataramiti cetrājayogyaṃ praśastaṃ karpūraṃ cānyathā cedbahutaramaśane sphoṭadāyi vraṇāya || 12.66
cīnakaścīnakarpūraḥ kṛtrimo dhavalaḥ paṭuḥ |
meghasārastuṣāraśca dvīpakarpūrajaḥ smṛtaḥ || 12.67
cīnakaḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇa īṣacchītaḥ kaphāpahaḥ |
kaṇṭhadoṣaharī medhyaḥ pācanaḥ krimināśanaḥ || 12.68
javādi gandharājaṃ syātkṛtrimaṃ mṛgacarmajam |
samūhagandhaṃ gandhāḍhyaṃ snigdhaṃ sāmrāṇikardamam |
sugandhaṃ tailaniryāsaṃ kuṭāmodaṃ daśābhidham || 12.69
saugandhikaṃ javādi syātsnigdhaṃ coṣṇaṃ sukhāvaham |
vāte hitaṃ ca rājñāṃ ca mohanāhlādakāraṇam || 12.70
javādi nīlaṃ sasnigdhamīṣatpītaṃ sugandhadam |
ātape bahulāmodaṃ rājñāṃ yogyaṃ na cānyathā || 12.71
tūṇīkastūṇikastūṇī pītakaḥ kacchapastathā |
nandī kurakaḥ kānto nandīvṛkṣo navāhvayaḥ || 12.72
vṛkṣaḥ kaṭustiktaḥ śītastiktāsradāhajit |
śiro'rtiśvetakuṣṭhaghnaḥ sugandhiḥ puṣṭivīryadaḥ || 12.73
jātīpattrī jātikośaḥ sumanaḥpattrikāpi sā |
mālatīpattrikā pañcanāmnī saumanasāyinī || 12.74
jātīpatrī kaṭustiktā surabhiḥ kaphanāśanī |
vaktravaiśadyajananī jāḍyadoṣanikṛntanī || 12.75
jātīphalaṃ jātiśasyaṃ śālūkaṃ mālatīphalam |
majjāsāraṃ jātisāraṃ puṭaṃ ca sumanaḥphalam || 12.76
jātīphalaṃ kaṣāyoṣṇaṃ kaṭu kaṇṭhāmayārtijit |
vātātisāramehaghnaṃ laghu vṛṣyaṃ ca dīpanam || 12.77
kakkolakaṃ kṛtaphalaṃ kolakaṃ kaṭukaṃ phalam |
vidveṣyaṃ sthūlamaricaṃ karkolaṃ mādhavocitam |
kaṅkolaṃ kaṭphalaṃ proktaṃ mārīcaṃ rudrasaṃmitam || 12.78
kakkolaṃ kaṭu tiktoṣṇaṃ vaktrajāḍyaharaṃ param |
dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ rucyaṃ kaphavātanikṛntanam || 12.79
lavaṃgakalikā divyaṃ lavaṃgaṃ śekharaṃ lavam |
śrīpuṣpaṃ devakusumaṃ ruciraṃ vārisambhavam || 12.80
tīkṣṇapuṣpaṃ tu bhṛṅgāraṃ gīrvāṇakusumaṃ tathā |
puṣpakaṃ candanādi syātjñeyaṃ trayodaśāhvayam || 12.81
lavaṃgaṃ śītalaṃ tiktaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ bhaktarocanam |
vātapittakaphaghnaṃ ca tīkṣṇaṃ mūrdharujāpaham || 12.82
lavaṃgaṃ soṣṇakaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ vipāke madhuraṃ himam |
vātapittakaphāmaghnaṃ kṣayakāsāsradoṣanut || 12.83
svādustvagarusāraḥ syātsudhūmyo gandhadhūmajaḥ |
svāduḥ kaṭukaṣāyoṣṇaḥ sadhūmāmodavātajit || 12.84
kṛṣṇāgaru syādagaru śṛṅgāraṃ viśvarūpakam |
śīrṣaṃ kālāgaru keśyaṃ vasukaṃ kṛṣṇakāṣṭhakam |
dhūpārhaṃ vallaraṃ gandharājakaṃ dvādaśāhvayam || 12.85
kṛṣṇāgaru kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca tiktaṃ lepe ca śītalam |
pāne pittaharaṃ kiṃcittridoṣaghnamudāhṛtam || 12.86
anyāgaru pītakaṃ ca lohaṃ varṇaprasādanam |
anāryakamasāraṃ ca kṛmijagdhaṃ ca kāṣṭhakam || 12.87
kāṣṭhāgaru kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca lepe rūkṣaṃ kaphāpaham || 12.88
dāhāgaru dahanāgaru dāhakakāṣṭhaṃ ca vahnikāṣṭhaṃ ca |
dhūpāgaru tailāgaru puraṃ ca puramathanavallabhaṃ caiva || 12.89
dāhāgaru kaṭukoṣṇaṃ keśānāṃ vardhanaṃ ca varṇyaṃ ca |
apanayati keśadoṣānātanute saṃtataṃ ca saugandhyam || 12.90
maṅgalyā mallikā gandhamaṅgalāgaruvācakā |
maṅgalyā guruśiśirā gandhāḍhyā yogavāhikā || 12.91
māṃsī tu jaṭilā peśī kravyādī piśitā miśī |
keśinī ca jaṭā hiṃsrā jaṭāmāṃsī ca māṃsinī || 12.92
jaṭālā naladā meṣī tāmasī cakravartinī |
mātā bhūtajaṭā caiva jananī ca jaṭāvatī |
mṛgabhakṣāpi cetyetā ekaviṃśatidhābhidhāḥ || 12.93
surabhistu jaṭāmāṃsī kaṣāyā kaṭuśītalā |
kaphahṛdbhūtadāhaghnī pittaghnī modakāntikṛt || 12.94
dvitīyā gandhamāṃsī ca keśī bhūtajaṭā smṛtā |
piśācī pūtanā caiva bhūtakeśī ca lomaśā |
jaṭālā laghumāṃsī ca khyātā cāṅkamitāhvayā || 12.95
gandhamāṃsī tiktaśītā kaphakaṇṭhāmayāpahā |
raktapittaharā varṇyā viṣabhūtajvarāpahā || 12.96
ākāśamāṃsī sūkṣmānyā nirālambā khasambhavā |
sevālī sūkṣmapattrī ca gaurī parvatavāsinī || 12.97
abhramāṃsī himā śophavraṇanāḍīrujāpahā |
lūtāgardabhajālādihāriṇī varṇakāriṇī || 12.98
turuṣko yāvano dhūmro dhūmravarṇaḥ sugandhikaḥ |
sihlakaḥ sihlasāraśca pītasāraḥ kapistathā || 12.99
piṇyākaḥ kapijaḥ kalkaḥ piṇḍitaḥ piṇḍatailakaḥ |
karevaraḥ kṛtrimako lepano munibhūhvayaḥ || 12.100
turuṣkaḥ surabhistiktaḥ kaṭusnigdhaśca kuṣṭhajit |
kaphapittāśmarīmūtrāghātabhūtajvarārtijit || 12.101
gugguluryavanadviṣṭo bhavābhīṣṭo niśāṭakaḥ |
jaṭālaḥ kālaniryāsaḥ pūro bhūtaharaḥ śivaḥ || 12.102
kauśikaḥ śāmbhavo durgo yātughno mahiṣākṣakaḥ |
deveṣṭo marudeśyo'pi rakṣohā rūkṣagandhakaḥ |
divyastu mahiṣākṣaśca nāmānyetāni viṃśatiḥ || 12.103
gugguluḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇaḥ kaphamārutakāsajit |
krimivātodaraplīhaśophārśoghno rasāyanaḥ || 12.104
gandharājaḥ svarṇakaṇaḥ suvarṇaḥ kaṇagugguluḥ |
kanako vaṃśapotaśca surasaśca palaṃkaṣaḥ || 12.105
kaṇagugguluḥ kaṭūṣṇaḥ surabhirvātanāśanaḥ |
śūlagulmodarādhmānakaphaghnaśca rasāyanaḥ || 12.106
gugguluśca tṛtīyo'nyo bhūmijo daityamedajaḥ |
durgāhlāda iḍājāta āśādiripusambhavaḥ |
majjājo madajaścaiva mahiṣāsurasambhavaḥ || 12.107
guggulurbhūmijastiktaḥ kaṭūṣṇaḥ kaphavātajit |
umāpriyaśca bhūtaghno medhyaḥ saurabhyadaḥ sadā || 12.108
rālaḥ sarjarasaścaiva śālaḥ kanakalodbhavaḥ |
lalanaḥ śālaniryāso deveṣṭaḥ śītalastathā || 12.109
bahurūpaḥ śālarasaḥ sarjaniryāsakastathā |
surabhiḥ suradhūpaśca yakṣadhūpo'gnivallabhaḥ |
kālaḥ kalalajaḥ prokto nāmnā saptadaśāṅkitaḥ || 12.110
rālastu śiśiraḥ snigdhaḥ kaṣāyastiktasaṃgrahaḥ |
vātapittaharaḥ sphoṭakaṇḍūtivraṇanāśanaḥ |
kundurukaḥ saurāṣṭraḥ śikharī kundurukakundukastīkṣṇaḥ |
gopurako bahugandhaḥ pālindo bhīṣaṇaśca daśasaṃjñaḥ || 12.111
kundururmadhurastiktaḥ kaphapittārtidāhanut |
pāne lepe ca śiśiraḥ pradarāmayaśāntikṛt || 12.112
kuṣṭhaṃ rujāgado vyādhirāmayaṃ pāribhadrakam |
rāmaṃ vānīrajaṃ vāpyaṃ jñeyaṃ tvagdoṣamutpalam |
kutsaṃ ca pāṭavaṃ caiva padmakaṃ manusaṃjñakam || 12.113
kuṣṭhaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ tiktaṃ syātkaphamārutakuṣṭhajit |
visarpaviṣakaṇḍūtikharjūdadrughnakāntikṛt || 12.114
sārivā śāradā gopā gopavallī pratānikā |
gopakanyā latāsphotā navāhvā kāṣṭhasārivā || 12.115
sārivānyā kṛṣṇamūlī kṛṣṇā candanasārivā |
bhadrā candanagopā tu candanā kṛṣṇavallyapi || 12.116
sārive dve tu madhure kaphavātāsranāśane |
kuṣṭhakaṇḍūjvarahare mehadurgandhināśane || 12.117
nakhaḥ kararuhaḥ śilpī śuktiḥ śaṅkhaḥ khuraḥ śaphaḥ |
valaḥ kośī ca karajo hanurnāgahanustathā || 12.118
pāṇijo badarīpattro dhūpyaḥ paṇyavilāsinī |
saṃdhinālaḥ pāṇiruhaḥ syādaṣṭādaśasaṃjñakaḥ || 12.119
nakhaḥ syāduṣṇakaṭuko viṣaṃ hanti prayojitaḥ |
kuṣṭhakaṇḍūvraṇaghnaśca bhūtavidrāvaṇaḥ paraḥ || 12.120
nakho'nyaḥ syādbalanakhaḥ kūṭasthaścakranāyakaḥ |
cakrī cakranakhastryasraḥ kālo vyāghranakhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 12.121
dvīpinakho vyālanakhaḥ khapuṭo vyālapāṇijaḥ |
vyālāyudho vyālabalo vyālakhaḍgaśca ṣoḍaśa || 12.122
vyālanakhastu tiktoṣṇaḥ kaṣāyaḥ kaphavātajit |
kuṣṭhakaṇḍūvraṇaghnaśca varṇyaḥ saugandhyadaḥ paraḥ || 12.123
spṛkkā ca devī piśunā vadhūśca koṭirmanurbrāhmaṇikā sugandhā |
samudrakāntā kuṭilā tathā ca mālālikā bhūtalikā ca laghvī || 12.124
nirmālyā sukumārā ca mālālī devaputrikā |
pañcaguptirasṛkproktā nakhapuṣpī ca viṃśatiḥ || 12.125
spṛkkā kaṭukaṣāyā ca tiktā śleṣmārtikāsajit |
śleṣmamehāśmarīkṛcchranāśanī ca sugandhadā || 12.126
sthauṇeyakaṃ barhiśikhaṃ śukacchadaṃ mayūracūḍaṃ śukapucchakaṃ tathā |
vikīrṇaromāpi ca kīravarṇakaṃ vikarṇasaṃjñaṃ haritaṃ navāhvayam || 12.127
sthauṇeyaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ sugandhi kaṭutiktakam |
pittaprakopaśamanaṃ balapuṣṭivivardhanam || 12.128
murā gandhavatī daityā gandhāḍhyā gandhamādanī |
surabhirbhūrigandhā ca kuṭī gandhakuṭī tathā || 12.129
murā tiktā kaṭuḥ śītā kaṣāyā kaphapittahṛt |
śvāsāsṛgviṣadāhārtibhramamūrchātṛṣāpahā || 12.130
śaileyaṃ śilajaṃ vṛddhaṃ śilāpuṣpaṃ śilodbhavam |
sthaviraṃ palitaṃ jīrṇaṃ tathā kālānusāryakam || 12.131
śilotthaṃ ca śilādadruḥ śailajaṃ giripuṣpakam |
śilāprasūnaṃ subhagaṃ śailakaṃ ṣoḍaśāhvayam || 12.132
śaileyaṃ śiśiraṃ tiktaṃ sugandhi kaphapittajit |
dāhatṛṣṇāvamiśvāsavraṇadoṣavināśanam || 12.133
corakaḥ śaṅkitaścaṇḍā duṣpattraḥ kṣemako ripuḥ |
capalaḥ kitavo dhūrtaḥ paṭurnīco niśācaraḥ || 12.134
gaṇahāsaḥ kopanakaścaurakaḥ phalacorakaḥ |
duṣkulo granthilaścaiva sugranthiḥ parṇacorakaḥ |
granthiparṇo granthidalo granthipattrastrinetradhā || 12.135
corakastīvragandhoṣṇastikto vātakaphāpahaḥ |
nāsāmukharujājīrṇakrimidoṣavināśanaḥ || 12.136
padmakaṃ pītakaṃ pītaṃ mālayaṃ śītalaṃ himam |
śubhraṃ kedārajaṃ raktaṃ pāṭalāpuṣpasaṃnibham |
padmakāṣṭhaṃ padmavṛkṣaṃ proktaṃ syāddvādaśāhvayam || 12.137
padmakaṃ śītalaṃ tiktaṃ raktapittavināśanam |
mohadāhajvarabhrāntikuṣṭhavisphoṭaśāntikṛt || 12.138
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ puṇḍaryaṃ puṇḍarīyakam |
pauṇḍaryaṃ ca supuṣpaṃ ca sānujaṃ cānujaṃ smṛtam || 12.139
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ madhuraṃ tiktaśītalam |
pittaraktavraṇān hanti jvaradāhatṛṣāpaham || 12.140
lāmajjakaṃ sunālaṃ syādamṛṇālaṃ lavaṃ laghu |
iṣṭakāpathakaṃ śīghraṃ dīrghamūlaṃ jalāśrayam || 12.141
lāmajjakaṃ himaṃ tiktaṃ madhuraṃ vātapittajit |
tṛḍdāhaśramamūrchārtiraktapittajvarāpaham || 12.142
māṃsarohiṇyatiruhā vṛttā carmakaṣā ca sā |
vikasā māṃsarohī ca jñeyā māṃsaruhā muniḥ || 12.143
anyā māṃsī sadāmāṃsī māṃsarohā rasāyanī |
sulomā lomakaraṇī rohiṇī māṃsarohikā || 12.144
vikasā kaṭukā tiktā tathoṣṇā svarasādanut |
rasāyanaprayogācca sarvarogaharā matā |
kaṣāyā grāhiṇī varṇyā raktapittaprasādanī || 12.145
rohiṇīyugalaṃ śītaṃ kaṣāyaṃ krimināśanam |
kaṇṭhaśuddhikaraṃ rucyaṃ vātadoṣaniṣūdanam || 12.146
śrīveṣṭo vṛkṣadhūpaśca cīḍāgandho rasāṅgakaḥ |
śrīvāsaḥ śrīraso veṣṭo lakṣmīveṣṭastu veṣṭakaḥ || 12.147
veṣṭasāro rasāveṣṭaḥ kṣīraśīrṣaḥ sudhūpakaḥ |
dhūpāṅgastilaparṇaśca saralāṅgo'pi ṣoḍaśa || 12.148
śrīveṣṭaḥ kaṭutiktaśca kaṣāyaḥ śleṣmapittajit |
yonidoṣarujājīrṇavraṇaghnādhmānadoṣajit || 12.149
uśīramamṛṇālaṃ syājjalavāsaṃ haripriyam |
mṛṇālamabhayaṃ vīraṃ vīraṇaṃ samagandhikam || 12.150
raṇapriyaṃ vāritaraṃ śiśiraṃ śitimūlakam |
veṇīgamūlakaṃ caiva jalāmodaṃ sugandhikam |
sugandhimūlakaṃ śubhraṃ bālakaṃ grahabhūhvayam || 12.151
uśīraṃ śītalaṃ tiktaṃ dāhaśramaharaṃ param |
pittajvarārtiśamanaṃ jalasaugandhyadāyakam || 12.152
nalikā vidrumalatikā kapotabāṇā nalī ca nirmathyā |
suṣirā dhamanī stutyā raktadalā nartakī naṭī rudrāḥ || 12.153
nalikā tiktakaṭukā tīkṣṇā ca madhurā himā |
kṛmivātodarārtyarśaḥśūlaghnī malaśodhanī || 12.154
itthaṃ gandhadravyakadambāhvayavīryavyākhyāvācoyuktiviviktojjvalasargam |
vargaṃ vaktrāmbhoruhamodārhamadhīyāthainaṃ madhye saṃsadasau dīvyatu vaidyaḥ || 12.155
ye gandhayanti sakalāni ca bhūtalāni lokāṃśca ye'pi sukhayanti ca gandhalubdhān |
teṣāmayaṃ malayajādisugandhināmnāṃ bhūrgandhavarga iti viśrutimeti vargaḥ || 12.156
yasyoccaiścaritāni śītasurabhīṇyabhyasya satyātmano duścāritrajanā niṣaṅgajanitaṃ drāgdauḥsthamāsthan svakaṃ tasyāyaṃ kṛtinaḥ kṛtau narahareḥ śrīcandanādiḥ sthitiṃ vargo vāñchati nāmanaigamaśikhābhūṣāmaṇau dvādaśaḥ || 12.157


rājanighaṇṭu, ṣuvarṇādivarga
trisvarṇaraupyatāmrāṇi trapu sīsaṃ dvirītikā |
kāṃsyāyo vartakaṃ kāntaṃ kiṭṭaṃ muṇḍaṃ ca tīkṣṇakam || 13.1
śilā sindūrabhūnāgaṃ hiṅgulaṃ gairikaṃ dvidhā |
tuvarī haritālaṃ ca gandhakaṃ ca śilājatu || 13.2
sikthakaṃ ca dvikāsīsaṃ mākṣikau pañcadhāñjanam |
kampillatuttharasakaṃ pāradaścābhrakaṃ catuḥ || 13.3
sphaṭī ca kṣullakaḥ śaṅkhau kapardaḥ śuktikā dvidhā |
khaṭinī dugdhapāṣāṇo maṇiśca karpūrādyakaḥ || 13.4
sikatā ca dvikaṅguṣṭhaṃ vimalā ca dvidhā matā |
tathākhuprastaraścaiva śaravedamitāhvayāḥ |
atha ratnaṃ navaṃ vakṣye padmarāgādikaṃ kramāt || 13.5
māṇikyamuktāphalavidrumāṇi gārutmataṃ syādatha puṣparāgaḥ |
vajraṃ ca nīlaṃ ca nava krameṇa gomedavaiḍūryayutāni tāni || 13.6
sphaṭikaśca sūryakānto vaikrāntaścandrakāntakaḥ |
rājāvartaḥ perojaṃ syādubhau bāṇāśca saṃkhyayā || 13.7
svarṇaṃ suvarṇakanakojjvalakāñcanāni kalyāṇahāṭakahiraṇyamanoharāṇi |
gāṅgeyagairikamahārajatāgnivīryarukmāgnihematapanīyakabhāskarāṇi || 13.8
jāmbūnadāṣṭāpadajātarūpapiñjānacāmīkarakarburāṇi |
kārtasvarāpiñjarabharmabhūritejāṃsi dīptānalapītakāni || 13.9
maṅgalyasaumeravaśātakumbhaśṛṅgāracandrājarajāmbavāni |
āgneyaniṣkāgniśikhāni ceti netrābdhinirdhāritanāma hema || 13.10
svarṇaṃ snigdhakaṣāyatiktamadhuraṃ doṣatrayadhvaṃsanaṃ śītaṃ svādu rasāyanaṃ ca rucikṛccakṣuṣyamāyuṣpradam |
prajñāvīryabalasmṛtisvarakaraṃ kāntiṃ vidhatte tanoḥ saṃdhatte duritakṣayaṃ śriyamidaṃ dhatte nṝṇāṃ dhāraṇāt || 13.11
dāhe ca raktamatha yacca sitaṃ chidāyāṃ kāśmīrakānti ca vibhāti nikāṣapaṭṭe |
snigdhaṃ ca gauravamupaiti ca yattulāyāṃ jātyā tadeva kanakaṃ mṛdu raktapītam || 13.12
taccaikaṃ rasavedhajaṃ tadaparaṃ jātaṃ svayaṃ bhūmijaṃ kiṃcānyadbahulohasaṃkarabhavaṃ ceti tridhā kāñcanam |
tatrādyaṃ kila pītaraktamaparaṃ raktaṃ tato'nyattathā mairālaṃ tadatikrameṇa tadidaṃ syātpūrvapūrvottamam || 13.13
raupyaṃ śubhraṃ vasuśreṣṭhaṃ ruciraṃ candralohakam |
śvetakaṃ tu mahāśubhraṃ rajataṃ taptarūpakam || 13.14
candrabhūtiḥ sitaṃ tāraṃ kaladhautendulohakam |
kupyaṃ dhautaṃ tathā saudhaṃ candrahāsaṃ munīndukam || 13.15
raupyaṃ snigdhaṃ kaṣāyāmlaṃ vipāke madhuraṃ saram |
vātapittaharaṃ rucyaṃ valīpalitanāśanam || 13.16
dāhacchedanikāṣeṣu sitaṃ snigdhaṃ ca yadguru |
sugharṣe'pi ca varṇāḍhyamuttamaṃ tadudīritam || 13.17
tāmraṃ mlecchamukhaṃ śulvaṃ tapaneṣṭamudumbaram |
tryambakaṃ cāravindaṃ ca ravilohaṃ ravipriyam |
raktaṃ nepālakaṃ caiva raktadhātuḥ karendudhā || 13.18
tāmraṃ supakvaṃ madhuraṃ kaṣāyaṃ tiktaṃ vipāke kaṭu śītalaṃ ca |
kaphāpahaṃ pittaharaṃ vibandhaśūlaghnapāṇḍūdaragulmanāśi || 13.19
ghanaghātasahaṃ snigdhaṃ raktapattrāmalaṃ mṛdu |
śuddhākarasamutpannaṃ tāmraṃ śubhamasaṃkaram || 13.20
trapu trapusamāṇḍūkaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca madhuraṃ himam |
kurūpyaṃ piccaṭaṃ raṅgaṃ pūtigandhaṃ daśāhvayam || 13.21
trapusaṃ kaṭutiktahimaṃ kaṣāyalavaṇaṃ saraṃ ca mehaghnam |
krimidāhapāṇḍuśamanaṃ kāntikaraṃ tadrasāyanaṃ caiva || 13.22
śvetaṃ laghu mṛdu svacchaṃ snigdhamuṣṇāpahaṃ himam |
sūtapattrakaraṃ kāntaṃ trapu śreṣṭhamudāhṛtam || 13.23
sīsakaṃ tu jaḍaṃ sīsaṃ yavaneṣṭaṃ bhujaṃgamam |
yogīṣṭaṃ nāgapuragaṃ kuvaṅgaṃ paripiṣṭakam || 13.24
mṛdu kṛṣṇāyasaṃ padmaṃ tāraśuddhikaraṃ smṛtam |
sirāvṛttaṃ ca vaṅgaṃ syāccīnapiṣṭaṃ ca ṣoḍaśa || 13.25
sīsaṃ tu vaṅgatulyaṃ syātrasavīryavipākataḥ |
uṣṇaṃ ca kaphavātaghnamarśoghnaṃ guru lekhanam || 13.26
svarṇe nīlaṃ mṛdu snigdhaṃ nirmalaṃ ca sugauratvam |
raupyasaṃśodhanaṃ kṣipraṃ sīsakaṃ ca taduttamam || 13.27
rītiḥ kṣudrasuvarṇaṃ siṃhalakaṃ piṅgalaṃ ca pittalakam |
lauhitakamārakuṭṭaṃ piṅgalalohaṃ ca pītakaṃ navadhā || 13.28
rājarītiḥ kākatuṇḍī rājaputrī maheśvarī |
brāhmaṇī brahmarītiśca kapilā piṅgalāpi ca || 13.29
rītikāyugalaṃ tiktaṃ śītalaṃ lavaṇaṃ rase |
śodhanaṃ pāṇḍuvātaghnaṃ krimiplīhārtipittajit || 13.30
śuddhā snigdhā mṛduḥ śītā suraṅgā sūtrapattriṇī |
hemopamā śubhā svacchā janyā rītiḥ prakīrtitā || 13.31
kāṃsyaṃ saurāṣṭrikaṃ ghoṣaṃ kaṃsīyaṃ vahnilohakam |
dīptaṃ lohaṃ ghorapuṣpaṃ dīptakaṃ sumanāhvayam || 13.32
kāṃsyaṃ tu tiktamuṣṇaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ vātakaphavikāraghnam |
rūkṣaṃ kaṣāyarucyaṃ laghu dīpanapācanaṃ pathyam || 13.33
śvetaṃ dīptaṃ mṛdu jyotiḥ śabdāḍhyaṃ snigdhanirmalam |
ghanāgnisahasūtrāṅgaṃ kāṃsyamuttamamīritam || 13.34
vartalohaṃ vartatīkṣṇaṃ vartakaṃ lohasaṃkaram |
nīlikā nīlalohaṃ ca lohajaṃ vaṭṭalohakam || 13.35
idaṃ lohaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca tiktaṃ ca śiśiraṃ tathā |
kaphahṛtpittaśamanaṃ madhuraṃ dāhamehanut || 13.36
ayaskāntaṃ kāntalohaṃ kāntaṃ syāllohakāntikam |
kāntāyasaṃ kṛṣṇalohaṃ mahālohaṃ ca saptadhā || 13.37
syādbhrāmakaṃ tadanu cumbakaromakākhyaṃ syācchedakākhyamiti tacca caturvidhaṃ syāt |
kāntāśmalohaguṇavṛddhi yathākrameṇa dārḍhyāṅgakāntikacakārṣṇyavirogadāyi || 13.38
ayaskāntaviśeṣāḥ syurbhrāmakāścumbakādayaḥ |
rasāyanakarāḥ sarve dehasiddhikarāḥ parāḥ || 13.39
na sūtena vinā kāntaṃ na kāntena vinā rasaḥ |
sūtakāntasamāyogādrasāyanamudīritam || 13.40
lohakiṭṭaṃ tu kiṭṭaṃ syāllohacūrṇamayomalam |
lohajaṃ kṛṣṇacūrṇaṃ ca kārṣṇyaṃ lohamalaṃ tathā || 13.41
lohakiṭṭaṃ tu madhuraṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ krimivātanut |
paktiśūlaṃ marucchūlaṃ mehagulmārtiśophanut || 13.42
muṇḍaṃ muṇḍāyasaṃ lohaṃ dṛṣatsāraṃ śilātmajam |
aśmajaṃ kṛṣilohaṃ ca āraṃ kṛṣṇāyasaṃ nava || 13.43
tīkṣṇaṃ śastrāyasaṃ śastraṃ piṇḍaṃ piṇḍāyasaṃ śaṭham |
āyasaṃ niśitaṃ tīvraṃ lohakhaḍgaṃ ca muṇḍajam |
ayaścitrāyasaṃ proktaṃ cīnajaṃ vedabhūmitam || 13.44
lohaṃ rūkṣoṣṇatiktaṃ syādvātapittakaphāpaham |
pramehapāṇḍuraśūlaghnaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ muṇḍādhikaṃ smṛtam || 13.45
svarṇaṃ samyagaśodhitaṃ śramakaraṃ svedāvahaṃ duḥsahaṃ raupyaṃ jāṭharajāḍyamāndyajananaṃ tāmraṃ vamibhrāntidam |
nāgaṃ ca trapu cāṅgadoṣadamayo gulmādidoṣapradaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ śūlakaraṃ ca kāntamuditaṃ kārṣṇyāmayasphoṭadam || 13.46
viśuddhihīnau yadi muṇḍatīkṣṇau kṣudhāpahau gauravagulmadāyakau |
kāṃsyāyasaṃ kledakatāpakārakaṃ rītyau ca sammohanaśoṣadāyike || 13.47
manaḥśilā syātkunaṭī manojñā śilā manohvāpi ca nāgajihvā |
nepālikā syānmanasaśca guptā kalyāṇikā rogaśilā daśāhvā || 13.48
manaḥśilā kaṭuḥ snigdhā lekhanī viṣanāśanī |
bhūtāveśabhayonmādahāriṇī vaśyakāriṇī || 13.49
sindūraṃ nāgareṇuḥ syādraktaṃ sīmantakaṃ tathā |
nāgajaṃ nāgagarbhaṃ ca śoṇaṃ vīrarajaḥ smṛtam || 13.50
gaṇeśabhūṣaṇaṃ saṃdhyārāgaṃ śṛṅgārakaṃ smṛtam |
saubhāgyamaruṇaṃ caiva maṅgalyaṃ manusaṃmitam || 13.51
sindūraṃ kaṭukaṃ tiktamuṣṇaṃ vraṇaviropaṇam |
kuṣṭhāsraviṣakaṇḍūtivīsarpaśamanaṃ param || 13.52
suraṅgo'gnisahaḥ sūkṣmaḥ snigdhaḥ svaccho gururmṛduḥ |
suvarṇakarajaḥ śuddhaḥ sindūro maṅgalapradaḥ || 13.53
bhūnāgaḥ kṣitināgaśca bhūjantū raktajantukaḥ |
kṣitijaḥ kṣitijantuśca bhūmijo raktatuṇḍakaḥ || 13.54
bhūnāgo vajramāraḥ syānnānāvijñānakārakaḥ |
rasasya jāraṇe tūktaṃ tatsattvaṃ tu rasāyanam || 13.55
hiṅgulaṃ barbaraṃ raktaṃ suraṅgaṃ sugaraṃ smṛtam |
rañjanaṃ daradaṃ mlecchaṃ citrāṅgaṃ cūrṇapāradam || 13.56
anyacca mārakaṃ caiva maṇirāgaṃ rasodbhavam |
rañjakaṃ rasagarbhaṃ ca bāṇabhūsaṃkhyasaṃmitam || 13.57
hiṅgulaṃ madhuraṃ tiktamuṣṇavātakaphāpaham |
tridoṣadvaṃdvadoṣotthaṃ jvaraṃ harati sevitam || 13.58
gairikaṃ raktadhātuḥ syādgiridhāturgavedhukam |
dhātuḥ suraṅgadhātuśca girijaṃ girimṛdbhavam || 13.59
suvarṇagairikaṃ cānyatsvarṇadhātuḥ suraktakam |
saṃdhyābhraṃ babhrudhātuśca śilādhātuḥ ṣaḍāhvayam || 13.60
gairikaṃ madhuraṃ śītaṃ kaṣāyaṃ vraṇaropaṇam |
visphoṭārśo'gnidāhaghnaṃ varaṃ svarṇādikaṃ śubham || 13.61
tuvarī mṛcca saurāṣṭrī mṛtsnā saṅgā surāṣṭrajā |
bhūghnī mṛtālakaṃ kāsī mṛttikā suramṛttikā |
stutyā kāṅkṣī sujātā ca jñeyā caiva caturdaśa || 13.62
tuvarī tiktakaṭukā kaṣāyāmlā ca lekhanī |
cakṣuṣyā grahaṇīchardipittasaṃtāpahāriṇī || 13.63
haritālaṃ godantaṃ pītaṃ naṭamaṇḍanaṃ ca gauraṃ ca |
citrāṅgaṃ piñjarakaṃ bhavedālaṃ tālakaṃ ca tālaṃ ca || 13.64
kanakarasaṃ kāñcanakaṃ biḍālakaṃ caiva citragandhaṃ ca |
piṅgaṃ ca piṅgasāraṃ gaurīlalitaṃ ca saptadaśasaṃjñam || 13.65
haritālaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca snigdhaṃ tvagdoṣanāśanam |
bhūtabhrāntipraśamanaṃ viṣavātarujārtijit || 13.66
gandhako gandhapāṣāṇo gandhāśmā gandhamodanaḥ |
pūtigandho'tigandhaśca vaṭaḥ saugandhikastathā || 13.67
sugandho divyagandhaśca gandhaśca rasagandhakaḥ |
kuṣṭhāriḥ krūragandhaśca kīṭaghnaḥ śarabhūmitaḥ || 13.68
gandhakaḥ kaṭuruṣṇaśca tīvragandho'tivahnikṛt |
viṣaghnaḥ kuṣṭhakaṇḍūtikharjūtvagdoṣanāśanaḥ || 13.69
śveto raktaśca pītaśca nīlaśceti caturvidhaḥ |
gandhako varṇato jñeyo bhinno bhinnaguṇāśrayaḥ || 13.70
śvetaḥ kuṣṭhāpahārī syādrakto lohaprayogakṛt |
pīto rasaprayogārho nīlo varṇāntarocitaḥ || 13.71
śilājatu syādaśmotthaṃ śailaṃ girijamaśmajam |
aśmalākṣāśmajatukaṃ jatvaśmakamiti smṛtam || 13.72
śilājatu bhavettiktaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca rasāyanam |
mehonmādāśmarīśophakuṣṭhāpasmāranāśanam || 13.73
sikthakaṃ madhukaṃ sikthaṃ madhujaṃ madhusambhavam |
madanakaṃ madhūcchiṣṭaṃ madanaṃ makṣikāmalam || 13.74
kṣaudreyaṃ pītarāgaṃ ca snigdhaṃ mākṣikajaṃ tathā |
kṣaudrajaṃ madhuśeṣaṃ ca drāvakaṃ makṣikāśrayam |
madhūṣitaṃ ca samproktaṃ madhūtthaṃ conaviṃśati || 13.75
sikthakaṃ kapilaṃ svādu kuṣṭhavātārtijinmṛdu |
kaṭu snigdhaṃ ca lepena sphuṭitāṅgaviropaṇam || 13.76
kāsīsaṃ dhātukāsīsaṃ kesaraṃ haṃsalomaśam |
śodhanaṃ pāṃśukāsīsaṃ śubhraṃ saptāhvayaṃ matam || 13.77
kāsīsaṃ tu kaṣāyaṃ syātśiśiraṃ viṣakuṣṭhajit |
kharjūkrimiharaṃ caiva cakṣuṣyaṃ kāntivardhanam || 13.78
dvitīyaṃ puṣpakāsīsaṃ vatsakaṃ ca malīmasam |
hrasvaṃ netrauṣadhaṃ yojyaṃ viśadaṃ nīlamṛttikā || 13.79
puṣpakāsīsaṃ tiktaṃ śītaṃ netrāmayāpaham |
lepenātyāmakuṣṭhādinānātvagdoṣanāśanam || 13.80
mākṣikaṃ caiva mākṣīkaṃ pītakaṃ dhātumākṣikam |
tāpījaṃ tāpyakaṃ tāpyamāpītaṃ pītamākṣikam || 13.81
āvartaṃ madhudhātuḥ syātkṣaudradhātustathāparaḥ |
proktaṃ mākṣikadhātuśca vedabhūrhemamākṣikam || 13.82
mākṣikaṃ madhuraṃ tiktamamlaṃ kaṭu kaphāpaham |
bhramahṛllāsamūrchārtiśvāsakāsaviṣāpaham || 13.83
mākṣikaṃ dvividhaṃ proktaṃ hemāhvaṃ tāramākṣikam |
bhinnavarṇaviśeṣatvātrasavīryādikaṃ pṛthak || 13.84
tāravādādike tāramākṣikaṃ ca praśasyate |
dehe hemādikaṃ śastaṃ rogahṛdbalapuṣṭidam || 13.85
añjanaṃ yāmunaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ nādeyaṃ mecakaṃ tathā |
srotojaṃ dṛkpradaṃ nīlaṃ sauvīraṃ ca suvīrajam || 13.86
tathā nīlāñjanaṃ caiva cakṣuṣyaṃ vārisambhavam |
kapotakaṃ ca kāpotaṃ samproktaṃ śarabhūmitam || 13.87
śītaṃ nīlāñjanaṃ proktaṃ kaṭu tiktaṃ kaṣāyakam |
cakṣuṣyaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ viṣaghnaṃ ca rasāyanam || 13.88
kulatthā dṛkprasādā ca cakṣuṣyātha kulatthikā |
kulālī locanahitā kumbhakārī malāpahā || 13.89
kulatthikā tu cakṣuṣyā kaṣāyā kaṭukā himā |
viṣavisphoṭakaṇḍūtivraṇadoṣanibarhiṇī || 13.90
puṣpāñjanaṃ puṣpaketuḥ kausumbhaṃ kusumāñjanam |
rītikaṃ rītikusumaṃ rītipuṣpaṃ ca pauṣpakam || 13.91
puṣpāñjanaṃ himaṃ proktaṃ pittahikkāpradāhanut |
nāśayedviṣakāsārtiṃ sarvanetrāmayāpaham || 13.92
rasāñjanaṃ rasodbhūtaṃ rasagarbhaṃ rasāgrajam |
kṛtakaṃ bālabhaiṣajyaṃ dārvīkvāthodbhavaṃ tathā || 13.93
rasajātaṃ tārkṣyaśailaṃ jñeyaṃ varyāñjanaṃ tathā |
rasanābhaṃ cāgnisāraṃ dvādaśāhvaṃ ca kīrtitam || 13.94
rītyāṃ tu dhmāyamānāyāṃ tatkiṭṭaṃ tu rasāñjanam |
tadabhāve tu kartavyaṃ dārvīkvāthasamudbhavam || 13.95
srotoñjanaṃ vāribhavaṃ tathānyaṃ srotodbhavaṃ srotanadībhavaṃ ca |
sauvīrasāraṃ ca kapotasāraṃ valmīkaśīrṣaṃ munisaṃmitāhvam || 13.96
srotoñjanaṃ śītakaṭu kaṣāyaṃ krimināśanam |
rasāñjanaṃ rase yogyaṃ stanyavṛddhikaraṃ param || 13.97
valmīkaśikharākāraṃ bhinnanīlāñjanaprabham |
ghṛṣṭe ca gairikāvarṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ srotoñjanaṃ ca tat || 13.98
kampillako'tha raktāṅgo recano recakastathā |
rañjako lohitāṅgaśca kampillo raktacūrṇakaḥ || 13.99
kampillako virecī syātkaṭūṣṇo vraṇanāśanaḥ |
kaphakāsārtihārī ca jantukrimiharo laghuḥ || 13.100
tutthaṃ nīlāśmajaṃ nīlaṃ haritāśmaṃ ca tutthakam |
mayūragrīvakaṃ caiva tāmragarbhāmṛtodbhavam |
mayūratutthaṃ samproktaṃ śikhikaṇṭhaṃ daśāhvayam || 13.101
tutthaṃ kaṭu kaṣāyoṣṇaṃ śvitranetrāmayāpaham |
viṣadoṣeṣu sarveṣu praśastaṃ vāntikārakam || 13.102
dvitīyaṃ kharparītutthaṃ kharparī rasakaṃ tathā |
cakṣuṣyamamṛtotpannaṃ tutthakharparikā tu ṣaṭ || 13.103
kharparī kaṭukā tiktā cakṣuṣyā ca rasāyanī |
tvagdoṣaśamanī rucyā dīpyā puṣṭivivardhanī || 13.104
pārado rasarājaśca rasanātho mahārasaḥ |
rasaścaiva mahatejā rasaloho rasottamaḥ || 13.105
sūtarāṭcapalo jaitraḥ śivabījaṃ śivastathā |
amṛtaṃ ca rasendraḥ syāllokeśo dhūrtaraḥ prabhuḥ || 13.106
rudrajo haratejaśca rasadhāturacintyajaḥ |
khecaraścāmaraḥ prokto dehado mṛtyunāśanaḥ || 13.107
skandaḥ skandāṃśakaḥ sūto devo divyarasastathā |
prokto rasāyanaśreṣṭho yaśodastritridhāhvayaḥ || 13.108
pāradaḥ sakalaroganāśanaḥ ṣaḍraso nikhilayogavāhakaḥ |
pañcabhūtamaya eṣa kīrtito dehalohaparasiddhidāyakaḥ || 13.109
mūrchito harate vyādhīn baddhaḥ khecarasiddhidaḥ |
sarvasiddhikaro nīlo niruddho dehasiddhidaḥ || 13.110
vividhavyādhibhayodayamaraṇajarāsaṃkaṭe'pi martyānām |
pāraṃ dadāti yasmāttasmādayameva pāradaḥ kathitaḥ || 13.111
abhrakamabhraṃ bhṛṅgaṃ vyomāmbaramantarikṣamākāśam |
bahupattraṃ khamanantaṃ gaurījaṃ gaurijeyamiti ravayaḥ || 13.112
śvetaṃ pītaṃ lohitaṃ nīlamabhraṃ cāturvidhyaṃ yāti bhinnakriyārham |
śvetaṃ tāre kāñcane pītarakte nīlaṃ vyādhāvagryamagryaṃ guṇāḍhyam || 13.113
nīlābhraṃ darduro nāgaḥ pināko vajra ityapi |
caturvidhaṃ bhavettasya parīkṣā kathyate kramāt || 13.114
yadvahnau nihitaṃ tanoti nitarāṃ bhekāravaṃ darduro nāgaḥ phūtkurute dhanuḥsvanamupādatte pinākaḥ kila |
vajraṃ naiva vikārameti tadimānyāsevamānaḥ kramātgulmī ca vraṇavāṃśca kutsitagadī nīrukca saṃjāyate || 13.115
manojabhāvabhāvitau yadā śivau parasparam |
tadā kilābhrapāradau guhodbhavau babhūvatuḥ || 13.116
sphaṭī ca sphaṭikī proktā śvetā śubhrā ca raṅgadā |
raṅgadṛḍhā dṛḍharaṅgā raṅgāṅgā vasusaṃmitā || 13.117
sphaṭī ca kaṭukā snigdhā kaṣāyā pradarāpahā |
mehakṛcchravamīśoṣadoṣaghnī dṛḍharaṅgadā || 13.118
kṣullakaḥ kṣudraśaṅkhaḥ syātśambūko nakhaśaṅkhakaḥ |
kṣullakaḥ kaṭukastiktaḥ śūlahārī ca dīpanaḥ || 13.119
śaṅkho hyarṇobhavaḥ kamburjalajaḥ pāvanadhvaniḥ |
kuṭilo'ntarmahānādaḥ kambūḥ pūtaḥ sunādakaḥ || 13.120
susvaro dīrghanādaśca bahunādo haripriyaḥ |
evaṃ ṣoḍaśadhā jñeyo dhavalo maṅgalapradaḥ || 13.121
śaṅkhaḥ kaṭurasaḥ śītaḥ puṣṭivīryabalapradaḥ |
gulmaśūlaharaḥ śvāsanāśano viṣadoṣanut || 13.122
krimiśaṅkhaḥ krimijalajaḥ krimivāriruhaśca jantukambuśca |
kathito rasavīryādyaiḥ kṛtadhībhiḥ śaṅkhasadṛśo'yam || 13.123
kapardako varāṭaśca kapardiśca varāṭikā |
carācaraścaro varyo bālakrīḍaranakaśca saḥ || 13.124
kapardaḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇaḥ karṇaśūlavraṇāpahaḥ |
gulmaśūlāmayaghnaśca netradoṣanikṛntanaḥ || 13.125
śuktirmuktāprasūścaiva mahāśuktiśca śuktikā |
muktāsphoṭastautikaṃ tu mauktikaprasavā ca sā |
jñeyā mauktikaśuktiśca muktāmātāṅkadhā smṛtā || 13.126
muktāśuktiḥ kaṭuḥ snigdhā śvāsahṛdrogahāriṇī |
śūlapraśamanī rucyā madhurā dīpanī parā || 13.127
jalaśuktirvāriśuktiḥ krimisūḥ kṣudraśuktikā |
śambūkā jalaśuktiśca puṭikā toyaśuktikā || 13.128
jalaśuktiḥ kaṭuḥ snigdhā dīpanī gulmaśūlanut |
viṣadoṣaharā rucyā pācanī baladāyinī || 13.129
khaṭinī khaṭikā caiva khaṭī dhavalamṛttikā |
sitadhātuḥ śvetadhātuḥ pāṇḍumṛtpāṇḍumṛttikā || 13.130
khaṭinī madhurā tiktā śītalā pittadāhanut |
vraṇadoṣakaphāsraghnī netraroganikṛntanī || 13.131
dugdhāśmā dugdhapāṣāṇaḥ kṣīrī gomedasaṃnibhaḥ |
vajrābho dīptikaḥ saudho dugdhī kṣīrayavo'pi ca || 13.132
