dugdhapāṣāṇako rucya īṣaduṣṇo jvarāpahaḥ |
pittahṛdrogaśūlaghnaḥ kāsādhmānavināśanaḥ || 13.133
karpūranāmabhiścādāvante ca maṇivācakaḥ |
karpūramaṇināmāyaṃ yuktyā vātādidoṣanut || 13.134
sikatā vālukā siktā śītalā sūkṣmaśarkarā |
pravāhotthā mahāślakṣṇā sūkṣmā pānīyacūrṇakā || 13.135
vālukā madhurā śītā saṃtāpaśramanāśinī |
sekaprayogataścaiva śākhāśaityānilāpahā || 13.136
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ kālakuṣṭhaṃ ca viraṅgaṃ raṅgadāyakam |
recakaṃ pulakaṃ caiva śodhakaṃ kālapālakam || 13.137
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ ca dvidhā proktaṃ tārahemābhrakaṃ tathā |
kaṭukaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ recakaṃ vraṇaśūlahṛt || 13.138
vimalaṃ nirmalaṃ svacchamamalaṃ svacchadhātukam |
bāṇasaṃkhyābhidhaṃ proktaṃ tārahema dvidhā matam || 13.139
vimalaṃ kaṭutiktoṣṇaṃ tvagdoṣavraṇanāśanam |
rasavīryādike tulyaṃ vedhe syādbhinnavīryakam || 13.140
mūṣakasyābhidhā pūrvaṃ pāṣāṇasyābhidhā tataḥ |
ākhupāṣāṇanāmāyaṃ lohasaṃkarakārakaḥ || 13.141
dhanārthino janāḥ sarve ramante'sminnatīva yat |
tato ratnamiti proktaṃ śabdaśāstraviśāradaiḥ || 13.142
dravyaṃ kiṃcana lakṣmībhogyaṃ vasuvastusampado vṛddhiḥ |
śrīrvyavahāryaṃ draviṇaṃ dhanamartho rāḥ svāpateyaṃ ca || 13.143
ratnaṃ vasumaṇirupalo dṛṣaddraviṇadīptavīryāṇi |
rauhiṇakamabdhisāraṃ khānikamākarajamityabhinnārthāḥ || 13.144
māṇikyaṃ śoṇaratnaṃ ca ratnarāḍraviratnakam |
śṛṅgāri raṅgamāṇikyaṃ taralo ratnanāyakaḥ || 13.145
rāgadṛkpadmarāgaśca ratnaṃ śoṇopalastathā |
saugandhikaṃ lohitakaṃ kuruvindaṃ śarendukam || 13.146
māṇikyaṃ madhuraṃ snigdhaṃ vātapittapraṇāśanam |
ratnaprayogaprajñānāṃ rasāyanakaraṃ param || 13.147
snigdhaṃ gurugātrayutaṃ dīptaṃ svacchaṃ suraṅgaṃ ca |
iti jātyādimāṇikyaṃ kalyāṇaṃ dhāraṇātkurute || 13.148
dvichāyamabhrapihitaṃ karkaśaśarkarilaṃ bhinnadhūmraṃ ca |
rāgavikalaṃ virūpaṃ laghu māṇikyaṃ na dhārayeddhīmān || 13.149
tadraktaṃ yadi padmarāgamatha tatpītātiraktaṃ dvidhā jānīyātkuruvindakaṃ yadaruṇaṃ syādeṣu saugandhikam |
tannīlaṃ yadi nīlagandhikamiti jñeyaṃ caturdhā budhairmāṇikyaṃ kaṣagharṣaṇe'pyavikalaṃ rāgeṇa jātyaṃ jaguḥ || 13.150
muktā saumyā mauktikaṃ mauktikeyaṃ tāraṃ tārā bhautikaṃ tārakā ca |
ambhaḥsāraṃ śītalaṃ nīrajaṃ ca nakṣatraṃ syādindraratnaṃ valakṣam || 13.151
muktāphalaṃ binduphalaṃ ca muktikā śaukteyakaṃ śuklamaṇiḥ śaśipriyam |
svacchaṃ himaṃ haimavataṃ sudhāṃśubhaṃ sudhāṃśuratnaṃ śaranetrasaṃmitam || 13.152
mauktikaṃ ca madhuraṃ suśītalaṃ dṛṣṭirogaśamanaṃ viṣāpaham |
rājayakṣmamukharoganāśanaṃ kṣīṇavīryabalapuṣṭivardhanam || 13.153
nakṣatrābhaṃ vṛttamatyantamuktaṃ snigdhaṃ sthūlaṃ nirmalaṃ nirvraṇaṃ ca |
nyastaṃ dhatte gauravaṃ yattulāyāṃ tannirmūlyaṃ mauktikaṃ saukhyadāyi || 13.154
yadvicchāyaṃ mauktikaṃ vyaṅgakāyaṃ śuktisparśaṃ raktatāṃ cāpi dhatte |
matsyākṣyābhaṃ rūkṣamuttānanimnaṃ naitaddhāryaṃ dhīmatā doṣadāyi || 13.155
mātaṃgoragamīnapotriśirasastvaksāraśaṅkhāmbubhṛtśuktīnāmudarācca mauktikamaṇiḥ spaṣṭaṃ bhavatyaṣṭadhā |
chāyāḥ pāṭalanīlapītadhavalāstatrāpi sāmānyataḥ saptānāṃ bahuśo na labdhiritaracchaukteyakaṃ tūlvaṇam || 13.156
lavaṇakṣārakṣodini pātre gomūtrapūrite kṣiptam |
marditamapi śālituṣairyadavikṛtaṃ tattu mauktikaṃ jātyam || 13.157
pravālo'ṅgārakamaṇirvidrumo'mbhodhipallavaḥ |
bhaumaratnaṃ ca raktāṅgo raktāṅkuro latāmaṇiḥ || 13.158
pravālo madhuro'mlaśca kaphapittādidoṣanut |
vīryakāntikaraḥ strīṇāṃ dhṛto maṅgaladāyakaḥ || 13.159
śuddhaṃ dṛḍhaghanaṃ vṛtaṃ snigdhagātraṃ suraṅgakam |
samaṃ guru sirāhīnaṃ pravālaṃ dhārayetśubham || 13.160
gauraraṅgaṃ jalākrāntaṃ vakraṃ sūkṣmaṃ sakoṭaram |
rūkṣaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ laghu śvetaṃ pravālamaśubhaṃ tyajet || 13.161
bālārkakiraṇaraktā sāgarasalilodbhavā pravālalatā |
yā na tyajati nijaruciṃ nikaṣe ghṛṣṭāpi sā smṛtā jātyā || 13.162
gārutmataṃ marakataṃ rauhīṇeyaṃ harinmaṇiḥ |
sauparṇaṃ garuḍodgīrṇaṃ budharatnāśmagarbhajam |
garalārirvāyavālaṃ gāruḍaṃ rudrasaṃmitam || 13.163
marakataṃ viṣaghnaṃ ca śītalaṃ madhuraṃ rase |
āmapittaharaṃ rucyaṃ puṣṭidaṃ bhūtanāśanam || 13.164
svacchaṃ guru succhāyaṃ snigdhaṃ gātre ca mārdavasametam |
avyaṅgaṃ bahuraṅgaṃ śṛṅgāri marakataṃ śubhaṃ bibhṛyāt || 13.165
śarkarilakalilarūkṣaṃ malinaṃ laghu hīnakānti kalmāṣam |
trāsayutaṃ vikṛtāṅgaṃ marakatamamaro'pi nopabhuñjīta || 13.166
yatśaivālaśikhaṇḍiśādvalaharitkācaiśca cāṣacchadaiḥ khadyotena ca bālakīravapuṣā śairīṣapuṣpeṇa ca |
chāyābhiḥ samatāṃ dadhāti tadidaṃ nirdiṣṭamaṣṭātmakaṃ jātyaṃ yattapanātapaiśca parito gārutmataṃ rañjayet || 13.167
pītastu puṣparāgaḥ pītasphaṭikaśca pītaraktaśca |
pītāśmā gururatnaṃ pītamaṇiḥ puṣparāgaśca || 13.168
puṣparāgo'mlaśītaśca vātajiddīpanaḥ paraḥ |
āyuḥ śriyaṃ ca prajñāṃ ca dhāraṇātkurute nṛṇām || 13.169
sacchāyapītagurugātrasuraṅgaśuddhaṃ snigdhaṃ ca nirmalamatīva suvṛttaśītam |
yaḥ puṣparāgamamalaṃ kalayedamuṣya puṣṇāti kīrtimatiśauryasukhāyurarthān || 13.170
kṛṣṇabindvaṅkitaṃ rūkṣaṃ dhavalaṃ malinaṃ laghu |
vicchāyaṃ śarkarāṅgābhaṃ puṣparāgaṃ sadoṣakam || 13.171
ghṛṣṭaṃ nikāṣapaṭṭe yatpuṣyati rāgamadhikamātmīyam |
tena khalu puṣparāgo jātyatayāyaṃ parīkṣakairuktaḥ || 13.172
vajramindrāyudhaṃ hīraṃ bhiduraṃ kuliśaṃ paviḥ |
abhedyamaśiraṃ ratnaṃ dṛḍhaṃ bhārgavakaṃ smṛtam |
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ bahudhāraṃ ca śatakoṭyabdhibhūmitam || 13.173
vajraṃ ca ṣaḍrasopetaṃ sarvarogāpahārakam |
sarvāghaśamanaṃ saukhyaṃ dehadārḍhyaṃ rasāyanam || 13.174
bhasmāṅgaṃ kākapādaṃ ca rekhākrāntaṃ tu vartulam |
adhāraṃ malinaṃ bindusaṃtrāsaṃ sphuṭitaṃ tathā |
nīlābhaṃ cipiṭaṃ rūkṣaṃ tadvajraṃ doṣadaṃ tyajet || 13.175
śvetālohitapītakamecakatayā chāyāścatasraḥ kramātviprāditvamihāsya yatsumanasaḥ śaṃsanti satyaṃ tataḥ |
sphītāṃ kīrtimanuttamāṃ śriyamidaṃ dhatte yathāsvaṃ dhṛtaṃ martyānāmayathāyathaṃ tu kuliśaṃ pathyaṃ hi nānyattataḥ || 13.176
yatpāṣāṇatale nikāṣanikare nodghṛṣyate niṣṭhurairyaccolūkhalalohamudgaraghanairlekhāṃ na yātyāhatam |
yaccānyannijalīlayaiva dalayedvajreṇa vā bhidyate tajjātyaṃ kuliśaṃ vadanti kuśalāḥ ślāghyaṃ mahārghyaṃ ca tat || 13.177
vipraḥ so'pi rasāyaneṣu balavānaṣṭāṅgasiddhiprado rājanyastu nṛṇāṃ valīpalitajitmṛtyuṃ jayedañjasā |
dravyākarṣaṇasiddhidastu sutarāṃ vaiśvo'tha śūdro bhavetsarvavyādhiharastadeṣa kathito vajrasya varṇyo guṇaḥ || 13.178
nīlastu sauriratnaṃ syānnīlāśmā nīlaratnakaḥ |
nīlopalastṛṇagrāhī mahānīlaḥ sunīlakaḥ |
masāramindranīlaṃ syādgallarkaḥ padmarāgajaḥ || 13.179
nīlaḥ satiktakoṣṇaśca kaphapittānilāpahaḥ |
yo dadhāti śarīre syātsaurirmaṅgalado bhavet || 13.180
na nimno nirmalo gātramasṛṇo gurudīptikaḥ |
tṛṇagrāhī mṛdurnīlo durlabho lakṣaṇānvitaḥ || 13.181
mṛccharkarāśmakalilo vicchāyo malino laghuḥ |
rūkṣaḥ sphuṭitagartaśca varjyo nīlaḥ sadoṣakaḥ || 13.182
sitaśoṇapītakṛṣṇāśchāyā nīle kramādimāḥ kathitāḥ |
viprādivarṇasiddhyai dhāraṇamasyāpi vajravatphalavat || 13.183
āstyānaṃ candrikāsyandaṃ sundaraṃ kṣīrapūritam |
yaḥ pātraṃ rañjayatyāśu sa jātyo nīla ucyate || 13.184
gomedakastu gomedo rāhuratnaṃ tamomaṇiḥ |
svarbhānavaḥ ṣaḍāhvo'yaṃ piṅgasphaṭika ityapi || 13.185
gomedako'mla uṣṇaśca vātakopavikārajit |
dīpanaḥ pācanaścaiva dhṛto'yaṃ pāpanāśanaḥ || 13.186
gomūtrābhaṃ yanmṛdu snigdhamugdhaṃ śuddhacchāyaṃ gauravaṃ yacca dhatte |
hemāraktaṃ śrīmatāṃ yogyametatgomedākhyaṃ ratnamākhyānti santaḥ || 13.187
pātre yatra nyaste payaḥ prayātyeva gojalojjvalatām |
gharṣe'pyahīnakāntiṃ gomedaṃ taṃ budhā vidurjātyam || 13.188
araṅgaṃ śvetakṛṣṇāṅgaṃ rekhātrāsayutaṃ laghu |
vicchāyaṃ śarkarāgāraṃ gomedaṃ vibudhastyajet || 13.189
vaiḍūryaṃ keturatnaṃ ca kaitavaṃ bālavīyajam |
prāvṛṣyamabhralohaṃ ca khaśabdāṅkurakastathā |
vaiḍūryaratnaṃ samproktaṃ jñeyaṃ vidūrajaṃ tathā || 13.190
vaiḍūryamuṣṇamamlaṃ ca kaphamārutanāśanam |
gulmādidoṣaśamanaṃ bhūṣitaṃ ca śubhāvaham || 13.191
ekaṃ veṇupalāśapeśalarucā māyūrakaṇṭhatviṣā mārjārekṣaṇapiṅgalacchavijuṣā jñeyaṃ tridhā chāyayā |
yadgātre gurutāṃ dadhāti nitarāṃ snigdhaṃ tu doṣojjhitaṃ vaiḍūryaṃ vimalaṃ vadanti sudhiyaḥ svacchaṃ ca tacchobhanam || 13.192
vicchāyaṃ mṛcchilāgarbhe laghu rūkṣaṃ ca sakṣatam |
satrāsaṃ paruṣaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ vaiḍūryaṃ dūratastyajet || 13.193
ghṛṣṭaṃ yadātmanā svacchaṃ svachāyāṃ nikaṣāśmani |
sphuṭaṃ pradarśayedetadvaiḍūryaṃ jātyamucyate || 13.194
māṇikyaṃ padmabandhorativimalatamaṃ mauktikaṃ śītabhānormāheyasya pravālaṃ marakatamatulaṃ kalpayedindusūnoḥ |
devejye puṣparāgaṃ kuliśamapi kavernīlamarkātmajasya svarbhānoścāpi gomedakamatha vidurodbhāvitaṃ kiṃtu ketoḥ || 13.195
itthametāni ratnāni tattaduddeśataḥ kramāt |
yo dadyādbibhṛyādvāpi tasmin sānugrahā grahāḥ || 13.196
saṃtyajya vajramekaṃ sarvatrānyatra saṃghāte |
lāghavamatha komalatā sādhāraṇadoṣa eva vijñeyaḥ || 13.197
lohitakavajramauktikamarakatanīlā mahopalāḥ pañca |
vaiḍūryapuṣparāgapravālagomedakādayo'rvāñcaḥ || 13.198
gomedapravālavāyavyaṃ devejyamaṇīndrataraṇikāntādyāḥ |
nānāvarṇaguṇāḍhyā vijñeyāḥ sphaṭikajātayaḥ prājñaiḥ || 13.199
sphaṭikaḥ sitopalaḥ syādamalamaṇirnirmalopalaḥ svacchaḥ |
svacchamaṇiramalaratnaṃ nistuṣaratnaṃ śivapriyaṃ navadhā || 13.200
sphaṭikaḥ samavīryaśca pittadāhārtidoṣanut |
tasyākṣamālā japatāṃ datte koṭiguṇaṃ phalam || 13.201
yadgaṅgātoyabinduchavivimalatamaṃ nistuṣaṃ netrahṛdyaṃ snigdhaṃ śuddhāntarālaṃ madhuramatihimaṃ pittadāhāsrahāri |
pāṣāṇairyannighṛṣṭaṃ sphuṭitamapi nijāṃ svacchatāṃ naiva jahyāttajjātyaṃ jātvalabhyaṃ śubhamupacinute śaivaratnaṃ vicitram || 13.202
atha bhavati sūryakāntastapanamaṇistapanaśca ravikāntaḥ |
dīptopalo'gnigarbho jvalanāśmā'rkopalaśca vasunāmā || 13.203
sūryakānto bhaveduṣṇo nirmalaśca rasāyanaḥ |
vātaśleṣmaharo medhyaḥ pūjanādravituṣṭidaḥ || 13.204
śuddhaḥ snigdho nirvraṇo nistuṣo'ntaryo nirmṛṣṭo vyomni nairmalyameti |
yaḥ sūryāṃśusparśaniṣṭhyūtavahnirjātyaḥ so'yaṃ jāyate sūryakāntaḥ || 13.205
vaikrāntaṃ caiva vikrāntaṃ nīcavajraṃ kuvajrakam |
gonāsaḥ kṣudrakuliśaṃ cūrṇavajraṃ ca gonasaḥ || 13.206
vajrābhāve ca vaikrāntaṃ rasavīryādike samam |
kṣayakuṣṭhaviṣaghnaṃ ca puṣṭidaṃ surasāyanam || 13.207
vajrākāratayaiva prasahya haraṇāya sarvarogāṇām |
yadvikrāntiṃ dhatte tadvaikrāntaṃ budhairidaṃ kathitam || 13.208
indrakāntaścandrakāntaścandrāśmā candrajopalaḥ |
śītāśmā candrikādrāvaḥ śaśikāntaśca saptadhā || 13.209
candrakāntastu śiśiraḥ snigdhaḥ pittāsratāpahṛt |
śivaprītikaraḥ svaccho grahālakṣmīvināśakṛt || 13.210
snigdhaṃ śvetaṃ pītamātrāsametaṃ dhatte citte svacchatāṃ yanmunīnām |
yacca srāvaṃ yāti candrāṃśusaṅgājjātyaṃ ratnaṃ candrakāntākhyametat || 13.211
rājāvarto nṛpāvarto rājanyāvartakastathā |
āvartamaṇirāvartaḥ syādityeṣaḥ śarāhvayaḥ || 13.212
rājāvartaḥ kaṭuḥ snigdhaḥ śiśiraḥ pittanāśanaḥ |
saubhāgyaṃ kurute nṝṇāṃ bhūṣaṇeṣu prayojitaḥ || 13.213
nirgāramasitamasṛṇaṃ nīlaṃ guru nirmalaṃ bahuchāyam |
śikhikaṇṭhasamaṃ saumyaṃ rājāvartaṃ vadanti jātyamaṇim || 13.214
perojaṃ haritāśmaṃ ca bhasmāṅgaṃ haritaṃ dvidhā |
perojaṃ sukaṣāyaṃ syānmadhuraṃ dīpanaṃ param || 13.215
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva saṃyogācca yathā viṣam |
tatsarvaṃ nāśayetśīghraṃ śūlaṃ bhūtādidoṣajam || 13.216
siddhāḥ pāradamabhrakaṃ ca vividhān dhātūṃśca lohāni ca prāhuḥ kiṃca maṇīnapīha sakalān saṃskārataḥ siddhidān |
yatsaṃskāravihīnameṣu hi bhavedyaccānyathā saṃskṛtaṃ tanmartyaṃ viṣavannihanti tadiha jñeyā budhaiḥ saṃskriyāḥ || 13.217
yān saṃskṛtān śubhaguṇānatha cānyathā ceddoṣāṃśca yānapi diśanti rasādayo'mī |
yāśceha santi khalu saṃskṛtayastadetannātrābhyadhāyi bahuvistarabhītibhāgbhiḥ || 13.218
iti lohadhāturasaratnatadbhidādyabhidhāguṇaprakaṭanasphuṭākṣaram |
avadhārya vargamimamādyavaidyakapraguṇaprayogakuśalo bhavedbudhaḥ || 13.219
kurvanti ye nijaguṇena rasādhvagena nṝṇāṃ jarantyapi vapūṃṣi punarnavāni |
teṣāmayaṃ nivasatiḥ kanakādikānāṃ vargaḥ prasidhyati rasāyanavarganāmnā || 13.220
nityaṃ yasya guṇāḥ kilāntaralasatkalyāṇabhūyastathā cittākarṣaṇacañcavastribhuvanaṃ bhūmnā parikurvate |
tenātraiṣa kṛte nṛsiṃhakṛtinā nāmādicūḍāmaṇau saṃsthāmeti mitastrayodaśatayā vargaḥ suvarṇādikaḥ || 13.221


rājanighaṇṭu, ড়ānīyādivarga
pānīyajīvanavanāmṛtapuṣkarāmbhaḥpātho'mbuśambarapayaḥsalilodakāni |
āpaḥ kavāruṇakabandhajalāni nīrakīlālavārikamalāni viṣārṇasī ca || 14.1
bhuvanaṃ dahanārātirvāstoyaṃ sarvatomukhaṃ kṣīram |
ghanarasanimnagameghaprasavarasāśceti vahnimitāḥ || 14.2
pānīyaṃ madhuraṃ himaṃ ca rucidaṃ tṛṣṇāviśoṣāpahaṃ mohabhrāntimapākaroti kurute bhuktānnapaktiṃ parām |
nidrālasyanirāsanaṃ viṣaharaṃ śrāntātisaṃtarpaṇaṃ nṝṇāṃ dhībalavīryatuṣṭijananaṃ naṣṭāṅgapuṣṭipradam || 14.3
divyodakaṃ kharāri syādākāśasalilaṃ tathā |
vyomodakaṃ cāntarikṣajalaṃ ceṣvabhidhāhvayam || 14.4
vyomodakaṃ tridoṣaghnaṃ madhuraṃ pathyadaṃ param |
rucyaṃ dīpanadaṃ tṛṣṇāśramamehāpahārakam || 14.5
sadyovṛṣṭyambu bhūmisthaṃ kaluṣaṃ doṣadāyakam |
cirasthitaṃ laghu svacchaṃ pathyaṃ svādu sukhāvaham || 14.6
yādonāthasamudrasindhujaladākūpārapāthodhayaḥ pārāvārapayodhisāgarasarinnāthāśca vārāṃ nidhiḥ |
ambhorāśisarasvadambudhinadīnāthābdhinityārṇavodanvadvāridhivārdhayaḥ kadhirapāṃnātho'pi ratnākaraḥ || 14.7
sāgarasalilaṃ visraṃ lavaṇaṃ raktāmayapradaṃ coṣṇam |
vaivarṇyadoṣajananaṃ viśeṣāddāhārtipittakaraṇaṃ ca || 14.8
nadī dhunī nirjhariṇī taraṃgiṇī sarasvatī śaivalinī samudragā |
kūlaṃkaṣā kūlavatī ca nimnagā śaivālinī sindhurathāpagāpi ca || 14.9
hradinī samudrakāntā sāgaragā hrādinī saritkarṣūḥ |
srotasvinī sunīrā rodhovakrā ca vāhinī taṭinī || 14.10
nādeyaṃ salilaṃ svacchaṃ laghu dīpanapācanam |
rucyaṃ tṛṣṇāpahaṃ pathyaṃ madhuraṃ ceṣaduṣṇakam || 14.11
gaṅgā bhānusutā revā candrabhāgā sarasvatī |
madhumatī vipāśātha śoṇo ghargharakastathā || 14.12
vetrāvatī kṣaudravatī payoṣṇī tāpī vitastā sarayūśca sindhuḥ |
mahāśatadrurhyatha gautamī syātkṛṣṇā ca tuṅgā ca kaverikanyā || 14.13
ityevamādyāḥ saritaḥ samastāstaḍāgavāpīhradakūpakādyāḥ |
anye'pyanūpātmakadeśabhedāḥ kaulābhidhānaiḥ svayamūhanīyāḥ || 14.14
dhārākārādikātoyamantarikṣodbhavaṃ tathā |
parīkṣyeta yathā coktaṃ jñātavyaṃ jalavedibhiḥ || 14.15
gaṅgā svargasaridvarā tripathagā mandākinī jāhnavī puṇyā viṣṇupadī samudrasubhagā bhāgīrathī svarṇadī |
triḥsrotā suradīrghikā suranadī siddhāpagā svardhunī jyeṣṭhā jahnusutā ca bhīṣmajananī śubhrā ca śailendrajā || 14.16
śītaṃ svādu svacchamatyantarucyaṃ pathyaṃ pākyaṃ pāvanaṃ pāpahāri |
tṛṣṇāmohadhvaṃsanaṃ dīpanaṃ ca prajñāṃ datte vāri bhāgīrathīyam || 14.17
yamunā tapanatanujā kalindakanyā yamasvasā ca kālindī || 14.18
pittadāhavamanaśramāpahaṃ svādu vātajananaṃ ca pācanam |
vahnidīpanakaraṃ virocanaṃ yāmunaṃ jalamidaṃ balapradam || 14.19
revā mekalakanyā somasutā narmadā ca vijñeyā || 14.20
salilaṃ śītalaṃ supathyaṃ kurute pittakaphaprakopaṇam |
sakalāmayamardanaṃ ca rucyaṃ madhuraṃ mekalakanyakāsamuttham || 14.21
cāndrabhāgasalilaṃ suśītalaṃ dāhapittaśamanaṃ ca vātadam || 14.22
sarasvatī plakṣasamudbhavā ca sā vākpradā brahmasatī ca bhāratī |
vedāgraṇīścaiva payoṣṇijātā vāṇī viśālā kuṭilā daśāhvā || 14.23
sarasvatījalaṃ svādu pūtaṃ sarvarujāpaham |
rucyaṃ dīpanadaṃ pathyaṃ dehakāntikaraṃ laghu || 14.24
cāndrabhāgaguṇasāmyadaṃ jalaṃ kiṃca mādhumatamagnidīpanam || 14.25
śatadrorvipāśāyujaḥ sindhunadyāḥ suśītaṃ laghu svādu sarvāmayaghnam |
jalaṃ nirmalaṃ dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ ca pradatte balaṃ buddhimedhāyuṣaṃ ca || 14.26
śoṇe ghargharake jalaṃ tu rucidaṃ saṃtāpaśoṣāpahaṃ pathyaṃ vahnikaraṃ tathā ca baladaṃ kṣīṇāṅgapuṣṭipradam |
tatrānyā dadhate jalaṃ sumadhuraṃ kāntipradaṃ puṣṭidaṃ vṛṣyaṃ dīpanapācanaṃ balakaraṃ vetrāvatī tāpinī || 14.27
payoṣṇīsalilaṃ rucyaṃ pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam |
sarvāmayaharaṃ saukhyaṃ balakāntipradaṃ laghu || 14.28
vitastāsalilaṃ svādu tridoṣaśamanaṃ laghu |
prajñābuddhipradaṃ pathyaṃ tāpajāḍyaharaṃ param || 14.29
sarayūsalilaṃ svādu balapuṣṭipradāyakam || 14.30
godāvarī gautamasambhavā sā brahmādrijātāpyatha gautamī ca || 14.31
pittārtiraktārtisamīrahāri pathyaṃ paraṃ dīpanapāpahāri |
kuṣṭhādiduṣṭāmayadoṣahāri godāvarīvāri tṛṣānivāri || 14.32
kṛṣṇānadī kṛṣṇasamudbhavā syātsā kṛṣṇaveṇāpi ca kṛṣṇagaṅgā || 14.33
kārṣṇyaṃ jāḍyakaraṃ svādu pūtaṃ pittāsrakopanam |
kṛṣṇaveṇājalaṃ svacchaṃ rucyaṃ dīpanapācanam || 14.34
malāpahā bhīmarathī ca ghaṭṭagā yathā ca kṛṣṇājalasāmyadā guṇaiḥ |
malāpahāghaṭṭagayostathāpi pathyaṃ laghu svādutaraṃ sukāntidam || 14.35
tuṅgabhadrājalaṃ snigdhaṃ nirmalaṃ svādadaṃ guru |
kaṇḍūpittāsradaṃ prāyaḥ sātmye pathyakaraṃ param || 14.36
kāverīsalilaṃ svādu śramaghnaṃ laghu dīpanam |
dadrukuṣṭhādidoṣaghnaṃ medhābuddhirucipradam || 14.37
nadīnāmitthamanyāsāṃ deśadoṣādibhedataḥ |
tattadguṇānvitaṃ vāri jñātavyaṃ kṛtabuddhibhiḥ || 14.38
sarvā gurvī prāṅmukhī vāhinī yā laghvī paścādvāhinī niścayena |
deśe deśe tadguṇānāṃ viśeṣādeṣā dhatte gauravaṃ lāghavaṃ ca || 14.39
vindhyātprācī yāpyavācī pratīcī yā codīcī syānnadī sā krameṇa |
vātāṭopaṃ śleṣmapittārtilopaṃ pittodrekaṃ pathyapākaṃ ca dhatte || 14.40
himavati malayācale ca vindhye prabhavati sahyagirau ca yā sravantī |
sṛjati kila śirorujādidoṣānapanudate'pi ca pāriyātrajātā || 14.41
nadyaḥ prāvṛṣijāstu pīnasakaphaśvāsārtikāsapradāḥ pathyā vātakaphāpahāḥ śaradijā hemantajā buddhidāḥ |
saṃtāpaṃ śamayanti śaṃ vidadhate śaiśiryavāsantajāstṛṣṇādāhavamiśramārtiśamadā grīṣme yathā sadguṇāḥ || 14.42
anūpasalilaṃ svādu snigdhaṃ pittaharaṃ guru |
tanoti pāmakaṇḍūtikaphavātajvarāmayān || 14.43
jāṅgalasalilaṃ svādu tridoṣaghnaṃ rucipradam |
pathyaṃ cāyurbalavīryapuṣṭidaṃ kāntikṛtparam || 14.44
sādhāraṇaṃ jalaṃ rucyaṃ dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ laghu |
śramatṛṣṇāpahaṃ vātakaphamedoghnapuṣṭidam || 14.45
jātaṃ tāmramṛdastadeva salilaṃ vātādidoṣapradaṃ deśājjāḍyakaraṃ ca durjarataraṃ doṣāvahaṃ dhūsaram |
vātaghnaṃ tu śilāśirotthamamalaṃ pathyaṃ laghu svādu ca śreṣṭhaṃ śyāmamṛdastridoṣaśamanaṃ sarvāmayaghnaṃ payaḥ || 14.46
hradavāri vahnijananaṃ madhuraṃ kaphavātahāri pathyaṃ ca || 14.47
prasravaṇajalaṃ svacchaṃ laghu madhuraṃ rocanaṃ ca dīpanakṛt || 14.48
taḍāgasalilaṃ svādu kaṣāyaṃ vātadaṃ kiyat || 14.49
vāpījalaṃ tu saṃtāpi vātaśleṣmakaraṃ guru || 14.50
kaphaghnaṃ kūpapānīyaṃ kṣāraṃ pittakaraṃ laghu || 14.51
audbhidaṃ pittaśamanaṃ salilaṃ laghu ca smṛtam || 14.52
kedārasalilaṃ svādu vipāke doṣadaṃ guru |
tadeva baddhamuktaṃ tu viśeṣāddoṣadaṃ bhavet || 14.53
nādeyaṃ navamṛdghaṭeṣu nihitaṃ saṃtaptamarkāṃśubhiryāminyāṃ ca niviṣṭamindrakiraṇairmandānilāndolitam |
elādyaiḥ parivāsitaṃ śramaharaṃ pittoṣṇadāhe viṣe mūrchāraktamadātyayeṣu ca hitaṃ śaṃsanti haṃsodakam || 14.54
yaḥ pānīyaṃ pibati śiśiraṃ svādu nityaṃ niśīthe pratyūṣe vā pibati yadi vā ghrāṇarandhreṇa dhīraḥ |
so'yaṃ sadyaḥ patagapatinā spardhate netraśaktyā svargācāryaṃ prahasati dhiyā dveṣṭi dasrau ca tanvā || 14.55
viṇmūtrāruṇanīlikāviṣahataṃ taptaṃ ghanaṃ phenilaṃ dantagrāhyamanārtavaṃ salavaṇaṃ śaivālakaiḥ saṃvṛtam |
jantuvrātavimiśritaṃ gurutaraṃ parṇaughapaṅkāvilaṃ candrārkāṃśutirohitaṃ ca na pibennīraṃ jaḍaṃ doṣalam || 14.56
pārśvaśūle pratiśyāye vātadoṣe navajvare |
hikkādhmānādidoṣeṣu śītāmbu parivarjayet || 14.57
dhātukṣaye raktavikāradoṣe vāntyasramehe viṣavibhrameṣu |
jīrṇajvare śaithilyasaṃnipāte jalaṃ praśastaṃ śṛtaśītalaṃ tu || 14.58
taptaṃ pāthaḥ pādabhāgena hīnaṃ proktaṃ pathyaṃ vātajātāmayaghnam |
ardhāṃśonaṃ nāśayedvātapittaṃ pādaprāyaṃ tattu doṣatrayaghnam || 14.59
hemante pādahīnaṃ tu pādārdhonaṃ tu śārade |
prāvṛḍvasante śiśire grīṣme cārdhāvaśeṣitam || 14.60
kaupaṃ prāsravaṇaṃ vāpi śiśirartuvasantayoḥ |
grīṣme cauḍaṃ tu seveta doṣadaṃ syādato'nyathā || 14.61
taptaṃ divā jāḍyamupaiti naktaṃ naktaṃ ca taptaṃ tu divā guru syāt |
divā ca naktaṃ ca nṛbhistadātvataptaṃ jalaṃ yuktamato grahītum || 14.62
uṣṇaṃ kvāpi kvāpi śītaṃ kavoṣṇaṃ kvāpi kvāpi kvāthaśītaṃ ca pāthaḥ |
itthaṃ nṝṇāṃ pathyametatprayuktaṃ kālāvasthādehasaṃsthānurodhāt || 14.63
apanayati pavanadoṣaṃ dalayati kaphamāśu nāśayatyarucim |
pācayati cānnamanalaṃ puṣṇāti niśīthapītamuṣṇāmbhaḥ || 14.64
rātrau pītamajīrṇadoṣaśamanaṃ śaṃsanti sāmānyataḥ pītaṃ vāri niśāvasānasamaye sarvāmayadhvaṃsanam |
bhuktvā tūrdhvamidaṃ ca puṣṭijananaṃ prākcedapuṣṭipradaṃ rucyaṃ jāṭharavahnipāṭavakaraṃ pathyaṃ ca bhuktyantare || 14.65
atyambupānānna vipacyate'nnamanambupānācca sa eva doṣaḥ |
tasmānnaro vahnivivardhanārthaṃ muhurmuhurvāri pibedabhūri || 14.66
jalaṃ caturvidhaṃ prāhurantarikṣodbhavaṃ budhāḥ |
dhāraṃ ca kārakaṃ caiva tauṣāraṃ haimamityapi || 14.67
ambu varṣodbhavaṃ dhāraṃ kāraṃ varṣopalodbhavam |
nīhāratoyaṃ tauṣāraṃ haimaṃ prātarhimodbhavam || 14.68
dhāraṃ ca dvividhaṃ proktaṃ gāṅgasāmudrabhedataḥ |
tatra gāṅgaṃ guṇāḍhyaṃ syātadoṣaṃ pācanaṃ param || 14.69
yadā syādāśvine māsi sūryaḥ svātiviśākhayoḥ |
tadāmbu jaladairmuktaṃ gāṅgamuktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ || 14.70
anyadā mṛgaśīrṣādinakṣatreṣu yadambudaiḥ |
abhivṛṣṭamidaṃ toyaṃ sāmudramiti śabditam || 14.71
dhārādhare varṣati raupyapātre vinyasya śālyodanasiddhapiṇḍe |
dadhnopadigdhe nihitaṃ muhūrtādavikriyaṃ gāṅgamathānyathā syāt || 14.72
gāṅgaṃ jalaṃ svādu suśītalaṃ ca rucipradaṃ pittakaphāpahaṃ ca |
nirdoṣamacchaṃ laghu tacca nityaṃ guṇādhikaṃ vyomni gṛhītamāhuḥ || 14.73
candrakāntodbhavaṃ vāri pittaghnaṃ vimalaṃ laghu |
mūrchāpittāsradāheṣu hitaṃ kāsamadātyaye || 14.74
sāmudrasalilaṃ śītaṃ kaphavātapradaṃ guru |
citrāyāmāśvine tacca guṇāḍhyaṃ gāṅgavadbhavet || 14.75
patitaṃ bhuvi yattoyaṃ gāṅgaṃ sāmudrameva vā |
svasvāśrayavaśādgacchedanyadanyadrasādikam || 14.76
asraṃ ca lavaṇaṃ ca syātpatitaṃ pārthivasthale |
āpye tu madhuraṃ proktaṃ kaṭu tiktaṃ ca taijase || 14.77
kaṣāyaṃ vāyavīye syādavyaktaṃ nābhase smṛtam |
tatra nābhasamevoktamuttamaṃ doṣavarjitam || 14.78
yatra cedāśvine māsi naiva varṣati vāridaḥ |
gāṅgatoyavihīne syuḥ kāle tatrādhikā rujaḥ || 14.79
kvaciduṣṇaṃ kvacicchītaṃ kvacitkvathitaśītalam |
kvacidbheṣajasaṃyuktaṃ na kvacidvāri vāryate || 14.80
ikṣavaḥ pañcadhā proktā nānāvarṇaguṇānvitāḥ |
sitaḥ puṇḍraḥ karaṅkekṣuḥ kṛṣṇo raktaśca te kramāt || 14.81
ikṣuḥ karkaṭako vaṃśaḥ kāntāraḥ sukumārakaḥ |
asipattro madhutṛṇo vṛṣyo guḍatṛṇo nava || 14.82
śvetekṣustu sitekṣuḥ syātkāṣṭhekṣurvaṃśapattrakaḥ |
suvaṃśaḥ pāṇḍurekṣuśca kāṇḍekṣurdhavalekṣukaḥ || 14.83
sitekṣuḥ kaṭhino rucyo guruśca kaphamūtrakṛt |
dīpanaḥ pittadāhaghno vipāke koṣṇadaḥ smṛtaḥ || 14.84
puṇḍrakastu rasālaḥ syātrasekṣuḥ sukumārakaḥ |
karburo miśravarṇaśca nepālekṣuśca saptadhā || 14.85
puṇḍro'timadhuraḥ śītaḥ kaphakṛtpittanāśanaḥ |
dāhaśramaharo rucyo rase saṃtarpaṇaḥ paraḥ || 14.86
anyaḥ karaṅkaśāliḥ syādikṣuvāṭīkṣuvāṭikā |
yāvanī cekṣuyoniśca rasālī rasadālikā || 14.87
karaṅkaśālirmadhuraḥ śītalo rucikṛnmṛduḥ |
pittadāhaharo vṛṣyastejobalavivardhanaḥ || 14.88
kṛṣṇekṣurikṣuraḥ proktaḥ śyāmekṣuḥ kokilākṣakaḥ |
śyāmavaṃśaḥ śyāmalekṣuḥ kokilekṣuśca kathyate || 14.89
kṛṣṇekṣurukto madhuraśca pāke svāduḥ suhṛdyaḥ kaṭuko rasāḍhyaḥ |
tridoṣahārī śamavīryadaśca subalyadāyī bahuvīryadāyī || 14.90
raktekṣuḥ sūkṣmapattraśca śoṇo lohita utkaṭaḥ |
madhuro hrasvamūlaśca lohitekṣuśca kīrtitaḥ || 14.91
lohitekṣuśca madhuraḥ pāke syācchītalo mṛduḥ |
pittadāhaharo vṛṣyastejobalavivardhanaḥ || 14.92
ikṣumūlaṃ tvikṣunetraṃ tacca moraṭakaṃ tathā |
vaṃśanetraṃ vaṃśamūlaṃ moraṭaṃ vaṃśapūrakam || 14.93
mūlādūrddhvantu madhurā madhye'timadhurāstathā |
ikṣavaste'grabhāgeṣu kramāllavaṇanīrasāḥ || 14.94
abhukte pittahāścaite bhukte vātaprakopaṇāḥ |
bhuktamadhye gurutarā itīkṣūṇāṃ guṇāstrayaḥ || 14.95
vṛṣyo raktāsrapittaśramaśamanapaṭuḥ śītalaḥ ślesmado'lpaḥ snigdho hṛdyaśca rucyo racayati ca mudaṃ sūtraśuddhiṃ vidhatte |
kāntiṃ dehasya datte balamati kurute bṛṃhaṇaṃ tṛptidāyī dantairniṣpīdya kāṇḍaṃ mṛduyatirasito mohanaścekṣudaṇḍaḥ || 14.96
pīyūṣopamitaṃ tridoṣaśamanaṃ syāddantaniṣpīḍitaṃ tadvaccedgṛhayantrajaṃ tadaparaṃ ślesmānilaghnaṃ kiyat |
etadvātaharaṃ tu vātajananaṃ jāḍyapratiśvāyadaṃ proktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ kaphānilakaraṃ pānīyamikṣadbhavam || 14.97
madhuraṃ lavaṇakṣāraṃ snigdhaṃ soṣṇaṃ rucipradam |
vṛṣyaṃ vātakaphaghnaṃ ca yāvanālaśarātrasam || 14.98
pakvekṣurasaḥ snigdhaḥ syātkaphavātanāśano'tiguruḥ |
atipākena vidāhaṃ tanute pittāsradoṣaśoṣāṃśca || 14.99
guḍaḥ syādikṣusārastu madhuro rasapākajaḥ |
śiśupriyaḥ sitādiḥ syādaruṇo rasajaḥ smṛtaḥ || 14.100
pittaghnaḥ pavanārtijidrucikaro hṛdyastridoṣāpahaḥ saṃyogena viśeṣato jvaraharaḥ saṃtāpaśāntipradaḥ |
viṇmūtrāmayaśodhano'gnijananaḥ pāṇḍupramehāntakaḥ snigdhaḥ svādutaro laghuḥ śramaharaḥ pathyaḥ purāṇo guḍaḥ || 14.101
syādyāvanālarasapākabhavo guḍo'yaṃ kṣāraḥ kaṭuḥ sumadhuraḥ kaphavātahārī |
pittapradaḥ satatameṣa niṣevyamāṇaḥ kaṇḍūtikuṣṭhajanano'sravidāhahārī || 14.102
śarkaroktā tu mīnāṇḍī śvetā matsyaṇḍikā ca sā |
ahicchatrā tu sikatā sitā caiva guḍodbhavā || 14.103
śarkarā madhurā śītā pittadāhaśramāpahā |
raktadoṣaharā bhrāntikrimikopapraṇāśinī || 14.104
snigdhā puṇḍrakaśarkarā hitakarī kṣīṇe kṣaye'rocake cakṣuṣyā balavardhinī sumadhurā rūkṣā ca vaṃśekṣujā |
vṛṣyā tṛptibalapradā śramaharā śyāmekṣujā śītalā snigdhā kāntikarī rasālajanitā raktekṣujā pittajit || 14.105
yāvanālī himotpannā himānī himaśarkarā |
kṣudraśarkarikā kṣudrā gudguḍā jālabindujā || 14.106
himajā śarkarā gaulyā soṣṇā tiktātipicchilā |
vātaghnī sārikā rucyā dāhapittāsradāyinī || 14.107
sitajānyā śarkarajā mādhavī madhuśarkarā |
mākṣīkaśarkarā proktā sitākhaṇḍaśca khaṇḍakaḥ || 14.108
sitākhaṇḍo'timadhuraścakṣuṣyaḥ chardināśanaḥ |
kuṣṭhavraṇakaphaśvāsahikkāpittāsradoṣanut || 14.109
yavāsaśarkarā tvanyā sudhā modakamodakaḥ |
tavarājaḥ khaṇḍasāraḥ khaṇḍajā khaṇḍamodakaḥ || 14.110
tavarājo'timadhuraḥ pittaśramatṛṣāpahaḥ |
vṛṣyo vidāhamūrchārtibhrāntiśāntikaraḥ saraḥ || 14.111
tavarājodbhavaḥ khaṇḍaḥ sudhā modakajastathā |
khaṇḍajo dravajaḥ siddhamodakāmṛtasārajaḥ || 14.112
dāhaṃ nivārayati tāpamapākaroti tṛptiṃ niyacchati nihanti ca mohamūrchām |
śvāsaṃ nivārayati tarpayatīndriyāṇi śītaḥ sadā sumadhuraḥ khalu siddhikhaṇḍaḥ || 14.113
madhu kṣaudraṃ ca mākṣīkaṃ mākṣikaṃ kusumāsavam |
puṣpāsavaṃ pavitraṃ ca pitryaṃ puṣparasāhvayam || 14.114
mākṣikaṃ bhrāmaraṃ kṣaudraṃ pauttikaṃ chāttrakaṃ tathā |
ārghyamauddālakaṃ dālamityaṣṭau madhujātayaḥ || 14.115
nānāpuṣparasāhārāḥ kapilā vanamakṣikāḥ |
yāḥ sthūlāstābhirutpannaṃ madhu mākṣikamucyate || 14.116
ye snigdhāñjanagolābhāḥ puṣpāsavaparāyaṇāḥ |
bhramarairjanitaṃ taistu bhrāmaraṃ madhu bhaṇyate || 14.117
piṅgalā makṣikāḥ sūkṣmāḥ kṣudrā iti hi viśrutāḥ |
tābhirutpāditaṃ yattu tatkṣaudraṃ madhu kathyate || 14.118
annajā makṣikāḥ piṅgāḥ puttikā iti kīrtitāḥ |
tajjātaṃ madhu dhīmadbhiḥ pauttikaṃ samudāhṛtam || 14.119
chattrākāraṃ tu paṭalaṃ saraghāḥ pītapiṅgalāḥ |
yatkurvanti tadutpannaṃ madhu chāttrakamīritam || 14.120
makṣikāstīkṣṇatuṇḍā yāstathā ṣaṭpadasaṃnibhāḥ |
tadudbhūtaṃ yadarghārhaṃ tadārghyaṃ madhu varṇyate || 14.121
auddālāḥ kapilāḥ kīṭā bhūmeruddalanāḥ smṛtāḥ |
valmīkāntastadutpannamauddālakamudīryate || 14.122
indranīladalākārāḥ sūkṣmāścinvanti makṣikāḥ |
yadvṛkṣakoṭarāntasthaṃ madhu dālamidaṃ smṛtam || 14.123
ityetasyāṣṭadhā bhedairutpattiḥ kathitā kramāt |
atha vakṣyāmyahaṃ teṣāṃ varṇavīryādikaṃ kramāt || 14.124
mākṣikaṃ tailavarṇaṃ syātśvetaṃ bhrāmaramucyate |
kṣaudraṃ tu kapilābhāsaṃ pauttikaṃ ghṛtasaṃnibham || 14.125
āpītavarṇaṃ chāttraṃ syātpiṅgaṃ cārghyanāmakam |
auddālaṃ svarṇasadṛśamāpītaṃ dālamucyate || 14.126
mākṣikaṃ madhuraṃ rūkṣaṃ laghu śvāsādidoṣanut |
bhrāmaraṃ picchilaṃ rūkṣaṃ madhuraṃ mukhajāḍyajit || 14.127
kṣaudraṃ tu śītaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ picchilaṃ pittavātahṛt |
pauttikaṃ madhu rūkṣoṣṇamasrapittādidāhakṛt || 14.128
śvitramehakrimighnaṃ ca vidyācchāttraṃ guṇottaram |
ārghyamadhvaticakṣuṣyaṃ kaphapittādidoṣahṛt || 14.129
auddālakaṃ tu kuṣṭhādidoṣaghnaṃ sarvasiddhidam |
dālaṃ kaṭu kaṣāyāmlaṃ madhuraṃ pittadāyi ca || 14.130
navaṃ madhu bhavetsthaulyaṃ nātiśleṣmakaraṃ param |
dehasthaulyāpahaṃ grāhi purāṇaṃ madhu lekhanam || 14.131
pakvaṃ doṣatrayaghnaṃ madhu vividharujājāḍyajihvāmayādidhvaṃsaṃ dhatte ca rucyaṃ balamatidhṛtidaṃ vīryavṛddhiṃ vidhatte |
āmaṃ cedāmagulmāmayapavanarujāpittadāhāsradoṣaṃ hanyādvātaṃ ca śoṣaṃ janayati niyataṃ dhvaṃsayatyantavṛddhim || 14.132
vraṇaśodhanasaṃdhāne vraṇasaṃropaṇādiṣu |
sādhāraṇyā madhu hitaṃ tattulyā madhuśarkarā || 14.133
uṣṇaiḥ sahoṣṇakāle vā svayamuṣṇamathāpi vā |
āmaṃ madhu manuṣyāṇāṃ viṣavattāpadāyakam || 14.134
kīṭakādiyutamamladūṣitaṃ yacca paryuṣitakaṃ madhu svataḥ |
kaṇṭakoṭaragataṃ ca mecakaṃ tacca gehajanitaṃ ca doṣakṛt || 14.135
daṇḍairnihatya yadupāttamapāstadaṃśaṃ tādṛgvidhaṃ madhu rasāyanayogayogyam |
hikkāgudāṅkuraviśophakaphavraṇādidoṣāpahaṃ bhavati doṣadamanyathā cet || 14.136
mādhvī sitā madhūtpannā madhujā madhuśarkarā |
mākṣīkaśarkarā caiṣā kṣaudrajā kṣaudraśarkarā || 14.137
yadguṇaṃ yanmadhu proktaṃ tadguṇā tasya śarkarā |
viśeṣādbalavṛṣyaṃ ca tarpaṇaṃ kṣīṇadehinām || 14.138
madyaṃ surā prasannā syānmadirā vāruṇī varā |
mattā kādambarī śītā capalā kāminī priyā || 14.139
madagandhā ca mādhvīkaṃ madhu saṃdhānamāsavaḥ |
parisṛtāmṛtā vīrā medhāvī madanī ca sā || 14.140
supratibhā manojñā ca vipānā modinī tathā |
hālāhalaguṇāriṣṭaṃ sarako'tha madhūlikā |
madotkaṭā mahānandā dvātriṃśadabhidhāḥ kramāt || 14.141
madyaṃ sumadhurāmlaṃ ca kaphamārutanāśanam |
baladīptikaraṃ hṛdyaṃ sarametanmadāvaham || 14.142
syāddhātakīrasaguḍādikṛtā tu gauḍī puṣpadravādimadhusāramayī tu mādhvī |
paiṣṭī punarvividhadhānyavikārajātā khyātā madādhikatayātra ca pūrvapūrvā || 14.143
tālādirasaniryāsaiḥ saindhīṃ hālāṃ surāṃ jaguḥ |
nānādravyakadambena madyaṃ kādambaraṃ smṛtam |
saindhī kādambarī caiva dvividhaṃ madyalakṣaṇam || 14.144
gauḍī tīkṣṇoṣṇamadhurā vātahṛtpittakāriṇī |
balakṛddīpanī pathyā kāntikṛttarpaṇī parā || 14.145
mādhvī tu madhurā hṛdyā nātyuṣṇā pittavātahṛt |
pāṇḍukāmalagulmārśaḥpramehaśamanī parā || 14.146
paiṣṭī kaṭūṣṇā tīkṣṇā syānmadhurā dīpanī parā || 14.147
saindhī śītā kaṣāyāmlā pittahṛdvātadā ca sā || 14.148
gauḍī tu śiśire peyā paiṣṭī hemantavarṣayoḥ |
śaradgrīṣmavasanteṣu mādhvī grāhyā na cānyathā || 14.149
kādambarīśarkarajādi madyaṃ suśītalaṃ vṛṣyakaraṃ madāḍhyam |
mādhvīsamaṃ syāttṛṇavṛkṣajātaṃ madyaṃ suśītaṃ guru tarpaṇaṃ ca || 14.150
sarveṣāṃ tṛṇavṛkṣāṇāṃ niryāsaṃ śītalaṃ guru |
mohanaṃ balakṛddhṛdyaṃ tṛṣṇāsaṃtāpanāśanam || 14.151
aikṣavaṃ tu bhavenmadyaṃ śiśiraṃ ca madotkaṭam |
yavadhānyakṛtaṃ madyaṃ guru viṣṭambhadāyakam || 14.152
śarkarādhātakītoye kṛtaṃ śītaṃ manoharam |
śārkaraṃ kathyate madyaṃ vṛṣyaṃ dīpanamohanam || 14.153
anye dvādaśadhā madyabhedānāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ |
ukteṣvantarbhavantīti nānyaistu pṛthagīritāḥ || 14.154
madyaṃ navaṃ sarvavikārahetuḥ sarvaṃ tu vātādikadoṣadāyi |
jīrṇaṃ tu sarvaṃ sakalāmayaghnaṃ balapradaṃ vṛṣyakaraṃ ca dīpanam || 14.155
madyaprayogaṃ kurvanti śūdrādiṣu mahārtiṣu |
dvijaistribhistu na grāhyaṃ yadyapyujjīvayenmṛtam || 14.156
itthaṃ vārdhinadīnadahradasaraḥkulyāditīrāntaraprakrāntekṣuguḍādimākṣikabhidāmadyaprabhedānapi |
prāgasmātpratibudhya nāmaguṇato nirṇītayogaucitī yāthātathyavaśādviniścitamanāḥ kurvīta vaidyaḥ kriyām || 14.157
yai rasyamānā hi nṛṇāṃ yathāsvaṃ doṣānnirasyantyapi durnirāsān |
teṣāṃ rasānāṃ vasatiḥ kilāyaṃ vargaḥ prasiddho rasavarganāmnā || 14.158
nisyandaṃ dugdhasindhāvamṛtamatha samastauṣadhīnāṃ na dohaṃ tāpāhaṃ no cikitsāmabhilaṣati rasaṃ nāpi doṣākarasya |
labdhvā yatsauhṛdayyaṃ jagati budhajanastena vargaḥ kṛto'smin pānīyādiḥ prasiddhiṃ vrajati manumito nāmagīrmauliratne || 14.159


rājanighaṇṭu, Kṣīrādivarga
kṣīraṃ pīyūṣamūdhasyaṃ dugdhaṃ stanyaṃ payo'mṛtam || 15.1
kṣīrajaṃ dadhi tadrūpyaṃ viralaṃ mastu tajjalam || 15.2
dadhijaṃ navanītaṃ syātsāro haiyaṃgavīnakam || 15.3
ghṛtamājyaṃ haviḥ sarpiḥ pavitraṃ navanītajam |
amṛtaṃ cābhighāraśca homyamāyuśca taijasam || 15.4
mrakṣaṇaṃ snehanaṃ snehaḥ snigdhatā mrakṣa eva ca |
abhyaṅgo'bhyañjanaṃ caiva copaḍaśca ghṛtādikaḥ || 15.5
takraṃ gorasajaṃ gholaṃ kālaśeyaviloḍitam |
daṇḍāhatamariṣṭo'mlamudaśvinmathitaṃ dravaḥ || 15.6
takraṃ tribhāgadadhisaṃyutamambu dhīrairuktaṃ dadhidviguṇavāriyutaṃ tu mastu |
dadhyambhasī yadi same tadudaśvidāhustatkevalaṃ tu mathitaṃ munayo vadanti || 15.7
gomūtraṃ gojalaṃ gombho goniṣyandaśca godravaḥ |
gomayaṃ gopurīṣaṃ syādgoviṣṭhā gomalaṃ ca tat || 15.8
gomahiṣīchāgalāvikagajaturagakharoṣṭramānuṣastrīṇām |
kṣīrādikaguṇadoṣau vakṣye kramato yathāyogam || 15.9
gavyaṃ kṣīraṃ pathyamatyantarucyaṃ svādu snigdhaṃ pittavātāmayaghnam |
kāntiprajñābuddhimedhāṅgapuṣṭiṃ dhatte spaṣṭaṃ vīryavṛddhiṃ vidhatte || 15.10
gaulyaṃ tu mahiṣīkṣīraṃ vipāke śītalaṃ guru |
balapuṣṭipradaṃ vṛṣyaṃ pittadāhāsranāśanam || 15.11
ajānāṃ laghukāyatvānnānādravyaniṣevaṇāt |
nātyambupānādvyāyāmātsarvavyādhiharaṃ payaḥ || 15.12
sūkṣmājadugdheti ca saṃvadārhaṃ godugdhavīryāttvadhikaṃ guṇe ca |
sukṣīṇadeheṣu ca pathyamuktaṃ sthūlājadugdhaṃ kila kiṃcidūnam || 15.13
āvikaṃ tu payaḥ snigdhaṃ kaphapittaharaṃ param |
sthaulyamehaharaṃ pathyaṃ lomaśaṃ guru vṛddhidam || 15.14
madhuraṃ hastinīkṣīraṃ vṛṣyaṃ guru kaṣāyakam |
snigdhaṃ sthairyakaraṃ śītaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ balavardhanam || 15.15
aśvīkṣīraṃ tu rūkṣāmlaṃ lavaṇaṃ dīpanaṃ laghu |
dehasthairyakaraṃ balyaṃ gauravakāntikṛtparam || 15.16
balakṛdgardabhīkṣīraṃ vātaśvāsaharaṃ param |
madhurāmlarasaṃ rūkṣaṃ dīpanaṃ pathyadaṃ smṛtam || 15.17
uṣṭrīkṣīraṃ kuṣṭhaśophāpahaṃ tatpittārśoghnaṃ tatkaphāṭopahāri |
ānāhārtijantugulmodarākhyaṃ śvāsollāsaṃ nāśayatyāśu pītam || 15.18
madhuraṃ mānuṣīkṣīraṃ kaṣāyaṃ ca himaṃ laghu |
cakṣuṣyaṃ dīpanaṃ pathyaṃ pācanaṃ rocanaṃ ca tat || 15.19
kṣīraṃ kāsaśvāsakopāya sarvaṃ gurvāmaṃ syātprāyaśo doṣadāyi |
taccettaptaṃ vartitaṃ pathyamuktaṃ nārīkṣīraṃ tvāmamevāmayaghnam || 15.20
uktaṃ gavyādikaṃ dugdhaṃ dhāroṣṇamamṛtopamam |
sarvāmayaharaṃ pathyaṃ cirasaṃsthaṃ tu doṣadam || 15.21
ke'pyāvikaṃ pathyatamaṃ śṛtoṣṇaṃ kṣīraṃ tvajānāṃ śṛtaśītamāhuḥ |
dohāntaśītaṃ mahiṣīpayaśca gavyaṃ tu dhāroṣṇamidaṃ praśastam || 15.22
vṛṣyaṃ bṛṃhaṇamagnivardhanakaraṃ pūrvāhṇapītaṃ payo madhyāhne baladāyakaṃ kaphaharaṃ kṛcchrasya vicchedakam |
bālye vahnikaraṃ tato balakaraṃ vīryapradaṃ vārddhake rātrau kṣīramanekadoṣaśamanaṃ sevyaṃ tataḥ sarvadā || 15.23
kṣīraṃ muhūrtatritayoṣitaṃ yadataptametadvikṛtiṃ prayāti |
uṣṇaṃ tu doṣaṃ kurute tadūrdhvaṃ viṣopamaṃ syāduṣitaṃ daśānām || 15.24
jīrṇajvare kaphe kṣīṇe kṣīraṃ syādamṛtopamam |
tadeva taruṇe pītaṃ viṣavaddhanti mānuṣam || 15.25
caturthabhāgaṃ salilaṃ nidhāya yatnādyadāvartitamuttamaṃ tat |
sarvāmayaghnaṃ balapuṣṭikāri vīryapradaṃ kṣīramatipraśastam || 15.26
gavyaṃ pūrvāhṇakāle syādaparāhṇe tu māhiṣam |
kṣīraṃ saśarkaraṃ pathyaṃ yadvā sātmye ca sarvadā || 15.27
pittaghnaṃ śṛtaśītalaṃ kaphaharaṃ pakvaṃ taduṣṇaṃ bhavecchītaṃ yattu na pācitaṃ tadakhilaṃ viṣṭambhadoṣapradam |
dhāroṣṇaṃ tvamṛtaṃ payaḥ śramaharaṃ nidrākaraṃ kāntidaṃ vṛṣyaṃ bṛṃhaṇamagnivardhanamatisvādu tridoṣāpaham || 15.28
kṣīraṃ na yuñjīta kadāpyataptaṃ taptaṃ na caitallavaṇena sārdham |
piṣṭānnasaṃdhānakamāṣamudgakośātakīkandaphalādikaiśca || 15.29
matsyamāṃsaguḍamudgamūlakaiḥ kuṣṭhamāvahati sevitaṃ payaḥ |
śākajāmbavarasaistu sevitaṃ mārayatyabudhamāśu sarpavat || 15.30
snigdhaṃ śītaṃ guru kṣīraṃ sarvakālaṃ na sevayet |
dīptāgniṃ kurute mandaṃ mandāgniṃ naṣṭameva ca || 15.31
nityaṃ tīvrāgninā sevyaṃ supakvaṃ māhiṣaṃ payaḥ |
puṣṇanti dhātavaḥ sarve balapuṣṭivivardhanam || 15.32
kṣīraṃ gavājakādermadhuraṃ kṣāraṃ navaprasūtānām |
rūkṣañca pittadāhaṃ karoti raktāmayaṃ kurute || 15.33
madhuraṃ tridoṣaśamanaṃ kṣīraṃ madhyaprasūtānām |
lavaṇaṃ madhuraṃ kṣīraṃ vidāhajananaṃ ciraprasūtānām || 15.34
guṇahīnaṃ niḥsāraṃ kṣīraṃ prathamaprasūtānām |
madhyavayasāṃ rasāyanamuktamidaṃ durbalaṃ tu vṛddhānām || 15.35
tāsāṃ māsatrayādūrddhvaṃ gurviṇīnāñca yatpayaḥ |
taddāhi lavaṇaṃ kṣīraṃ madhuraṃ pittadoṣakṛt || 15.36
gavādīnāṃ varṇabhedādguṇā dugdhādike pṛthak |
kaiścidukto viśeṣācca viśeṣo deśabhedataḥ || 15.37
deśeṣu deśeṣu ca teṣu teṣu tṛṇāmbunī yādṛśadoṣayukte |
tatsevanādeva gavādikānāṃ guṇādi dugdhādiṣu tādṛśaṃ matam || 15.38
śītaṃ snigdhaṃ gururgaulyaṃ vṛṣyaṃ pittāpahaṃ param |
jñeyā caivābhidhā tasya kīlāṭaṃ tu payaḥcchadaḥ || 15.39
dadhi gavyamatipavitraṃ śītaṃ snigdhaṃ ca dīpanaṃ balakṛt |
madhuramarocakahāri grāhi ca vātāmayaghnaṃ ca || 15.40
māhiṣaṃ madhuraṃ snigdhaṃ śleṣmakṛdraktapittajit |
balāsravardhanaṃ vṛṣyaṃ śramaghnaṃ śodhanaṃ dadhi || 15.41
dadhyājaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ laghūṣṇaṃ netradoṣanut |
durnāmaśvāsakāsaghnaṃ rucyaṃ dīpanapācanam || 15.42
āvikaṃ dadhi susnigdhaṃ kaphapittakaraṃ guru |
vāte ca raktavāte ca pathyaṃ śophavraṇāpaham || 15.43
hastinīdadhi kaṣāyalaghūṣṇaṃ paktiśūlaśamanaṃ rucipradam |
dīptidaṃ khalu balāsagadaghnaṃ vīryavardhanabalapradamuktam || 15.44
aśvīdadhi syānmadhuraṃ kaṣāyaṃ kaphārtimūrchāmayahāri rūkṣam |
vātālpadaṃ dīpanakāri netradoṣāpahaṃ tatkathitaṃ pṛthivyām || 15.45
gardabhīdadhi rūkṣoṣṇaṃ laghu dīpanapācanam |
madhurāmlarasaṃ rucyaṃ vātadoṣavināśanam || 15.46
auṣṭramarśāṃsi kuṣṭhāni krimiśūlodarāṇi ca |
nihanti kaṭukaṃ svādu kiṃcidamlarasaṃ dadhi || 15.47
vipāke madhuraṃ balyamamlaṃ saṃtarpaṇaṃ guru |
cakṣuṣyaṃ grahadoṣaghnaṃ dadhi strīstanyasambhavam || 15.48
dadhyamlaṃ guru vātadoṣaśamanaṃ saṃgrāhi mūtrāvahaṃ balyaṃ śophakaraṃ ca rucyaśamanaṃ vahneśca śāntipradam |
kāsaśvāsasupīnaseṣu viṣame śītajvare syāddhitaṃ raktodrekakaraṃ karoti satataṃ śukrasya vṛddhiṃ parām || 15.49
dadhi madhuramīṣadamlaṃ madhurāmlaṃ vā hitaṃ na cātyuṣṇam |
yāvadyāvanmadhuraṃ doṣaharaṃ tāvaduktamidam || 15.50
lavaṇamaricasarpiḥśarkarāmudgadhātrīkusumarasavihīnaṃ naitadaśnanti nityam |
na ca śaradi vasante noṣṇakāle na rātrau na dadhi kaphavikāre pittadoṣe'pi nādyāt || 15.51
trikaṭukayutametadrājikācūrṇamiśraṃ kaphaharamanilaghnaṃ vahnisaṃdhukṣaṇaṃ ca |
tuhinaśiśirakāle sevitaṃ cātipathyaṃ racayati tanudārḍhyaṃ kāntimattvaṃ ca nṝṇām || 15.52
uṣṇāmlaṃ rucipaktidaṃ klamaharaṃ balyaṃ kaṣāyaṃ saraṃ bhukticchedakaraṃ tṛṣodaragadaplīhārśasāṃ nāśanam |
srotaḥśuddhikaraṃ kaphānilaharaṃ viṣṭambhaśūlāpahaṃ pāṇḍuśvāsavikāragulmaśamanaṃ mastu praśastaṃ laghu || 15.53
uktaṃ śleṣmasamīrahāri mathitaṃ tatśleṣmapittāpahaṃ rucyaṃ prāhurudaśvidākhyamadhikaṃ takraṃ tridoṣāpaham |
mandāgnāvarucau vidāhaviṣamaśvāsārtikāsādiṣu śreṣṭhaṃ pathyatamaṃ vadanti sudhiyastakratrayaṃ hyuttamam || 15.54
takraṃ tridoṣaśamanaṃ rucidīpanīyaṃ rucyaṃ vamiśramaharaṃ klamahāri mastu |
balyapradaṃ pavananāśamudaśvidākhyaṃ śastaṃ kaphaśramamarudvamaneṣu gholam || 15.55
amlena vātaṃ madhureṇa pittaṃ kaphaṃ kaṣāyeṇa nihanti sadyaḥ |
yathā surāṇāmamṛtaṃ hitāya tathā narāṇāmiha takramāhuḥ || 15.56
āmātisāre ca viṣūcikāyāṃ vātajvare pāṇḍuṣu kāmaleṣu |
pramehagulmodaravātaśūle nityaṃ pibettakramarocake ca || 15.57
śītakāle'gnimāndye ca kaphe pāṇḍvāmayeṣu ca |
mārgoparodhe kuṣṭhādivyādhau takraṃ praśasyate || 15.58
vātodarī pibettakraṃ pippalīlavaṇānvitam |
śarkarāmaricopetaṃ svādu pittodarī pibet || 15.59
yavānīsaindhavājājīvyoṣayuktaṃ kaphodare |
saṃnipātodare takraṃ trikaṭukṣārasaindhavam || 15.60
takraṃ dadyānno kṣate noṣṇakāle no daurbalye no tṛṣāmūrchite ca |
naiva bhrāntau naiva pittāsradoṣe naitaddadyātsūtikāyāṃ viśeṣāt || 15.61
takraṃ snehānvitaṃ tundanidrājāḍyapradaṃ guru |
ardhāvaśiṣṭaṃ sāmānyaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ laghu pathyadam || 15.62
śītaṃ varṇabalāvahaṃ sumadhuraṃ vṛṣyaṃ ca saṃgrāhakaṃ vātaghnaṃ kaphahārakaṃ rucikaraṃ sarvāṅgaśūlāpaham |
kāsaghnaṃ śramanāśanaṃ sukhakaraṃ kāntipradaṃ puṣṭidaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ navanītamuddhṛtanavaṃ goḥ sarvadoṣāpaham || 15.63
gavyaṃ ca māhiṣaṃ cāpi navanītaṃ navodbhavam |
śasyate bālavṛddhānāṃ balakṛtpuṣṭivardhanam || 15.64
māhiṣaṃ navanītaṃ tu kaṣāyaṃ madhuraṃ rase |
śītaṃ vṛṣyapradaṃ grāhi pittaghnaṃ tu balapradam || 15.65
laghvājaṃ tu madhuraṃ kaṣāyaṃ ca tridoṣanut |
cakṣuṣyaṃ dīpanaṃ balyaṃ navanītaṃ hitaṃ sadā || 15.66
navanītaṃ navotthaṃ tu chāgajaṃ kṣayakāsajit |
balyaṃ netrāmayaghnaṃ ca kaphaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ param || 15.67
āvikaṃ navanītaṃ tu vipāke tu himaṃ laghu |
yoniśūle kaphe vāte durnāmni ca hitaṃ sadā || 15.68
aiḍakaṃ navanītaṃ tu kaṣāyaṃ śītalaṃ laghu |
medhāhṛdguru puṣṭyaṃ ca sthaulyaṃ mandāgnidīpanam || 15.69
hastinīnavanītaṃ tu kaṣāyaṃ śītalaṃ laghu |
tiktaṃ viṣṭambhi jantughnaṃ hanti pittakaphakrimīn || 15.70
aśvīyaṃ navanītaṃ syātkaṣāyaṃ kaphavātajit |
cakṣuṣyaṃ kaṭukaṃ coṣṇamīṣadvātāpahārakam || 15.71
gardabhīnavanītaṃ tu kaṣāyaṃ kaphavātanut |
balyaṃ dīpanadaṃ pāke laghūṣṇaṃ mūtradoṣanut || 15.72
auṣṭraṃ tu navanītaṃ syādvipāke laghu śītalam |
vraṇakrimikaphāsraghnaṃ vātaghnaṃ viṣanāśanam || 15.73
navanītaṃ tu nārīṇāṃ rucyaṃ pāke laghu smṛtam |
cakṣuṣyaṃ sarvarogaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ viṣanāśanam || 15.74
śītaṃ rucyanavoddhṛtaṃ sumadhuraṃ vṛṣyaṃ ca vātāpahaṃ kāsaghnaṃ krimināśanaṃ kaphakaraṃ saṃgrāhi śūlāpaham |
balyaṃ puṣṭikaraṃ tṛṣārtiśamanaṃ saṃtāpavicchedanaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ śramahāri tarpaṇakaraṃ dadhyudbhavaṃ pittajit || 15.75
ekāhādyuṣitaṃ proktamuttarottaragandhadam |
ahṛdyaṃ sarvarogāḍhyaṃ dadhijaṃ tadghṛtaṃ smṛtam || 15.76
dhīkāntismṛtidāyakaṃ balakaraṃ medhāpradaṃ puṣṭikṛtvātaśleṣmaharaṃ śramopaśamanaṃ pittāpahaṃ hṛdyadam |
vahnervṛddhikaraṃ vipākamadhuraṃ vṛṣyaṃ vapuḥsthairyadaṃ gavyaṃ havyatamaṃ ghṛtaṃ bahuguṇaṃ bhogyaṃ bhavedbhāgyataḥ || 15.77
sarpirmāhiṣamuttamaṃ dhṛtikaraṃ saukhyapradaṃ kāntikṛtvātaśleṣmanibarhaṇaṃ balakaraṃ varṇapradāne kṣamam |
durnāmagrahaṇīvikāraśamanaṃ mandānaloddīpanaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ navagavyataḥ paramidaṃ hṛdyaṃ manohāri ca || 15.78
ājamājyaṃ tu cakṣuṣyaṃ dīpanaṃ balavardhanam |
kāsaśvāsakaphāntakaṃ rājayakṣmasu śasyate || 15.79
pāke laghvāvikaṃ sarpirnavaṃ pittaprakopaṇam |
yonidoṣe kaphe vāte śophe kampe ca taddhitam || 15.80
aiḍakaṃ ghṛtamatīva gauravādvarjyamiva sukumāradehinām |
buddhipāṭavakaraṃ balāvahaṃ sevitaṃ ca kurute nṛṇāṃ vapuḥ || 15.81
nihanti hastinīsarpiḥ kaphapittaviṣakrimīn |
kaṣāyaṃ laghu viṣṭambhi tiktaṃ cāgnikaraṃ param || 15.82
aśvīsarpistu kaṭukaṃ madhuraṃ ca kaṣāyakam |
īṣaddīpanadaṃ mūrchāhāri vātālpadaṃ guru || 15.83
ghṛtaṃ gārdabhikaṃ balyaṃ dīpanaṃ mūtradoṣanut |
pāke laghūṣṇavīryaṃ ca kaṣāyaṃ kaphanāśanam || 15.84
ghṛtamauṣṭraṃ tu madhuraṃ vipāke kaṭuśītalam |
kuṣṭhakrimiharaṃ vātakaphagulmodarāpaham || 15.85
nārīsarpistu cakṣuṣyaṃ pathyaṃ sarvāmayāpaham |
mandāgnidīpanaṃ rucyaṃ pāke laghu viṣāpaham || 15.86
madāpasmāramūrchādiśiraḥkarṇākṣijā rujaḥ |
sarpiḥ purāṇaṃ jayati vraṇaśodhanaropaṇam || 15.87
āyurvṛddhiṃ vapuṣi dṛḍhatāṃ saukumāryaṃ ca kāntiṃ buddhiṃ dhatte smṛtibalakaraṃ śītavidhvaṃsanaṃ ca |
pathyaṃ bālye vayasi taruṇe vārddhake cātibalyaṃ nānyatkiṃcijjagati guṇadaṃ sarpiṣaḥ pathyamasti || 15.88
kāñjikaṃ kāñjikā vīraṃ kulmāṣābhibhavaṃ tathā |
avantisomaṃ dhānyāmlamāranālo'mlasārakaḥ || 15.89
kāñjikaṃ vātaśophaghnaṃ pittaghnaṃ jvaranāśanam |
dāhamūrchāśramaghnaṃ ca śūlādhmānavibandhanut || 15.90
kāñjikaṃ kāñjitailaṃ ca palitaṃ vātakārakam |
dāhakaṃ gātraśaithilyaṃ mardanānna ca bhakṣaṇāt || 15.91
cukraṃ sahasravedhaṃ ca rasāmlaṃ cukravedhakam |
śākhāmlabhedanaṃ caivamamlasāraṃ ca cukrikā || 15.92
cukraṃ tiktāmlakaṃ svādu kaphapittavināśanam |
nāsikāgadadurgandhaśirorogaharaṃ param || 15.93
sauvīrakaṃ suvīrāmlaṃ jñeyaṃ godhūmasambhavam |
yavāmlajaṃ yavotthaṃ ca tuṣotthaṃ ca tuṣodakam || 15.94
sauvīrakaṃ cāmlarasaṃ keśyaṃ mastakadoṣajit |
jarāśaithilyaharaṇaṃ balasaṃtarpaṇaṃ param || 15.95
taṇḍulotthaṃ taṇḍulāmbu kaṣāyaṃ madhuraṃ laghu |
saṃgrāhi viṣaviccharditṛḍdāhavraṇanāśakṛt |
tuṣāmbu dīpanaṃ hṛdyaṃ hṛtpāṇḍukṛmiroganut || 15.96
annodajaḥ śivarasastryahātparyuṣite rase |
dīpano madhurāmlastu dāhajillaghutarpaṇaḥ || 15.97
gomūtraṃ kaṭutiktoṣṇaṃ kaphavātaharaṃ laghu |
pittakṛddīpanaṃ medhyaṃ tvagdoṣaghnaṃ matipradam || 15.98
māhiṣaṃ mūtramānāhaśophagulmākṣidoṣanut |
kaṭūṣṇaṃ kuṣṭhakaṇḍūtiśūlodararujāpaham || 15.99
ajāmūtraṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca rūkṣaṃ nāḍīviṣārtijit |
plīhodarakaphaśvāsagulmaśophaharaṃ laghu || 15.100
āvikaṃ tiktakaṭukaṃ mūtramuṣṇaṃ ca kuṣṭhajit |
durnāmodaraśūlāsraśophamehaviṣāpaham || 15.101
hastimūtraṃ tu tiktoṣṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ vātabhūtanut |
tiktaṃ kaṣāyaṃ śūlaghnaṃ hikkāśvāsaharaṃ param || 15.102
aśvamūtraṃ tu tiktoṣṇaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca viṣadoṣajit |
vātaprakopaśamanaṃ pittakāri pradīpanam || 15.103
kharamūtraṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca kṣāraṃ tīkṣṇaṃ kaphāpaham |
mahāvātāpahaṃ bhūtakamponmādaharaṃ param || 15.104
auṣṭrakaṃ kaṭu tiktoṣṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ pittakopanam |
balyaṃ jaṭhararogaghnaṃ vātadoṣavināśanam || 15.105
mānuṣaṃ mūtramāmaghnaṃ krimivraṇaviṣārtinut |
tiktoṣṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ bhūtatvagdoṣavātajit || 15.106
śūlagulmodarānāhavātavicchardanādiṣu |
mūtraprayogasādhyeṣu gomūtraṃ kalpayedbudhaḥ || 15.107
tailaṃ yattilasarṣapoditakusumbhotthātasīdhānyajaṃ yaccairaṇḍakarañjakeṅgudīphalairnimbākṣaśigrvasthibhiḥ |
jyotiṣmatyabhayodbhavaṃ madhurikākośāmraciñcābhavaṃ karpūratrapusādijaṃ ca sakalaṃ siddhyai kramātkathyate || 15.108
tilatailamalaṃkaroti keśaṃ madhuraṃ tiktakaṣāyamuṣṇatīkṣṇam |
balakṛtkaphavātajantukharjūvraṇakaṇḍūtiharaṃ ca kāntidāyi || 15.109
sarṣapatailaṃ tiktaṃ kaṭukoṣṇaṃ vātakaphavikāraghnam |
pittāsradoṣadaṃ krimikuṣṭhaghnaṃ tilajavacca cakṣuṣyam || 15.110
kusumbhatailaṃ krimihāri tejobalāvahaṃ yakṣmamalāpahaṃ ca |
tridoṣakṛtpuṣṭibalakṣayaṃ ca karoti kaṇḍūṃ ca karoti dṛṣṭeḥ || 15.111
madhuraṃ tvatasītailaṃ picchilaṃ cānilāpaham |
madagandhi kaṣāyaṃ ca kaphakāsāpahārakam || 15.112
godhūmayāvanālavrīhiyavādyakhiladhānyajaṃ tailam |
vātakaphapittaśamanaṃ kaṇḍūkuṣṭhādihāri cakṣuṣyam || 15.113
eraṇḍatailaṃ kṛmidoṣanāśanaṃ vātāmayaghnaṃ sakalāṅgaśūlahṛt |
kuṣṭhāpahaṃ svādu rasāyanottamaṃ pittaprakopaṃ kurute'tidīpanam || 15.114
karañjatailaṃ nayanārtināśanaṃ vātāmayadhvaṃsanamuṣṇatīkṣṇakam |
kuṣṭhārtikaṇḍūtivicarcikāpahaṃ lepena nānāvidhacarmadoṣanut || 15.115
snigdhaṃ syādiṅgudītailaṃ madhuraṃ pittanāśanam |
śītalaṃ kāntidaṃ balyaṃ śleṣmalaṃ keśavardhanam || 15.116
nimbatailaṃ tu nātyuṣṇaṃ krimikuṣṭhakaphāpaham || 15.117
ākṣaṃ svādu himaṃ keśyaṃ guru pittānilāpaham || 15.118
śigrutailaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca vātajitkaphanāśanam |
tvagdoṣavraṇakaṇḍūtiśophahāri ca picchilam || 15.119
kaṭu jyotiṣmatītailaṃ tiktoṣṇaṃ vātanāśanam |
pittasaṃtāpanaṃ medhāprajñābuddhivivardhanam || 15.120
śītaṃ harītakītailaṃ kaṣāyaṃ madhuraṃ kaṭu |
sarvavyādhiharaṃ pathyaṃ nānātvagdoṣanāśanam || 15.121
tīkṣṇaṃ tu rājikātailaṃ jñeyaṃ vātādidoṣanut |
śiśiraṃ kaṭu puṃstvaghnaṃ keśyaṃ tvagdoṣanāśanam || 15.122
saraṃ kośāmrajaṃ tailaṃ krimikuṣṭhavraṇāpaham |
tiktāmlamadhuraṃ balyaṃ pathyaṃ rocanapācanam || 15.123
yacca ciñcībhavaṃ tailaṃ kaṭu pāke vilekhanam |
kaphavātaharaṃ rucyaṃ kaṣāyaṃ nātiśītalam || 15.124
karpūratailahimatailasitāṃśutailaśītābhratailatuhināṃśusudhāṃśutailam |
karpūratailaṃ kaṭukoṣṇakaphāmahāri vātāmayaghnaradadārḍhyadapittahāri || 15.125
trapusairvārukacārakakuṣmāṇḍaprabhṛtibījajaṃ ca yattailam |
tanmadhuraṃ guru śiśiraṃ keśyaṃ kaphapittanāśi kāntikaram || 15.126
tailaṃ na sevayeddhīmān yasya kasya ca yadbhavet |
viṣasāmyaguṇatvācca yoge tanna prayojayet || 15.127
viṣasya tailasya na kiṃcidantaraṃ mṛtasya suptasya na kiṃcidantaram |
tṛṇasya dāsasya na kiṃcidantaraṃ mūrkhasya kāṣṭhasya na kiṃcidantaram || 15.128
itthaṃ gavādikapayaḥprabhṛtiprapañcaprastāvavarṇitatilādikatailajātam |
vargaṃ nisargalalitojjvalaśabdasargaṃ buddhvā bhiṣakpatiraśaṅkatayā bhiṣajyet || 15.129
pātāramātmanaḥ kila yānti pratyupacikīrṣayā yāni |
teṣāmeva nivāsaḥ parikathitaḥ peyavarga iti kṛtibhiḥ || 15.130
pāyaṃ pāyaṃ madhuravimalāṃ śītalāṃ yasya kīrtisrotodhārāṃ jahati sujanā durjanāsaṃgadausthyam |
vargastasya vrajati nṛharernāmanirmāṇanāmnaścūḍāratne khalu tithimitaḥ kṣīrakādiḥ samāptim || 15.131


rājanighaṇṭu, śālyādivarga
dhānyaṃ bhogyaṃ ca bhogārhamannādyaṃ jīvasādhanam |
tacca tāvattridhā jñeyaṃ śūkaśimbītṛṇāhvayam || 16.1
vrīhyādikaṃ yadiha śūkasamanvitaṃ syāttacchūkadhānyamatha mudgamakuṣṭakādi |
śimbīnigūḍhamiti tatpravadanti śimbīdhānyaṃ tṛṇodbhavatayā tṛṇadhānyamanyat || 16.2
vātādidoṣaśamanaṃ laghu śūkadhānyaṃ tejobalātiśayavīryavivṛddhidāyi |
śimbībhavaṃ guru himaṃ ca vibandhadāyi vātūlakaṃ tu śiśiraṃ tṛṇadhānyamāhuḥ || 16.3
deśe deśe śūkadhānyeṣu saṃkhyā jñātuṃ śakyā naiva taddaivatairvā |
tasmādeṣāṃ yeṣu bhogopayogāstānyasmābhirvyākriyante kiyanti || 16.4
śālayaḥ kalamā rucyā vrīhiśreṣṭhā nṛpapriyāḥ |
dhānyottamāśca vijñeyāḥ kaidārāḥ sukumārakāḥ || 16.5
rājānnaṣaṣṭikasitatararaktamuṇḍasthūlāṇugandhanirapādikaśālisaṃjñāḥ |
vrīhistatheti daśadhā bhuvi śālayastu teṣāṃ krameṇa guṇanāmagaṇaṃ bravīmi || 16.6
rājānnaṃ dīrghaśūkaḥ kharipudivasajaṃ ṣaṣṭiko varṇato dvau niḥśūko muṇḍaśāliḥ svaguṇaviśaditaḥ svābhidhānāstrayo'nye |
māsairyo'nyastribhiḥ syātsa bhavati nirapo yo'pi vṛṣṭyambusambhūreṣa syādvrīhisaṃjñastaditi daśavidhāḥ śālayastu prasiddhāḥ || 16.7
śālirnṛpānnaṃ rājānnaṃ rājārhaṃ dīrghaśūkakam |
dhānyaśreṣṭhaṃ rājadhānyaṃ rājeṣṭaṃ dīrghakūrakam || 16.8
rājānnaṃ tu tridoṣaghnaṃ susnigdhaṃ madhuraṃ laghu |
dīpanaṃ balakṛtpathyaṃ kāntidaṃ vīryavardhanam || 16.9
rājānnaṃ trividhaṃ svaśūkabhidayā jñeyaṃ sitaṃ lohitaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ceti rasādhikaṃ ca tadidaṃ syādauttarottaryataḥ |
traividhyādiha taṇḍulāśca haritāḥ śvetāstathā lohitāḥ sāmānyena bhavanti te'pyatha guṇaiḥ syuḥ pūrvapūrvottarāḥ || 16.10
ṣaṣṭikaḥ ṣaṣṭiśāliḥ syātṣaṣṭijaḥ snigdhataṇḍulaḥ |
ṣaṣṭivāsarajaḥ so'yaṃ jñeyo māsadvayodbhavaḥ || 16.11
gauro nīlaḥ ṣaṣṭiko'yaṃ dvidhā syādādyo rucyaḥ śītalo doṣahārī |
balyaḥ pathyo dīpano vīryavṛddhiṃ datte cāsmātkiṃcidūno dvitīyaḥ || 16.12
kṛṣṇaśāliḥ kālaśāliḥ śyāmaśāliḥ sitetaraḥ || 16.13
kṛṣṇaśālistridoṣaghno madhuraḥ puṣṭivardhanaḥ |
varṇakāntikaro balyo dāhajidvīryavṛddhikṛt || 16.14
raktaśālistāmraśāliḥ śoṇaśāliśca lohitaḥ |
raktaśāliḥ sumadhuro laghuḥ snigdho balāvahaḥ || 16.15
rucikṛddīpanaḥ pathyo mukhajāḍyarujāpahaḥ |
sarvāmayaharo rucyaḥ pittadāhānilāsrajit || 16.16
muṇḍaśālirmuṇḍanako niḥśūko yavaśūkajaḥ || 16.17
muṇḍaśālistridoṣaghno madhurāmlo balapradaḥ || 16.18
sthūlaśālirmahāśāliḥ sthūlāṅgaḥ sthūlataṇḍulaḥ |
evaṃgandhāḍhyaśāleśca nāmānyūhyāni sūribhiḥ || 16.19
mahāśāliḥ svādurmadhuraśiśiraḥ pittaśamano jvaraṃ jīrṇaṃ dāhaṃ jaṭhararujamahnāya śamayet |
śiśūnāṃ yūnāṃ vā yadapi jaratāṃ vā hitakaraḥ sadā sevyaḥ sarvairanalabalavīryāṇi kurute || 16.20
sūkṣmaśāliḥ sūciśāliḥ potaśāliśca sūcakaḥ || 16.21
sūkṣmaśāliḥ sumadhuro laghuḥ pittāsradāhanut |
dīpanaḥ pācanaścaiva kiṃcidvātavikārajit || 16.22
gandhaśālistu kalmāṣo gandhāluḥ kalamottamaḥ |
sugandhirgandhabahulaḥ surabhirgandhataṇḍulaḥ || 16.23
sugandhaśālirmadhuro'tivṛṣyadaḥ pittaśramāsrārucidāhaśāntidaḥ |
stanyastu garbhasthiratālpavātadaḥ puṣṭipradaścālpakaphaśca balyadaḥ || 16.24
nirapo madhuraḥ snigdhaḥ śītalo dāhapittajit |
tridoṣaśamano rucyaḥ pathyaḥ sarvāmayāpanut || 16.25
vrīhirgauro madhuraśiśiraḥ pittahārī kaṣāyaḥ snigdho vṛṣyaḥ kṛmikaphaharastāparaktāpahaśca |
puṣṭiṃ datte śramaśamanakṛdvīryavṛddhiṃ vidhatte rucyo'tyantaṃ janayati mudaṃ vātakṛnmecako'nyaḥ || 16.26
maṇḍakaḥ sthūlaśāliśca syādbimbaśālikastathā |
nijātiśāṇahulyāśca bimbī kausendukastathā || 16.27
prasādhikā jīrakākhyā saśyāmā madhurā matā |
rājānāṃ maulikasyāpi śāliḥ syādurvarī tathā || 16.28
sūkṣmaśāliḥ kuditikā suśālirguruśālayaḥ |
vanaśālirguṇḍurūkī kṣīrikā paṅktayaḥ pṛthak |
etāni śālināmāni prakhyātāni prasiddhataḥ || 16.29
aśocā pāṭalā vrīhirvrīhiko vrīhidhānyakaḥ |
vrīhisaṃdhānyamuddiṣṭaḥ ardhadhānyastu vrīhikaḥ || 16.30
garbhe pākaṇikaḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ ṣaṣṭiko balasambhavaḥ |
sudhānyaṃ pathyakārī ca mupaviḥ prajñavipriyaḥ || 16.31
śālistu kalamādyastu kalamo nākalāyakaḥ |
kadambapuṣpagandhaśca kalajātaḥ kalodbhavaḥ || 16.32
pittaśleṣmakaro vṛṣyaḥ kalamo madhurastathā || 16.33
lohito raktaśāliḥ syātkāṣṭhalohitaśālayaḥ |
ruṇālī ruṇaśālistu raktaśālyaḥ suśālyakaḥ || 16.34
tṛṣṇāghno malakṛcchraghno hṛdyastu matidāḥ pare || 16.35
mahāśāliḥ sugandhā syātsugandhā gandhasambhavā |
gandhāḍhyā gandhamālyā ca gandhānī gandhamālinī || 16.36
sugandhā madhurā hṛdyā kaphapittajvarāsrajit || 16.37
jalodbhavā jalaruhā jalajātā sujātakā |
raktāṅgulaṃ sukāraṃ ca kuṅkumaṃ samavarṇajā || 16.38
kuṅkumā madhurā śītā raktapittātisārajit || 16.39
tilajā nīlanāmā syāddīrghakṛṣṇā supūjakā |
madhurā ca sugandhā ca tilavāsī nigadyate || 16.40
rājādanī rājapriyā rājabhāvā munipriyā |
tilanī tilaparṇī ca āmagandhā pravāsinī || 16.41
kaphapittaharāḥ snigdhāḥ kāsaśvāsaharāḥ parāḥ |
śīghrapākakarā hṛdyā laghavaḥ śukravardhanāḥ || 16.42
komalāhārasambhūtāstilavāsīmahāguṇāḥ |
pāṇḍurogeṣu śūleṣu cāmavāte praśasyate || 16.43
vaktako vaktaśāliḥ syātdīrghastu āśukopitaḥ |
rājapriyā pathyakarā madhyadeśasamudbhavā || 16.44
vaktikā laghavaḥ proktā mukhapākakarāstathā || 16.45
kalāṭakaḥ kavilaḥ syādguruso garuḍaḥ smṛtaḥ |
guruvako guraḍakaḥ sukhabhojī subhojakaḥ || 16.46
kavilo gandhakārī ca laghupākakaro'pi ca |
kaphapittaharaḥ svāduḥ śūlaśvāsanivāraṇaḥ |
grahaṇīgulmakuṣṭhaghno vikalo bhojane śubhaḥ || 16.47
kuṣmāṇḍikā kumbhaḍikā raktā sumadhurā guruḥ |
sugandhā durjarā pītā sthūlataṇḍulakomalā || 16.48
kumbhikā madhurā snigdhā vātapittanibarhiṇī || 16.49
saurabhaṃ śuṇḍikaḥ śuṇḍī kausumbhī kaṭhino'phalaḥ || 16.50
kausumbhī laghupākā ca vātapittanibarhiṇī || 16.51
umpāsa umpikāśālirmadhurā gurutaṇḍulā |
bahuśūkā sugandhāḍhyā tāruṇyajanavallabhā || 16.52
umpikā madhurā snigdhā sugandhā ca kaṣāyakā |
pittaśleṣmaharā rūkṣā umpikānilanāśinī || 16.53
pakṣikaḥ pakṣilāvaṇyaḥ pakṣirājo munipriyaḥ |
sthūlataṇḍulasambhūtāgandho bahalagandhakṛt || 16.54
dagdhāyāmavanau jātāḥ śālayo laghupākinaḥ |
kiṃcitsatiktā madhurāḥ pācanā balavardhanāḥ || 16.55
kedārā madhurā vṛṣyā balyāḥ pittavivardhanāḥ |
īṣatkaṣāyālpamalā guravaḥ kaphanāśanāḥ || 16.56
śālayo ye chinnaruhā rūkṣāste baddhavarcasaḥ || 16.57
ropyātiropyā laghavaḥ śīghrapākā guṇottarāḥ |
vidāhino doṣaharā balyā mūtravivardhanāḥ || 16.58
yāvanālo yavanālaḥ śikharī vṛttataṇḍulaḥ |
dīrghanālo dīrghaśaraḥ kṣetrekṣuścekṣupattrakaḥ || 16.59
dhavalo yāvanālastu pāṇḍurastārataṇḍulaḥ |
nakṣatrākṛtivistāro vṛtto mauktikataṇḍulaḥ || 16.60
jūrṇāhvayo devadhānyaṃ jūrṇalo bījapuṣpakaḥ |
jūnalaḥ puṣpagandhaśca sugandhaḥ segurundakaḥ || 16.61
dhavalo yāvanālastu gaulyo balyastridoṣajit |
vṛṣyo ruciprado'rśoghnaḥ pathyo gulmavraṇāpahaḥ || 16.62
atha tuvarayāvanālastuvaraśca kaṣāyayāvanālaśca |
sa raktayāvanālo hitalohitastuvaradhānyaśca || 16.63
tuvaro yāvanālastu kaṣāyoṣṇo viśophakṛt |
saṃgrāhī vātaśamano vidāhī śoṣakārakaḥ || 16.64
śārado yāvanālastu śleṣmadaḥ picchilo guruḥ |
śiśiro madhuro vṛṣyo doṣaghno balapuṣṭidaḥ || 16.65
godhūmo bahudugdhaḥ syādapūpo mlecchabhojanaḥ |
yavano nistuṣaḥ kṣīrī rasālaḥ sumanaśca saḥ || 16.66
godhūmaḥ snigdhamadhuro vātaghnaḥ pittadāhakṛt |
guruḥ śleṣmāmado balyo ruciro vīryavardhanaḥ || 16.67
snigdho'nyo laghugodhūmo gururvṛṣyaḥ kaphāpahaḥ |
āmadoṣakaro balyo madhuro vīryapuṣṭidaḥ || 16.68
yavastu medhyaḥ sitaśūkasaṃjño divyo'kṣataḥ kañcukidhānyarājau |
syāttīkṣṇaśūkasturagapriyaśca sakturhayeṣṭaśca pavitradhānyam || 16.69
yavaḥ kaṣāyo madhuraḥ suśītalaḥ pramehajittiktakaphāpahārakaḥ |
aśūkamuṇḍastu yavo balaprado vṛṣyaśca nṝṇāṃ bahuvīryapuṣṭidaḥ || 16.70
veṇujo veṇubījaśca vaṃśajo vaṃśataṇḍulaḥ |
vaṃśadhānyaṃ ca vaṃśāhvo veṇuvaṃśadvidhāyavaḥ || 16.71
śītaḥ kaṣāyo madhurastu rūkṣo mehakrimiśleṣmaviṣāpahaśca |
puṣṭiṃ ca vīryaṃ ca balaṃ ca datte pittāpaho veṇuyavaḥ praśastaḥ || 16.72
mudgastu sūpaśreṣṭhaḥ syādvarṇārhaśca rasottamaḥ |
bhuktiprado hayānando bhūbalo vājibhojanaḥ || 16.73
kṛṣṇamudgastu vāsanto mādhavaśca surāṣṭrajaḥ || 16.74
kṛṣṇamudgastridoṣaghno madhuro vātanāśanaḥ |
laghuśca dīpanaḥ pathyo balavīryāṅgapuṣṭidaḥ || 16.75
śāradastu harinmudgo dhūsaro'nyaśca śāradaḥ || 16.76
harinmudgaḥ kaṣāyaśca madhuraḥ kaphapittahṛt |
raktamūtrāmayaghnaśca śītalo laghudīpanaḥ || 16.77
tadvacca dhūsaro mudgo rasavīryādiṣu smṛtaḥ |
kaṣāyo madhuro rucyaḥ pittavātavibandhakṛt || 16.78
pittajvarārtiśamanaṃ laghu mudgayūṣaṃ saṃtāpahāri tadarocakanāśanaṃ ca |
raktaprasādanamidaṃ yadi saindhavena yuktaṃ tadā bhavati sarvarujāpahāri || 16.79
māṣastu kuruvindaḥ syāddhānyavīro vṛṣākaraḥ |
māṃsalaśca balāḍhyaśca pitryaśca pitṛjottamaḥ || 16.80
māṣaḥ snigdho bahumalakaraḥ śoṣaṇaḥ śleṣmakārī vīryeṇoṣṇo jhaṭiti kurute raktapittaprakopam |
hanyādvātaṃ gurubalakaro rocano bhakṣyamāṇaḥ svādurnityaṃ śramasukhavatāṃ sevanīyo narāṇām || 16.81
rājamāṣo nīlamāṣo nṛpamāṣo nṛpocitaḥ || 16.82
kaphapittaharo rucyo vātakṛdbaladāyakaḥ || 16.83
caṇastu harimanthaḥ syātsugandhaḥ kṛṣṇakañcukaḥ |
bālabhojyo vājibhakṣaścaṇakaḥ kañcukī ca saḥ || 16.84
caṇako madhuro rūkṣo mehajidvātapittakṛt |
dīptivarṇakaro balyo rucyaścādhmānakārakaḥ || 16.85
āmaścaṇaḥ śītalarucyakārī saṃtarpaṇo dāhatṛṣāpahārī |
gaulyo'śmarīśoṣavināśakārī kaṣāya īṣatkaṭurvīryakārī || 16.86
kṛṣṇastu caṇakaḥ śīto madhuraḥ kāsapittahṛt |
pittātisārakāsaghno balyaścaiva rasāyanaḥ || 16.87
caṇo gaurastu madhuro balakṛdrocanaḥ paraḥ |
śveto vātakaro rucyaḥ pittaghnaḥ śiśiro guruḥ || 16.88
subhṛṣṭacaṇako rucyo vātaghno raktadoṣakṛt |
vīryeṇoṣṇo laghuścaiva kaphaśaityāpahārakaḥ || 16.89
caṇasya yūṣaṃ madhuraṃ kaṣāyaṃ kaphāpahaṃ vātavikārahetuḥ |
śvāsordhvakāsaklamapīnasānāṃ karoti nāśaṃ baladīpanatvam || 16.90
caṇodakaṃ candramarīciśītaṃ pītaṃ prage pittarujāpahāri |
puṣṭipradaṃ naijaguṇaṃ ca pāke saṃtarpaṇaṃ mañjulamādhurīkam || 16.91
makuṣṭako mayaṣṭaśca vanamudgaḥ kṛmīlakaḥ |
amṛto'raṇyamudgaśca vallīmudgaśca kīrtitaḥ || 16.92
makuṣṭakaḥ kaṣāyaḥ syānmadhuro raktapittajit |
jvaradāhaharaḥ pathyo rucikṛtsarvadoṣahṛt || 16.93
masūro rāgadālistu maṅgalyaḥ pṛthubījakaḥ |
śūraḥ kalyāṇabījaśca gurubījo masūrakaḥ || 16.94
masūro madhuraḥ śītaḥ saṃgrahī kaphapittajit |
vātāmayakaraścaiva mūtrakṛcchraharo laghuḥ || 16.95
kalāyo muṇḍacaṇako hareṇuśca satīnakaḥ |
trāsano nālakaḥ kaṇṭhī satīnaśca hareṇukaḥ || 16.96
kalāyaḥ kurute vātaṃ pittadāhakaphāpahaḥ |
rucipuṣṭipradaḥ śītaḥ kaṣāyaścāmadoṣakṛt || 16.97
laṅkā karālā tripuṭā kāṇḍikā rūkṣaṇātmikā || 16.98
laṅkā rucyā himā gaulyā pittajidvātakṛdguruḥ || 16.99
āḍhakī tuvarī varyā karavīrabhujā tathā |
vṛttabījā pītapuṣpā śvetā raktāsitā tridhā || 16.100
āḍhakī tu kaṣāyā ca madhurā kaphapittajit |
īṣadvātakarā rucyā vidalā gurugrāhikā || 16.101
sā ca śvetā doṣadātrī tu raktā rucyā balyā pittatāpādihantrī |
sā śyāmā ceddīpanī pittadāhadhvaṃsā balyaṃ cāḍhakīyūṣamuktam || 16.102
kulitthastāmrabījaśca śvetabījaḥ sitetaraḥ || 16.103
kulitthastu kaṣāyoṣṇo rūkṣo vātakaphāpahaḥ || 16.104
kṣavaḥ kṣudhābhijananaścapalo dīrghaśimbikaḥ |
sukumāro vṛttabījo madhuraḥ kṣavakaśca saḥ || 16.105
kṣavaḥ kaṣāyamadhuraḥ śītalaḥ kaphapittahṛt |
vṛṣyaḥ śramaharo rucyaḥ pavanādhmānakārakaḥ || 16.106
madhuraḥ śvetaniṣpāvo mādhvīkā madhuśarkarā |
palaṃkaṣā sthūlaśimbī vṛttā madhusitā sitā || 16.107
madhuśarkarā surucyā madhurālpakaṣāyakā |
śiśirā vātulā balyāpyādhmānagurupuṣṭidā || 16.108
so'nyaśca kaṭuniṣpāvaḥ kharvuro nadījastathā || 16.109
nadīniṣpāvakastiktaḥ kaṭuko'sraprado guruḥ |
vātalaḥ kaphado rūkṣaḥ kaṣāyo viṣadoṣanut || 16.110
tilastu homadhānyaṃ syātpavitraḥ pitṛtarpaṇaḥ |
pāpaghnaḥ pūtadhānyaṃ ca jaṭilastu vanodbhavaḥ || 16.111
snigdho varṇabalāgnivṛddhijananastanyānilaghno guruḥ soṣṇaḥ pittakaro'lpamūtrakaraṇaḥ keśyo'tipathyo vraṇe |
saṃgrāhī madhuraḥ kaṣāyasahitastikto vipāke kaṭuḥ kṛṣṇaḥ pathyatamaḥ sito'lpaguṇadaḥ kṣīṇāstathānye tilāḥ || 16.112
palalaṃ tilakalkaṃ syāttilacūrṇaṃ ca piṣṭakam || 16.113
palalaṃ madhuraṃ rucyaṃ pittāsrabalapuṣṭidam || 16.114
tilakiṭṭaṃ tu piṇyākaḥ khalaḥ syāttilakalkajaḥ || 16.115
piṇyākaḥ kaṭuko gaulyaḥ kaphavātapramehanut || 16.116
atasī picchalā devī madagandhā madotkaṭā |
umā kṣumā haimavatī sunīlā nīlapuṣpikā || 16.117
atasī madagandhā syānmadhurā balakārikā |
kaphavātakarī ceṣatpittahṛtkuṣṭhavātanut || 16.118
āsurī rājikā rājī raktikā raktasarṣapaḥ |
tīkṣṇagandhā madhurikā kṣavakaḥ kṣuvakaḥ kṣavaḥ || 16.119
āsurī kaṭutiktoṣṇā vātaplīhārtiśūlanut |
dāhapittapradā hanti kaphagulmakṛmivraṇān || 16.120
rājakṣavakaḥ kṛṣṇastīkṣṇaphalā rājarājikā rājñī |
sā kṛṣṇasarṣapākhyā vijñeyā rājasarṣapākhyā ca || 16.121
rājasarṣapakastiktaḥ kaṭūṣṇo vātaśūlanut |
pittadāhaprado gulmakaṇḍūkuṣṭhavraṇāpahaḥ || 16.122
tīkṣṇakaśca durādharṣo rakṣoghnaḥ kuṣṭhanāśanaḥ |
siddhaprayojanaḥ siddhasādhanaḥ sitasarṣapaḥ || 16.123
siddhārthaḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇo vātaraktagrahāpahaḥ |
tvagdoṣaśamano rucyo viṣabhūtavraṇāpahaḥ || 16.124
dhānyānāṃ kañcuke śimbī bījaguptiśca śāmbhavī |
tadguptāni ca dhānyāni śimbīdhānyāni cakṣate || 16.125
śyāmākaḥ śyāmakaḥ śyāmastribījaḥ syādavipriyaḥ |
sukumārī rājadhānyaṃ tṛṇabījottamaśca saḥ || 16.126
śyāmāko madhuraḥ snigdhaḥ kaṣāyo laghuśītalaḥ |
vātakṛtkaphapittaghnaḥ saṃgrāhī viṣadoṣanut || 16.127
kodravaḥ koradūṣaśca kuddālo madanāgrajaḥ |
sa ca deśaviśeṣeṇa nānābhedaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 16.128
kodravo madhurastikto vraṇināṃ pathyakārakaḥ |
kaphapittaharo rūkṣo mohakṛdvātalo guruḥ || 16.129
varakaḥ sthūlakaṅguśca rūkṣaḥ sthūlapriyaṅgukaḥ || 16.130
varako madhuro rūkṣaḥ kaṣāyo vātapittakṛt || 16.131
kaṅguṇī kaṅgunī proktā cīnakaḥ pītataṇḍulaḥ |
vātalaḥ sukumāraśca sa ca nānāvidhābhidhaḥ || 16.132
priyaṅgurmadhuro rucyaḥ kaṣāyaḥ svāduśītalaḥ |
vātakṛtpittadāhaghno rūkṣo bhagnāsthibandhakṛt || 16.133
nīvāro'raṇyadhānyaṃ syānmunidhānyaṃ tṛṇodbhavam || 16.134
nīvāro madhuraḥ snigdhaḥ pavitraḥ pathyado laghuḥ || 16.135
rāgī tu lāñchanaḥ syādbahudalakaṇiśaśca gucchakaṇiśaśca || 16.136
tikto madhurakaṣāyaḥ śītaḥ pittāsranāśano baladaḥ || 16.137
kurī tu tṛṇadhānyaṃ syānmadhuraṃ tadbalapradam |
haritaṃ vārddhakaṃ pakvaṃ vājināṃ puṣṭidāyakam || 16.138
ye ke ca vrīhayo bhṛṣṭāste lājā iti kīrtitāḥ || 16.139
yavādayaśca ye bhṛṣṭā dhānāste parikīrtitāḥ || 16.140
lājā ca yavadhānā ca tarpaṇī pittanāśinī |
godhūmayāvanālotthāḥ kiṃciduṣṇāśca dīpanāḥ || 16.141
taptairapakvagodhūmairākulāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
ākulā guravo vṛṣyā madhurāḥ balakāriṇaḥ || 16.142
vrīhayo'pyardhapakvāśca taptāste pṛthukāḥ smṛtāḥ |
pṛthukāḥ svādavaḥ snigdhā hṛdyā madanavardhanāḥ || 16.143
pūpalā madhurāḥ proktā vṛṣyāste baladāḥ smṛtāḥ |
pittahṛttarpaṇā hṛdyāḥ snigdhāste balavardhanāḥ || 16.144
ye cānye yāvanālādyāścipiṭāstaptataṇḍulāḥ || 16.145
śāleyayāvanālīyacipiṭāḥ puṣṭivardhanāḥ || 16.146
ataptataṇḍulāste tu dugdhabījāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 16.147
dugdhabījā sumadhurā durjarā vīryapuṣṭidā || 16.148
taptāstu mudgacaṇakāḥ sumanādilaṅkā sadyastṛṣārtirucipittakṛtaśca jagdhāḥ |
vātālpadāḥ sukhakarā hyabalāśca rūkṣā hṛdyā bhavanti yuvajarjarabālakānām || 16.149
mudgagodhūmacaṇakā yāvanālādayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
yadardhapakvaṃ taddhānyaṃ viṣṭambhādhmānadoṣakṛt || 16.150
śuṣkagodhūmacūrṇaṃ tu karṇikā samudāhṛtā || 16.151
sphoṭastu caṇakādīnāṃ dāliti parikīrtitāḥ || 16.152
pakvaṃ haritalūnaṃ ca dhānyaṃ sarvaguṇāvaham || 16.153
śuṣkalūnaṃ tu niḥsāraṃ rūkṣaṃ tatsattvanāśanam || 16.154
koṣadhānyaṃ navaṃ balyaṃ madhuraṃ vatsaroṣitam || 16.155
navaṃ dhānyamabhiṣyandi laghu saṃvatsaroṣitam |
dvyabdoṣitaṃ laghu pathyaṃ trivarṣādabalaṃ bhavet || 16.156
caṇāstu yavagodhūmatilamāṣā navā hitāḥ |
purāṇā virasā rūkṣāstvahitā durjarābalāḥ || 16.157
dhānyaṃ vāpitamuttamaṃ tadakhilaṃ chinnodbhavaṃ madhyamaṃ jñeyaṃ yadyadavāpitaṃ tadadhamaṃ niḥsāradoṣapradam |
dagdhāyāṃ bhuvi yatnato'pi vipine ye vāpitāḥ śālayo ye ca chinnabhavā bhavanti khalu te viṇmūtrabandhapradāḥ || 16.158
kṣārodakasamutpannaṃ dhānyaṃ śleṣmarujāpaham || 16.159
susnigdhamṛttikodbhūtaṃ dhānyamojobalāvaham || 16.160
balapuṣṭiprabhāvaghnaṃ vālukāmṛttikodbhavam || 16.161
dhānyaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ ṣaṣṭikaṃ rājabhogyaṃ māṃsaṃ tvājaṃ taittiraṃ lāvakīyam |
pānīyaṃ syātkṛṣṇamṛtsnāsamutthaṃ kṣīrājyādau gavyamājaṃ praśastam || 16.162
itthaṃ prasiddhataradhānyaguṇābhidhānavīryābhivarṇanaviśṛṅkhalavāgvilāsam |
āmnāya vargamimamāśu labheta vaidyo vidyāṃ viṣaṇṇajanajīvanadānadhanyām || 16.163
yāni sadā bhujyante bhuñjānajanāśca yāni bhuñjante |
teṣāṃ khalu dhānyānāṃ vargo'yaṃ bhojyavarga iti kathitaḥ || 16.164
yenācāracaṇena mugdhamadhuraśrīśālinā sanmahāmānārhā bahudhānyasampaducitā saṃnīyate saṃtatam |
tena śrīnṛharīśvareṇa racite nāmokticūḍāmaṇau vargo'yaṃ sthitameti nūtanaracano dhānyāhvayaḥ ṣoḍaśaḥ || 16.165


rājanighaṇṭu, ṃāṃsādivarga
māṃsaṃ tu piśitaṃ kravyaṃ palaṃ tu rasyamasrajam |
palalaṃ jāṅgalaṃ kīramāmiṣaṃ ca taducyate || 17.1
sadyohatasya māṃsaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ hariṇādikasya yūnastu |
jñeyaṃ sugandhi pathyaṃ jāṅgaladeśasthitasya pathyatamam || 17.2
bālasya vṛddhasya kṛśasya rogiṇo viṣāgnidagdhasya mṛtasya cāmbuṣu |
tyājyaṃ mṛgādeḥ piśitaṃ tu tasya vigandhi śuṣkaṃ ca cirasthitaṃ ca || 17.3
sarvaṃ māṃsaṃ vātavidhvaṃsi vṛṣyaṃ balyaṃ rucyaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ tacca māṃsam |
deśasthānāccātmasaṃsthaṃ svabhāvairbhūyo nānārūpatāṃ yāti nūnam || 17.4
tatrānūpīyamāṃsaṃ gavayarurumṛgakroḍagaṇḍādikānāṃ snigdhaṃ pathyaṃ ca balyaṃ laghu śaśaśikharādyudbhavaṃ jāṅgalīyam |
puṣṭiṃ dīptiṃ ca datte rucikṛdatha laghu svādu sādhāraṇīyaṃ vṛṣyaṃ balyaṃ ca rucyaṃ ruruhariṇamṛgakroḍasāraṅgakāṇām || 17.5
māṃsaṃ sārasahaṃsarātrivirahikrauñcādijaṃ śītalaṃ snigdhaṃ vātakaphāpahaṃ guru tataḥ svādu tridoṣāpaham |
pathyaṃ lāvakatittirādijanitaṃ vṛṣyaṃ laghu syātparaṃ cakrakrauñcamayūratittirabhavaṃ deśatrayādīdṛśam || 17.6
druto vilambitaścaiva plavaśceti gataistrayaḥ |
sthānato'pi trayaste tu bilasthalajalāśrayāḥ || 17.7
punaste tu prasahanāḥ pratudā viṣkirā iti |
svabhāvatastrayaḥ proktāḥ kramaśo mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ || 17.8
athaiṣāṃ kramaśo lakṣmaguṇān vakṣyāmi vargaśaḥ |
evaṃ navavidhāḥ proktāsta eva mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ || 17.9
ajaśaśahariṇādayaḥ svayaṃ ye drutagamanā drutasaṃjñakāḥ smṛtāste |
taduditapalalaṃ ca pathyabalyaṃ racayati vīryamadapradaṃ laghu syāt || 17.10
gajakhaḍgamukhā mahāmṛgā nijagatyaiva vilambitāḥ smṛtāste |
balakṛtpiśitaṃ ca picchalaṃ kaphakāsānilamāndyadaṃ guru syāt || 17.11
sārasahaṃsabalākāścakrakrauñcādayo jale plavanāt |
plavasaṃjñāḥ kathitāste tanmāṃsaṃ gurūṣṇaṃ ca baladāyi || 17.12
ahinakulaśalyagodhāmūṣakamukhyā bileśayāḥ kathitāḥ |
śvāsānilakāsaharaṃ tanmāṃsaṃ pittadāhakaram || 17.13
kroḍarurukuraṅgādyā vividhā ye mṛgādayaḥ |
sthaleśayāstu te sarve māṃsaṃ sarvaguṇāvaham || 17.14
jhaṣamakaranakrakarkaṭakūrmapramukhā jaleśayāḥ kathitāḥ |
māṃsaṃ teṣāṃ tu saraṃ vṛṣyaṃ guru śiśirabalasamīrakaram || 17.15
śārdūlasiṃhaśarabharkṣatarakṣumukhyā ye'nye prasahya vinihantyabhivartayanti |
te kīrtitāḥ prasahanāḥ palalaṃ tadīyamarśaḥpramehajaṭharāmayajāḍyahāri || 17.16
bhoktā niṣkṛṣyāmiṣaṃ sa pratudaḥ prokto gṛdhraśyenakākādiko yaḥ |
māṃsaṃ tasya svādu saṃtarpaṇaṃ ca snigdhaṃ balyaṃ pittadāhāsradāyi || 17.17
bhakṣyāśca kukkuṭakapotakatittirādyāḥ kṣauṇīṃ vilikhya nakharaiḥ khalu vartayanti |
te viṣkirāḥ prakathitāḥ piśitaṃ tadīyaṃ vṛṣyaṃ kaṣāyamadhuraṃ śiśiraṃ ca rucyam || 17.18
ayameva guṇo jñeyaḥ pakṣiṇāṃ ca yathākramam |
sarvasthānaviśeṣeṇa saṃkhyā ca gatirucyate || 17.19
yatra sthitā ye gatito'pi deśādanyatra yātā mṛgapakṣimukhyāḥ |
svasvocitasthānanivartanena māṃse'pi teṣāṃ guṇaparyayāḥ syuḥ || 17.20
māṃsaṃ khaḍgamṛgotthaṃ tu balakṛdbṛṃhaṇaṃ guru || 17.21
gavayasyāmiṣaṃ balyaṃ rucyaṃ vṛṣyaṃ ca bṛṃhaṇam || 17.22
rurukravyaṃ guru snigdhaṃ mandavahnibalapradam || 17.23
apūtaṃ gobhavaṃ kravyaṃ guru vātakaphapradam || 17.24
vanamahiṣāmiṣaṃ syādīṣallaghu dīpanaṃ ca baladāyi || 17.25
grāmīṇamahiṣamāṃsaṃ snigdhaṃ nidrākaraṃ ca pittaharam || 17.26
hastikravyaṃ guru snigdhaṃ vātalaṃ śleṣmakārakam |
bahupuṣṭipradaṃ caiva durjaraṃ mandavahnidam || 17.27
aśvamāṃsaṃ bhaveduṣṇaṃ vātaghnaṃ baladaṃ laghu |
pittadāhapradaṃ nṛṇāṃ tadetaccātisevanāt || 17.28
uṣṭramāṃsaṃ tu śiśiraṃ tridoṣaśamanaṃ laghu |
balapuṣṭipradaṃ rucyaṃ madhuraṃ vīryavardhanam || 17.29
gardabhaprabhavaṃ māṃsaṃ kiṃcidguru balapradam |
rucyaṃ tu vanyajaṃ śaityaṃ bahuvīryabalapradam || 17.30
eṇasya māṃsaṃ laghuśītavṛṣyaṃ tridoṣahṛtṣaḍrasajaṃ ca rucyam || 17.31
kuraṅgamāṃsaṃ madhuraṃ ca tadvatkaphāpahaṃ māṃsadapittanāśi || 17.32
sāraṅgaṃ jāṅgalaṃ snigdhaṃ madhuraṃ laghu vṛṣyakam || 17.33
śikharīsambhavaṃ māṃsaṃ laghu hṛdyaṃ balapradam || 17.34
varāhamāṃsaṃ guru vātahāri vṛṣyaṃ balasvedakaraṃ vanottham || 17.35
tasmādguru grāmavarāhamāṃsaṃ tanoti medo balavīryavṛddhim || 17.36
śaraśṛṅgasya māṃsaṃ tu guru snigdhaṃ kaphapradam |
balyaṃ vṛṣyakaraṃ puṣṭikiṃcidvātakaraṃ param || 17.37
chāgamāṃsaṃ laghu snigdhaṃ nātiśītaṃ rucipradam |
nirdoṣaṃ vātapittaghnaṃ madhuraṃ balapuṣṭidam || 17.38
chāgapotabhavaṃ māṃsaṃ laghu śītaṃ pramehajit |
īṣallaghu balaṃ datte tadeva tṛṇacāriṇaḥ || 17.39
aurabhraṃ madhuraṃ śītaṃ guru viṣṭambhi bṛṃhaṇam || 17.40
āvikaṃ madhuraṃ māṃsaṃ kiṃcidguru balapradam || 17.41
śalyamāṃsaṃ guru snigdhaṃ dīpanaṃ śvāsakāsajit || 17.42
picchilaṃ nākulaṃ māṃsaṃ vātaghnaṃ śleṣmapittakṛt || 17.43
godhāmāṃsaṃ tu vātaghnaṃ śvāsakāsaharaṃ ca tat || 17.44
śaśamāṃsaṃ tridoṣaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ śvāsakāsajit || 17.45
anye bileśayā ye syuḥ kokaḍondurukādayaḥ |
māṃsaṃ ca garhitaṃ teṣāṃ māndyaṃ gauravadurjaram || 17.46
āraṇyakukkuṭakravyaṃ hṛdyaṃ śleṣmaharaṃ laghu || 17.47
grāmyakukkuṭajaṃ snigdhaṃ vātahṛddīpanaṃ guru || 17.48
hārītapalalaṃ svādu kaphapittāsradoṣajit || 17.49
vardhanaṃ vīryabalayostadvadeva kapotajam || 17.50
pārāvatapalaṃ snigdhaṃ madhuraṃ guru śītalam |
pittāsradāhanudbalyaṃ tathānyadvīryavṛddhidam || 17.51
snigdhaṃ tittirijaṃ māṃsaṃ laghu vīryabalapradam |
kaṣāyaṃ madhuraṃ śītaṃ tridoṣaśamanaṃ param || 17.52
tadvacca lāvakaṃ māṃsaṃ pathyaṃ grāhi laghu smṛtam || 17.53
tadvacca vartakamāṃsaṃ nirdoṣaṃ vīryapuṣṭidam || 17.54
caṭakāyāḥ palaṃ śītaṃ laghu vṛṣyaṃ balapradam || 17.55
tadvaccāraṇyacaṭakakravyaṃ laghu ca pathyadam || 17.56
caṭakācchītalaṃ rucyaṃ vṛṣyaṃ kāpiñjalāmiṣam || 17.57
tadvaccakorajaṃ māṃsaṃ vṛṣyaṃ ca balapuṣṭidam || 17.58
kravyaṃ tu cakravākasya laghu snigdhaṃ balapradam |
vahnikṛtsarvaśūlaghnamuṣṇaṃ vātāmayāpaham || 17.59
sārasasya tu māṃsaṃ ca madhurāmlakaṣāyakam |
mahātīsārapittaghnaṃ grahaṇyarśorujāpaham || 17.60
snigdhahimaṃ guru vṛṣyaṃ māṃsaṃ jalapakṣiṇāṃ tu vātaharam || 17.61
teṣvapi ca haṃsamāṃsaṃ vṛṣyatamaṃ timiraharaṇaṃ ca || 17.62
anye bakabalākādyā guravo māṃsabhakṣaṇāt |
anuktaṃ tu mṛgādīnāṃ māṃsaṃ grāhyaṃ hitādiṣu || 17.63
matsyāḥ snigdhoṣṇaguravo vātaghnā raktapittadāḥ |
tatra kāṃścidapi brūmo viśeṣaguṇalakṣaṇān || 17.64
rohito gargaro bhīrurbālako barbarastathā |
chāgalo raktamatsyo'tha mahiṣaścāvilastathā || 17.65
vātūko'lomaśā cāpi jñeyā karṇavaśādayaḥ |
lakṣyalakṣaṇavīryādīn kathayāmi yathākramam || 17.66
kṛṣṇaḥ śalkī śvetakukṣistu matsyo yaḥ śreṣṭho'sau rohito vṛttavaktraḥ |
koṣṇaṃ balyaṃ rohitasyāpi māṃsaṃ vātaṃ hanti snigdhamāpnoti vīryam || 17.67
yaḥ pītavarṇo'pi ca picchilāṅgaḥ pṛṣṭhe tu rekhābahulaḥ saśalkaḥ |
sa gargaro barbaranādarūkṣo jaḍaśca śītaḥ kaphavātadāyī || 17.68
pṛṣṭhe pakṣau dvau gale pucchakaṃ cetsarpābhaḥ syātphūtkṛto vṛttatuṇḍaḥ |
jñeyaḥ śalkī matsyako bhīruruktaḥ snigdho vṛṣyo durjaro vātakārī || 17.69
nātisthūlo vṛttavaktro'pi śasto dhatte dantān śmaśrulo dīrghakāyaḥ |
saṃdhyāyāṃ vā rātriśeṣe ca varyaḥ prokto bālaḥ pathyabalyaḥ suvṛṣyaḥ || 17.70
pṛṣṭhe kukṣau kaṇṭakī dīrghatuṇḍaḥ sarpābho yaḥ so'pyayaṃ barbarākhyaḥ |
vātāṭopaṃ so'pi datte jaḍaśca balyaḥ snigdho durjaro vīryakārī || 17.71
śvetaṃ sukāyaṃ samadīrghavṛttaṃ niḥśalkakaṃ chāgalakaṃ vadanti |
nale dvikaṇṭaḥ kila tasya pṛṣṭhe kaṇṭaḥ supathyo rucido balapradaḥ || 17.72
yo raktāṅgo nātidīrgho na cālpo nātisthūlo raktamatsyaḥ sa coktaḥ |
śīto rucyaḥ puṣṭikṛddīpano'sau nāśaṃ dhatte kiṃca doṣatrayasya || 17.73
yaḥ kṛṣṇo dīrghakāyaḥ syātsthūlaśalko balādhikaḥ |
matsyo mahiṣanāmāsau dīpano balavīryadaḥ || 17.74
śuklāṅgastāmrapakṣo yaḥ svalpāṅgaścāvilāhvayaḥ |
surucyo madhuro balyo guṇāḍhyo vīryapuṣṭidaḥ || 17.75
yaḥ sthūlāṅgo māhiṣākārako yastālusthāne nīrajābhāṃ dadhāti |
śalkaṃ sthūlaṃ yasya vātūkako'sau datte vīryaṃ dīpanaṃ vṛṣyadāyī || 17.76
vitastimānaḥ śvetāṅgaḥ sūkṣmaśalkaḥ sudīpanaḥ |
alomaśāhvayo matsyo balavīryāṅgapuṣṭidaḥ || 17.77
yo vṛttagaulyaḥ kṛṣṇāṅgaḥ śalkī karṇavaśābhidhaḥ || 17.78
dīpanaḥ pācanaḥ pathyo vṛṣyo'sau balapuṣṭidaḥ || 17.79
niḥśalkā ninditā matsyāḥ sarve śalkayutā hitāḥ |
vapuḥsthairyakarā vīryabalapuṣṭivivardhanāḥ || 17.80
hradakulyājaladhinirjharataḍāgavāpījale ca ye matsyāḥ |
te tu jaḍā nādeyā yathottaraṃ laghutarāstu nādeyāḥ || 17.81
kṣārāmbumatsyā guravo'sradāhadā viṣṭambhadāste lavaṇārṇavādijāḥ |
tānaśnatāṃ svādujalasthitā api jñeyā jaḍāste'pi tathā śṛtānimān || 17.82
śailāṭavīnagarabhūjalacāriṇo ye ye ke'pi sattvanivahāḥ khalu saptasaṃkhyāḥ |
tanmāṃsamatra na vitathyamathābhyadhāyi granthasya vistarabhayācca navopayogāt || 17.83
pakvaṃ māṃsaṃ hitaṃ sarvaṃ balavīryavivardhanam |
bhṛṣṭamāṃsaṃ vidāhi syādasravātādidoṣakṛt || 17.84
pūrvārdhaṃ puruṣasya tadgurutaraṃ paścārdhabhāgaḥ striyāḥ strī gurvī kila gurviṇī yadi tathā yoṣicca tulyā laghuḥ |
pakṣī cetpuruṣo laghuḥ śṛṇu śiraḥskandhorupṛṣṭhe kramātmāṃsaṃ yacca kaṭisthitaṃ tadakhilaṃ gurveva sarvātmanā || 17.85
rasaraktādidhātūnāṃ guruḥ syāduttarottaraṃ meḍhravṛkkayakṛnmāṃsaṃ vārṣaṇaṃ cātimātrataḥ || 17.86
itthaṃ pratisthalavilāmbunabhaḥpracāraprāṇyaṅgamāṃsaguṇanirṇayapūrṇamenam |
vargaṃ vicārya bhiṣajā viniyujyamāno bhuktvāśanaṃ na vikṛtiṃ samupaiti martyaḥ || 17.87
yasyāsītsamitidvipādhipabṛhatkumbhāntarasthāmiṣaprāyābhyāsapipāsayeva taruṇī netrāmbudhārā dviṣām |
tasyāyaṃ puruṣapratāpasuhṛdaḥ śrīmannṛsiṃheśiturvargaḥ saptadaśo niṣīdati kṛtau nāmādicūḍāmaṇau || 17.88


rājanighaṇṭu, ṃnuṣyādivargaḥ
manuṣyā mānuṣā martyā manujā mānavā narāḥ |
dvipādāścetanā bhūsthā bhūmijā bhūspṛśo viśaḥ || 18.1
puruṣaḥ pūruṣo nā ca naraḥ pañcajanaḥ pumān |
arthāśrayo'dhikārī syātkarmārhaśca jano'rthavān || 18.2
strī yoṣidvanitābalā sunayanā nārī ca sīmantinī rāmā vāmadṛgaṅganā ca lalanā kāntā puraṃdhrī vadhūḥ |
subhrūḥ sā varavarṇinī ca sutanustanvī tanuḥ kāminī tanvaṅgī ramaṇī kuraṅganayanā bhīruḥ priyā bhāminī || 18.3
yoṣinmahelā mahilā vilāsinī nitambinī sāpi ca mattakāśinī |
janī sunetrā pramadā ca sundarī syādañcitabhrūrlalitā vilāsinī || 18.4
māninī ca varārohā natāṅgī ca natodarā |
pratīpadarśinī śyāmā kāminī darśanī ca sā || 18.5
bhartā patirvaraḥ kāntaḥ pariṇetā priyo gṛhī || 18.6
bhāryā patnī priyā jāyā dārāśca gṛhiṇī ca sā || 18.7
napuṃsakaṃ bhavetklībaṃ tṛtīyā prakṛtistathā |
ṣaṇḍḥḥ paṇḍaśca nārī tu poṭā strīpuṃsalakṣaṇā || 18.8
atha rājñī ca paṭṭārhā mahiṣī rājavallabhā || 18.9
bhoginyo'nyā vilāsinyaḥ saṃbhuṅkte yāstu pārthivaḥ || 18.10
rājabhogyāḥ sumukhyo yāstā bhaṭṭinya iti smṛtāḥ || 18.11
veśyā tu gaṇikā bhogyā vārastrī smaradīpikā || 18.12
brahmā tu brāhmaṇo vipraḥ ṣaṭkarmā ca dvijottamaḥ || 18.13
rājā tu sārvabhaumaḥ syātpārthivaḥ kṣatriyo nṛpaḥ || 18.14
vaiśyastu vyavahartā viḍvārttiko vāṇijo vaṇik || 18.15
śūdraḥ pajjaścaturthaḥ syātdvijadāsa upāsakaḥ || 18.16
vipraḥ kṣatro vaiśyaśūdrau ca varṇāścatvāro'mī tatra pūrve dvijāḥ syuḥ |
eṣāmeva prātilomyānulomyājjāyante'nyā jātayaḥ saṃkareṇa || 18.17
bālaḥ pāko'rbhako garbhaḥ potakaḥ pṛthukaḥ śiśuḥ |
śāvo'rbho bāliśo ḍimbho vaṭurmāṇavako mataḥ || 18.18
jāto'rbhakaḥ pakṣadinena māsataḥ pākastribhistairatha potakābhidhaḥ |
ṣaḍbhistu māsaiḥ pṛthuko'bdataḥ śiśustribhirvaṭurmāṇavakaśca saptabhiḥ || 18.19
bālo'bdaiḥ pañcadaśabhiḥ kumārastriṃśatā smṛtaḥ |
yuvā pañcāśatā varṣairvṛddhaḥ syādata uttaraiḥ || 18.20
kaumāraṃ pañcamābdāntaṃ paugaṇḍaṃ daśamāvadhi |
kaiśoramā pañcadaśādyauvanaṃ tu tataḥ param || 18.21
yuvā vayaḥsthastaruṇo vṛddhastu sthaviro jaran |
pravayā yātayāmaśca jīno jīrṇaśca jarjaraḥ || 18.22
bālottānaśayā ḍimbhā stanapā ca stanaṃdhayī || 18.23
kanyā kumārī gaurī tu nagnikānāgatārtavā || 18.24
sā madhyamā vayaḥsthā ca yuvatī sustanī ca sā |
ciraṇṭī suvayāḥ śyāmā prauḍhā dṛṣṭarajāśca sā || 18.25
gurviṇyāpannasattvā syādantarvatnī ca garbhiṇī || 18.26
niṣphalā jaratī vṛddhā sthavirā ca gatārtavā || 18.27
puṣpitā malinā mlānā pāṃśulā ca rajasvalā || 18.28
vandhyāvakeśinī śūnyā moghapuṣpā vṛthārtavā || 18.29
tanustanūḥ saṃhananaṃ śarīraṃ kalevaraṃ kṣetravapuḥpurāṇi |
gātraṃ ca mūrtirghanakāyadehāvaṣṭāṅgapīḍāni ca vigrahaśca || 18.30
aṅgamaṃsaḥ pratīkaścāpaghano'vayavo'pi ca || 18.31
śiraḥ śīrṣakamuṇḍaṃ ca mūrdhā mauliśca mastakam |
varāṅgamuttamāṅgaṃ ca kapālaṃ keśabhṛtsmṛtam || 18.32
keśāḥ śirasijā vālāḥ kuntalā mūrdhajāḥ kacāḥ |
cikurāḥ karuhāścātha tadveṣṭāḥ kavarīmukhāḥ || 18.33
dṛgdṛṣṭirlocanaṃ netraṃ cakṣurnayanamambakam |
īkṣaṇaṃ grahaṇaṃ cākṣi darśanaṃ ca vilocanam || 18.34
apāṅgo netraparyanto nayanopānta ityapi |
tayormadhyagatā tārā bimbinī ca kanīnikā || 18.35
bhālaṃ lalāṭamalikaṃ kathayanti godhirbhrūścillikā ca nayanordhvagaromarājiḥ |
madhyaṃ tayorbhavati kūrcamatha śrutistu śrotaḥ śravaḥ śravaṇakarṇavacograhāśca || 18.36
oṣṭho'dharo dantavāso dantavastraṃ radacchadaḥ |
tayorubhayato deśau yau prāntau sṛkkaṇī ca tau || 18.37
ghrāṇaṃ gandhavaho ghoṇā siṅghiṇī nāsikā ca sā || 18.38
śaṅkhaḥ karṇasamīpaḥ syātśiṅghāṇaṃ nāsikāmale || 18.39
tuṇḍamāsyaṃ mukhaṃ vaktraṃ vadanaṃ lapanānane || 18.40
oṣṭhādharastu cibukaṃ gaṇḍo gallaḥ kapolakaḥ || 18.41
hanūstadūrdhvaṃ daśanāśca dantā dvijā radāste radanāstathoktāḥ || 18.42
jihvā rasajñā rasanā ca soktā syātkākudaṃ tālu ca tālukaṃ ca || 18.43
tadūrdhvaṃ sūkṣmajihvā yā ghaṇṭikā lambikā ca sā || 18.44
anyādhomūlajihvā syātpratijihvopajihvikā || 18.45
avaṭustu śiraḥpaścātsaṃdhirghāṭā kṛkāṭikā || 18.46
grīvā ca kaṃdharā kaṃdhiḥ śirodhiśca śirodharā || 18.47
kaṇṭho galo nigālo'tha ghaṇṭikā galaśuṇṭhikā || 18.48
dhamanī tu śirāṃse tu skandho'dhaḥśikharaṃ tathā || 18.49
tasya saṃdhistu jatru syātkakṣā dormūlasaṃjñakā || 18.50
tadadhastādbhavetpārśvaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ paścāttanoḥ smṛtam || 18.51
dordoṣā ca praveṣṭaśca bāhurbāhā bhujo bhujā || 18.52
pāṇistu pañcaśākhaḥ syātkaro hastaḥ śayastathā || 18.53
karamūle maṇibandho bhujamadhye kūrparaḥ kaphoṇiśca || 18.54
tasmādadhaḥ prakoṣṭhaḥ pragaṇḍakaḥ kūrparāṃsamadhyaṃ syāt || 18.55
aṅgulyaḥ karaśākhāḥ syuḥ pradeśinyāṃ tu tarjanī |
paruḥ syādaṅgulīsaṃdhiḥ parvasaṃdhiśca kathyate || 18.56
athāṅguṣṭhapradeśinyau madhyamānāmikā tathā |
kaniṣṭhā ceti pañca syuḥ krameṇāṅgulayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 18.57
kāmāṅkuśāḥ kararuhāḥ karajā nakharā nakhāḥ |
pāṇijāṅgulīsambhūtāḥ punarbhavapunarnavāḥ || 18.58
karasyādhaḥ prapāṇiḥ syādūrdhvaṃ karatalaṃ smṛtam |
rekhāḥ sāmudrike jñeyāḥ śubhāśubhanivedikāḥ || 18.59
stanorasijavakṣojapayodharakucāstathā || 18.60
stanāgraṃ cūcukaṃ vṛttaṃ śikhā stanamukhaṃ ca tat || 18.61
vakṣo vatsamuraḥ kroḍo hṛdayaṃ hṛdbhujāntaram || 18.62
kukṣiḥ piciṇḍo jaṭharaṃ tundaṃ syādudaraṃ ca tat || 18.63
jīvasthānaṃ tu marma syātkaṭiprānte trikaṃ smṛtam || 18.64
nābhiḥ syādudarāvartastato'dho vastirucyate |
vastiśca vātaśīrṣaṃ syādgarbhasthānaṃ ca tatstriyāḥ || 18.65
garbhāśayo jarāyuśca garbhādhāraśca ca smṛtaḥ || 18.66
nābhistanāntaraṃ jantorāmāśayaḥ iti smṛtaḥ || 18.67
pakvāśayo hyadho nābhervastirmūtrāśayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 18.68
kaṭiḥ kakudmatī śroṇī nitambaśca kaṭīrakam |
ārohaṃ śroṇiphalakaṃ kalatraṃ rasanāpadam || 18.69
nitambaścaramaṃ śroṇeḥ strīṇāṃ jaghanamagrataḥ || 18.70
kakundarau tu sarveṣāṃ syātāṃ jaghanakūpakau |
kaṭiprothau sphicau pāyurgudāpānaṃ tadāsanam || 18.71
gudamuṣkadvayormadhye yo bhāgaḥ sa bhagaḥ smṛtaḥ || 18.72
muṣko'ṇḍamaṇḍakoṣaśca vṛṣaṇo bījapeśikā || 18.73
śiśnaṃ śephaśca liṅgaṃ ca meḍhraṃ sādhanamehane || 18.74
yonirbhago varāṅgaṃ syādupasthaṃ smaramandiram || 18.75
ūrū tu sakthinī śroṇisakthnoḥ saṃdhistu vaṅkṣaṇaḥ |
jaṅghorūmadhyaparva syājjānvaṣṭhīvacca cakrikā || 18.76
jaṅghā tu prasṛtā jñeyā tanmadhye piṇḍikā tathā || 18.77
jaṅghāṅghrisaṃdhigranthau tu ghuṭikā gulpha ityapi || 18.78
gulphasyādhastu pārṣṇiḥ syātpadāgraṃ prapadaṃ matam || 18.79
vikramaścaraṇaḥ pādaḥ pādāṅghriśca padaṃ kramaḥ || 18.80
kroḍamaṅkastathotsaṅgaḥ prāgbhāgo vapuṣaḥ smṛtaḥ || 18.81
karo bhavetsaṃhitavistṛtāṅgulastalaścapeṭaḥ pratalaḥ prahastakaḥ |
muṣṭirbhavetsaṃhṛtapiṇḍitāṅgulāvākuñcito'gre prasṛtaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 18.82
syāttarjanī madhyamikā tvanāmikā kaniṣṭhikāṅguṣṭhayutā yadā tadā |
prādeśatālābhidhagosravastathā vitastiratyarthamiha kramādiyam || 18.83
hastastu vistṛte pāṇāvā madhyāṅgulikūrparam || 18.84
baddhamuṣṭau saratniḥ syādaratnirakaniṣṭhakaḥ || 18.85
vyāmaḥ sahastayoḥ syāttu tiryagbāhvoryadantaram |
ūrdhvaṃ vistṛtadoṣpāṇirnṛmānaṃ pauruṣaṃ viduḥ || 18.86
jīvasthānaṃ tu marma syājjīvāgāraṃ taducyate || 18.87
marmasthānaṃ ca tatproktaṃ bhrūmadhyādiṣvanekadhā || 18.88
bhrūmadhyakaṇḍagalaśaṅkhakacāṃsapṛṣṭhagrīvāgudāṇḍapadapāṇiyugāsthisaṃdhīn |
vaidyāḥ śarekṣaṇamitāni vadanti marmasthānāni cāṅgagatināśakarāṇi martye || 18.89
lālā bhavenmukhasrāvaḥ sṛṇikā syandinī ca sā || 18.90
svedo gharmaśca gharmāmbho dūṣikā netrayormalam || 18.91
malaṃ viṣṭhā purīṣaṃ ca viṭkiṭṭaṃ pūtikaṃ ca tat |
mūtraṃ tu guhyaniṣyandaḥ prasrāvaḥ sravaṇaṃ sravaḥ || 18.92
valī carmataraṃgaḥ syāttvagūrmistvaktaraṃgakaḥ || 18.93
palitaṃ ca jarā lakṣma keśaśauklyaṃ ca tadbhavet || 18.94
rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedo'sthimajjānaḥ śukrasaṃyutāḥ |
śarīrasthairyadāḥ samyakvijñeyāḥ sapta dhātavaḥ || 18.95
rasastu rasikā proktā svedamātā vapuḥsravaḥ |
carmāmbhaścarmasāraśca raktasūrasramātṛkā || 18.96
raktāsraṃ rudhiraṃ tvagjaṃ kīlālakṣatajāni tu |
śoṇitaṃ lohitaṃ cāsṛkśoṇaṃ lohaṃ ca carmajam || 18.97
māṃsaṃ tu piśitaṃ kravyaṃ palaṃ tu rasyamasrajam |
palalaṃ jāṅgalaṃ kīramāmiṣaṃ ca taducyate || 18.98
medastu māṃsasāraḥ syānmāṃsasneho vasā vapā || 18.99
medojamasthidhātuḥ syātkulyaṃ kīkasakaṃ ca tat || 18.100
asthisārastu majjā syāttejo bījaṃ tathāsthijam |
jīvanaṃ dehasāraśca tathāsthisnehasaṃjñakam || 18.101
śukraṃ puṃstvaṃ reto bījaṃ vīryaṃ ca pauruṣaṃ kathitam |
indriyamannavikāro majjaraso harṣaṇaṃ balaṃ caiva || 18.102
rasādasraṃ tato māṃsaṃ māṃsānmedo'sthi tadbhavam |
asthno majjā tataḥ śukramitthameṣāṃ janikramaḥ || 18.103
tilakaṃ kloma mastiṣkaṃ snehastu mastakodbhavaḥ || 18.104
antraṃ purī tadākhyātaṃ plīhā gulma iti smṛtaḥ || 18.105
vasā tu vasnasā snāyurvatsoktā dehavalkalam |
sā tvak[... 10 Zeichen] || 18.106
śirodhijā manyā dhamanī dharaṇī dharā |
tantukī jīvitajñā ca nāḍī siṃhī ca kīrtitā || 18.107
kaṇḍarā tu mahāsnāyurmahānāḍī ca sā smṛtā || 18.108
śarīrāsthi tu kaṅkālaṃ syātkaraṅko'sthipañjaraḥ |
srotāṃsi khāni chidrāṇi kālakhaṇḍaṃ yakṛnmatam || 18.109
śiro'sthi tu karoṭiḥ syātśirastrāṇaṃ tu śīrṣakam |
tatkhaṇḍaṃ kharparaṃ prāhuḥ kapālaṃ ca tadīritam || 18.110
pṛṣṭhāsthi tu kaseruḥ syātśākhāsthi nalakaṃ smṛtam || 18.111
pārśvāsthi parśukā proktamiti dehāṅganirṇayaḥ || 18.112
ātmā śarīrī kṣetrajñaḥ pudgalaḥ prāṇa īśvaraḥ |
jīvo vibhuḥ pumānīśaḥ sarvajñaḥ śambhuravyayaḥ || 18.113
pradhānaṃ prakṛtirmāyā śaktiścaitanyamityapi || 18.114
ahaṃkāro'bhimānaḥ syādahaṃtāhaṃmatistathā || 18.115
mānasaṃ hṛdayaṃ svāntaṃ cittaṃ ceto manaśca hṛt || 18.116
sattvaṃ rajastamaśceti proktāḥ puṃsastrayo guṇāḥ || 18.117
śrotraṃ tvagrasanā netraṃ nāsā cetyakṣapañcakam || 18.118
